Geom Mugeuk saw the Drunken Demon laugh at his words. Everyone feared and worried about his father, but probably none more than the Drunken Demon. At least it was fortunate that the misunderstanding that his father disliked him had been resolved.

Geom Mugeuk approached the Drunken Demon, who was drinking tea, and sniffed at him.

“Young Cult Leader, what are you doing?”

“I’m checking because you and tea don’t suit each other at all, Drunken Demon. I wondered if there might be liquor in the pot instead of tea.”

With those words, Geom Mugeuk poured tea into an empty teacup and drank it.

“Somehow I feel like I’m getting drunk. Isn’t this liquor that tastes like tea?”

“Tea is nice too, once you start drinking it.”

“You’re holding up well, though.”

“You know, don’t you? There’s nothing as mysterious as the human body. As if it never drank so much liquor, my body is already adapting to tea.”

“That’s not what I mean. I’m saying you’re holding back well when you must want to charge over there and overturn everything.”

The Drunken Demon drank his tea in one long draught and said,

“I won’t be able to endure it for long.”

Between the long strands of hair hanging down, the Drunken Demon’s eyes were colder than ever.

Ian and Han Seol could tell. The Drunken Demon was not holding back liquor; he was holding back his rage.

Geom Mugeuk looked out the window and spoke quietly. Now, he was treating him not as a Demon Venerable, but as an older brother.

“We’ll drag them out soon. This place is too beautiful for villains to hide in.”

Everyone followed Geom Mugeuk’s gaze and looked for a moment at the pure white world outside the window.

Knowing what sort of person the Blood King was, Geom Mugeuk briefly imagined that white world covered in blood.

But that was all right.

Because soon it would be covered once more by pure white snow.

* * *

The Ice Palace Lord received me with tea and refreshments.

“This is my first time sitting together with the heir of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

“It is an honor.”

“Now, have some.”

When I drank the tea without hesitation, the Ice Palace Lord looked at me as though surprised. The meaning was clear: as the Young Cult Leader, how could I drink without even checking for poison?

“Before coming here, I investigated and studied you, Palace Lord.”

“What sort of person was I?”

“You were an intelligent and astute person. Even if half a century passed and dementia came upon you, at the very least you would not poison a Young Cult Leader who came from the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

The Ice Palace Lord gave a faint smile. She understood that I was complimenting her by saying she looked to be in her twenties or thirties.

“I studied a little about you as well.”

“What sort of person am I?”

Looking straight at me, the Ice Palace Lord answered.

“A person one cannot easily define with a single answer.”

“You saw correctly. I am not an easy person.”

I said it as though joking, but the Ice Palace Lord did not smile, as if she truly thought so.

“I hear there has been progress in the investigation?”

“Yes. That is why I paid a visit to Elder Seo yesterday.”

“Do you suspect Elder Seo?”

I slowly drank my tea and let a brief silence pass.

“What do you think, Palace Lord?”

“It is absurd.”

“There are suspicious circumstances.”

“Is that so?”

“And yet you are calm.”

“Because it is impossible.”

The Ice Palace Lord did not hide that she trusted Seo Nak more than anyone.

“You still have not properly answered my question.”

It was also a direct demand that I state my thoughts.

“I suspect him.”

“Why?”

“It is simply what I feel.”

For the force behind this to threaten you, Palace Lord, it would have to be someone on Elder Seo’s level.

“So you mean you have no direct evidence.”

Geom Mugeuk admitted it with a smile.

“Why would Elder Seo have killed Wonju?”

“I don’t know either.”

“He is not that sort of person.”

She drove the point home once again, but I could feel it. Some distrust was hidden within that trust. If she truly believed Seo Nak, she would have stopped the investigation right now.

There was another reason she had allowed me to remain in the Ice Palace and investigate this case.

‘What is it that you truly want?’

I could feel that there was a secret buried deep within the snow, beyond merely finding the culprit who had killed Wonju.

This time, I probed the Ice Palace Lord’s heart with the matter of the North Blood Sect.

“I believe the North Blood Sect is also involved in this affair.”

Even when the North Blood Sect was mentioned, she did not show any particular agitation.

“Did you expect that?”

“Their second son got entangled with you.”

“You know he is dead, don’t you?”

Of course, the Ice Palace Lord knew.

“I heard he took his own life, shocked by the kidnapping this time.”

“Do you believe that?”

Instead of answering, she drank her tea.

“What do you think of those who would kill even their own children for the sake of their desires?”

“I am one of those people as well. I am someone to whom the Ice Palace matters more than my child.”

Her answer was both self-deprecating and provocative.

“It seems I studied you incorrectly, Palace Lord. The Palace Lord I studied is someone who cherishes her daughter more than anyone.”

The Ice Palace Lord stared at me. Her eyes asked how I could know that.

I may not know anything else, but I do know that one thing for certain. How much you love your daughter.

Though you have never properly expressed it, and though you even seem unaware of your own heart.

“Well, even so, it is all right.”

I brought in someone who worked well and got through in any situation.

“My father would be the same.”

As expected, the Ice Palace Lord smiled faintly. She too would know well what kind of person the Heavenly Demon of this generation was.

And what I wanted to say was this.

“My father, and you as well, Palace Lord, are all thinking wrongly.”

At the words she had not expected, the Ice Palace Lord looked slightly surprised. She likely had not imagined I would say not only she, who sat before me, but even the Heavenly Demon was thinking wrongly.

“In what way?”

I said to her first what I wanted to say to my father someday.

“I want to tell you not to live a life where you sacrifice everything for your organization, then look back and find no one there.”

“!”

“It is a life you ran for the sake of those behind you. And because of that, you ran looking only ahead. Yet then you sigh, ‘How could you do this to me?’ That sort of life. You must look back. Far more often, and far more carefully, than you think is enough. Cruelly enough, seven or eight times out of ten, they will not even know you looked back. Because they too must look back at what is behind them.”

I rose from my seat and bowed politely to her.

“I want to tell you that now is the time to look back at your daughter. Fortunately, she still seems to be standing behind you.”

* * *

The next day, I went to see the Ice Palace Lord again.

“What brings you today?”

“I wondered whether I was too rude yesterday, so I came to see you again today.”

“You told me to speak with my daughter, yet you are the one who came again.”

Fortunately, she did not seem angry over yesterday’s matter. The issue of her daughter must have been an important one to her as well.

“You still haven’t called your daughter, have you?”

The Ice Palace Lord could not answer. If she had been someone who could look back the moment she was told to, she would have lived while looking back from the start.

“I know such things do not easily change after being told only once.”

“So? Do you mean to keep coming until they do?”

“That is my intention.”

“What is the reason you are doing this?”

I did feel for the two of them, but there was another reason as well.

It was precisely because of Seo Nak. He would be watching my meetings with the Ice Palace Lord.

I met her yesterday, and I met her today. What if I met her again tomorrow?

Judging by his obsessive nature, I guessed he would seek a certain perfection or order even in human relationships.

The Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult, who had clearly shown signs of suspecting him, was meeting the Ice Palace Lord every day? This would certainly shake his order.

I had to quickly find out what role he played in this affair. Whether he was merely a good-natured elder with obsessive tendencies, their hands and feet, or the body itself.

“What other reason could there be? I want things to go well for the Young Palace Lord. If not for her, I could not have come here this time. I would not have been able to see you so often either, Palace Lord. Since you studied me, you must know well. I truly like spending time with the rising talents of the younger generation. I am someone who even dances with them.”

No matter how hard she tried to grasp me, it would not be easy. As long as the things she was certain were false were in fact my truth.

“Have you ever felt as though you had lost your way?”

What was he going to say now, bringing this up? With that emotion plainly written on her face, the Ice Palace Lord shook her head.

“I feel it often. I felt it yesterday as I left after seeing you, Palace Lord. Who was I to say such things to you so arrogantly? In the first place, I had only followed the Drunken Demon to the North Sea because I wanted to spend time with him.”

I bowed my head politely to her.

“I apologize for my arrogance yesterday.”

After silently gazing at me for a moment, she asked,

“You said you were abstaining from alcohol?”

“Yes. Abstinence out of loyalty.”

“When does it end?”

I understood that she meant we should have a drink together.

“It will not be long.”

Of course, I added the decisive words.

“If you trust me, Palace Lord.”

* * *

Seo Nak made his move on the evening of the day Geom Mugeuk visited the Palace Lord’s Hall for the third day in a row.

“Welcome, Elder.”

“I stopped by on my way past.”

From the time the Ice Palace Lord had been the Young Palace Lord until now, Seo Nak had always treated her with courtesy.

Seo Nak immediately revealed the reason he had come.

“I heard you have been meeting often with the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult.”

“He has come to see me for three days straight.”

Seo Nak asked cautiously,

“Someone of the Young Cult Leader’s standing would not move without reason. What do you think his ulterior motive is?”

By using the expression ulterior motive, he revealed what his own feelings toward Geom Mugeuk were.

“He told me to try to get along well with my daughter.”

Seo Nak waited for the next words. Surely that was not all.

“For three days, that was mainly what we talked about.”

Seo Nak could not believe it.

‘Is the Palace Lord deceiving me?’

Naturally, he could not help but think that. The Geom Mugeuk who had faced him was seasoned beyond his years. Each and every one of the remarks he casually thrust at him had thorns and bones.

Yet he had come for three days just to tell her to get along with Han Seol? It was difficult to believe.

“As you know well, the demonic practitioners are people who cannot be trusted.”

“I do not trust them either.”

If you do not trust them, then why did you bring them in?

Forcing down the words that had risen to his throat, Seo Nak said,

“Whatever the Young Cult Leader’s intentions may be, I think it would be best to send him away as soon as possible.”

Then he added with emphasis,

“For the sake of the Ice Palace as well.”

At that, the Ice Palace Lord said quietly,

“You say it is for the sake of the Ice Palace again.”

Seo Nak flinched and was about to ask what she meant by that when the Ice Palace Lord spoke one step ahead.

“You are right. Nothing good can come of demonic practitioners staying in the palace for long. I will tell the Young Cult Leader to resolve this matter within a set time.”

Though she had obediently followed his will, Seo Nak noticed that the Ice Palace Lord was different from usual. Somehow, in some subtle way, she was different.

The Ice Palace Lord had always stood at the distance and position he had set for her. And in that moment, he felt it. She was standing just slightly askew from her original place.

“This is all the path for the sake of the Ice Palace.”

Seo Nak left those words behind and descended from the Palace Lord’s Hall. As he went down the steps, he recalled what the Ice Palace Lord had said earlier.

—You say it is for the sake of the Ice Palace again.

Again? When had he said it before? It was something he said as a habit, so he could not tell which time she meant.

When he came down from the Palace Lord’s Hall like that, Seo Nak met Geom Mugeuk at the entrance.

“Elder Seo?”

“Young Cult Leader.”

The two faced each other and exchanged greetings.

“Are you on your way back from seeing the Palace Lord?”

“I am. And you?”

He had shown a sight there was no particular need to show, but Seo Nak remained calm.

“I came to see the Palace Lord as well. If I had come just a little earlier, we could have seen her together. What a pity.”

“I hear you have been visiting her often these days.”

“Opportunities to learn from the Palace Lord’s lofty insight are rare.”

“Then go and speak with her.”

“After seeing the Palace Lord, I plan to go to the North Blood Sect.”

At the mention of the North Blood Sect, Seo Nak’s steps, which had been turning away, stopped.

“Why the North Blood Sect?”

“They have lost a child, so their grief must be great. I should go and offer my condolences.”

“Young Cult Leader, you are truly kind.”

Seo Nak did not believe the claim that he was going to offer condolences.

His mood soured. Geom Mugeuk was going here and there, disturbing everything around him.

“Then I will see you again next time.”

When Geom Mugeuk bent his upper body in a fist-and-palm salute, Seo Nak saw it. A single letter protruding from Geom Mugeuk’s robes.

‘No. Could it be? Is that the letter he said stated that Wonju had given me the Hyangseol Bingju?’

Geom Mugeuk saw it. The face that had always smiled benevolently in any situation stiffened for the first time.

His order had begun to collapse.

Chapter 411: The Young Cult Leader Is Exceptionally Skilled at Schemes.
-----------------------------------------------------------------------
“Let us go up together!”

Seo Nak knew how strange those words must have sounded. But having seen the letter in Geom Mugeuk’s robes, he could not simply send him to the Ice Palace Lord alone.

At his words, Geom Mugeuk, who had been about to head up to the palace lord’s hall, stopped in his tracks.

“Did you not say you had just met the Palace Lord and were on your way down?”

“I did. But just now, were you not disappointed? That you could not meet the Palace Lord together with me.”

“I only said that out of courtesy.”

As an awkwardness crept into Seo Nak’s smile, Geom Mugeuk waved a hand lightly, pretending it had been a joke.

“Come, let us go up together.”

As Seo Nak followed Geom Mugeuk up the stairs, the habitual smile vanished from his face.

If that letter was about the Hyangseol Ice Wine, he had to keep it from falling into the palace lord’s hands. Why?

Because he had received Hyangseol Ice Wine from the master of the snowfield.

In front of Geom Mugeuk and Han Seol, he had flatly denied receiving it. If Geom Mugeuk had been the only one present, he could have insisted that the Demonic Cult was framing him, but Han Seol had been there as well.

If a man respected by all spoke with two tongues? It would look suspicious to anyone.

‘Did he prepare that letter in advance? Or did he receive it by messenger hawk? Would there have been enough time for that? Or is it perhaps some other letter, and I’m doing this for nothing?’

All sorts of thoughts tangled in Seo Nak’s mind. When Geom Mugeuk had first come to see him and brought up the matter of the wine, he had thought it was merely a ploy to sound him out.

‘There really was a letter? And if the palace lord sees it?’

How should he respond? If the handwriting were examined, it would be revealed as the snowfield master’s writing. Even so, he would have to insist it was a forgery. There was one good thing about his opponent being from the Demonic Cult. People would believe that even a perfect forgery like this was possible.

“What are you thinking so deeply about?”

At the entrance to the palace lord’s hall, Geom Mugeuk was looking back at him.

What else would I be thinking? I’m thinking of smashing the back of your head, taking out that letter, and burning it.

Seo Nak smiled and said,

“Nothing. Let us go in.”

Geom Mugeuk and Seo Nak entered the palace lord’s hall.

When Seo Nak, who had just paid his respects and left, returned again, the Ice Palace Lord looked at him in puzzlement.

“Ah, I met the Young Cult Leader at the entrance, so we came up together.”

“I see.”

If you met at the entrance, wouldn’t it be normal to exchange greetings and each go your own way?

The thought crossed her mind, but she did not say it aloud. Rather, she was curious. It seemed that Geom Mugeuk had done something that left Elder Seo with no choice but to follow him.

‘Young Cult Leader, what sort of move did you make?’

Geom Mugeuk began lightly warming up before delivering the decisive blow. First, a pleasant little anecdote.

“Did you know? I told the Palace Lord that I found Elder Seo suspicious.”

Even so, Seo Nak wore the relaxed smile only an innocent man could display.

“And then the Palace Lord said this. Elder Seo would never be that sort of person. She rebuked me firmly.”

Seo Nak smiled brightly and conveyed his gratitude to the Ice Palace Lord.

“Thank you for trusting me.”

“You are the one person I trust.”

That truly was how she had lived until now. Every warrior of the Northern Sea Ice Palace acknowledged it as well. And now Geom Mugeuk was beginning to saw at that ancient, towering tree.

“I will say it again here. Elder Seo is the person I trust most. He is someone who has lived solely for the Ice Palace.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk asked Seo Nak,

“Elder Seo, do you cherish the Palace Lord as well?”

“Of course. I cherish the Palace Lord and the Ice Palace more than myself.”

Geom Mugeuk stared straight at him and asked,

“Then why did you do it?”

“What do you mean?”

“Why did you kill the master of Baekju Snowfield?”

Now Geom Mugeuk had gone one step further and pointed to Seo Nak as the one who had killed her. The atmosphere that had begun with smiles went cold in an instant.

“Why, exactly, are you insulting me like this?”

Both the one pressing the accusation and the one evading it were dignified.

In that moment, the Ice Palace Lord felt a slight sense of incongruity.

She had known Seo Nak for a very long time. Though he was usually laughing genially, he was by no means an easygoing man. Especially when it came to his own reputation, he was even more sensitive.

If being accused of killing the snowfield master was a false charge, the Seo Nak she knew would have been a little angrier than this. Even if the other party was the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult. Yet now, somehow, he seemed cowed.

Most decisively, these words were missing.

—Then show me the evidence.

Those were words Seo Nak could never say. If he did, Geom Mugeuk would take the letter from his robes and say, “The evidence is right here, is it not?”

Because she did not know this underlying situation, the Ice Palace Lord felt that strange incongruity. It had been odd from the beginning that he had followed Geom Mugeuk back up here.

‘Elder Seo is being swayed by someone this young?’

It was truly a surprising and new experience.

Then again, she was hardly in a position to speak of others.

After Geom Mugeuk had told her to look behind her as she lived, she too had spent the night sleepless.

Those words themselves were not anything special, but fortunately, the words that Han Seol was still standing behind her had kept circling in her mind, preventing her from sleeping.

If that was so, then before it grew any later, she would have to look back. Not merely look back once, as Geom Mugeuk had said, but look back, and look back again. She would have to keep looking back.

And yet she still had not even managed to call her daughter aside.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been facing Seo Nak, turned toward the Ice Palace Lord.

“Ah, I came to see the Palace Lord today because…”

At the same time, Geom Mugeuk’s hand moved toward the robe where the letter was tucked.

Just then, Seo Nak quickly cut in and changed the subject.

“Young Cult Leader, I hear you spoke to the Palace Lord about Seol?”

In that moment, the Ice Palace Lord took note of two things.

First, Seo Nak was not the sort of person to interrupt someone while they were speaking. Even if he had something to refute, he was someone who waited until the other person had finished before speaking calmly.

And second, the topic he had interrupted with was Seol?

Incongruity was piled upon incongruity.

As if Seo Nak had wanted to say those words. When Geom Mugeuk suddenly seemed about to take out the letter, they had slipped out in the moment. He knew better than anyone that this was unlike him, but for now, he had to get through this moment somehow.

‘Would it be better to let him deliver the letter and insist it is forged?’

During the brief silence that followed, the Ice Palace Lord saw it. A smile forming on Geom Mugeuk’s face as he looked at her. His gaze seemed to be saying, Watch closely. See what kind of person your Elder Seo is.

Geom Mugeuk turned toward Seo Nak. The hand that had been about to draw out the letter naturally lowered again.

“I mainly told her that mother and daughter should get along. The Young Palace Lord is softer-hearted than she looks.”

“The Young Palace Lord is soft-hearted? Among the rising talents, I thought she had a rarely strong character.”

“It seems our Elder Seo knows less about the Young Palace Lord than I do, though I have only seen her for a few days. That is what happens when you do not look at a person properly and only glance at them roughly. Once preconceptions pile up as well, you come to know the other person even less.”

“That is arrogance on your part!”

Every word truly infuriated him, but Seo Nak had to keep saying something, anything, to prevent that letter from coming out.

If Geom Mugeuk forgot to hand over the letter and left this place, he would somehow gain a chance to steal it away.

Of course, even in that moment, the incongruity the Ice Palace Lord felt toward Seo Nak was growing larger and larger.

And another thought occurred to her.

The Ice Palace Lord wondered whether what Geom Mugeuk had just said might also apply to her.

Because she had not looked properly at Seo Nak, only preconceptions had piled up.

Could this sense of incongruity be the dregs produced by those very preconceptions?

How could she deny that she, too, had borne the position of Ice Palace Lord on her shoulders and run forward all her life, looking only ahead?

The stone Geom Mugeuk had thrown struck Seo Nak and continued to send out ripples, and those ripples were reaching even her.

“You said you were going to the North Blood Sect?”

“That is right.”

“Go on, then. If you are too late, it would be discourteous to them.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at him and smiled meaningfully.

“Very well.”

Geom Mugeuk paid his respects to the Ice Palace Lord.

“I will see you next time.”

Seo Nak was relieved. Even if the letter were delivered, he would somehow prepare for it, but the best course was for it not to be delivered in the first place.

‘He says he is going to the North Blood Sect? How should I take that letter from him?’

Just as he was feeling that initial relief, Geom Mugeuk turned toward the Ice Palace Lord and spoke quickly.

“This is a surprise gift! I came today to give this to the Palace Lord.”

Only then did Seo Nak understand. Geom Mugeuk had been toying with him, pretending he might give it or might not. He had acted as though he had forgotten and mocked him until the very end. Damn him! Does he think I will meekly admit it just because he hands that over?

The Ice Palace Lord took the letter Geom Mugeuk handed her and read it.

When she checked its contents, she flinched in surprise. From that expression, Seo Nak was certain it was that letter. There was nothing else that could surprise this woman, who rarely showed emotional change, to such an extent.

“It is a fabricated letter.”

Seo Nak spoke in a subdued voice.

“When the Young Cult Leader came last time, he threatened me with that forged letter. But let me state this clearly: I have never received Hyangseol Ice Wine.”

His tone was calm and composed. Now that he had said it even to the palace lord, there was nowhere to retreat. The wine had become something he had never received, forever.

At that, the Ice Palace Lord asked with an expression of incomprehension,

“What are you talking about? Hyangseol Ice Wine?”

In that instant, Seo Nak’s heart sank.

‘It was not that letter!’

This had been the true mockery.

He could have simply stayed still, but with his own mouth he had brought up the Hyangseol Ice Wine.

“May I see that letter?”

The Ice Palace Lord handed him the letter. Written on it were the names of several dishes, along with nearby taverns and restaurants.

He could understand why the Ice Palace Lord had been surprised when she saw it. At the very bottom of the letter was written, “These are dishes your daughter likes. Please spend some quiet time together.”

“My subordinate Ian has been eating and sleeping together with the Young Palace Lord lately, so he found out. The inns and restaurants written beneath are the places nearby that make those dishes best. I investigated them myself. So that you might have a meal with your daughter in a different atmosphere.”

For the first time, Seo Nak’s expression collapsed. It was neither a smile nor a stiffened face, but a new expression altogether. The emotion in it was rage. Even when he tried to smile, no smile came.

The target of that rage was not only Geom Mugeuk. It was also anger and disappointment toward himself for being played by such a shallow trick. He had clearly thought from the beginning that it might not be that letter, yet swept up by the strange atmosphere, he had become convinced that it was.

But he did not know. Today’s mistake had not been caused merely by one moment of faulty judgment.

It had happened because his order had already begun to collapse under Geom Mugeuk’s hand.

Even so, the wall of the man called Seo Nak, built up over a long time, was thick. He smiled as though it were nothing and gathered himself.

“I overreacted because I thought that Young Cult Leader was so adept at schemes. I hope the Palace Lord will understand. The Young Cult Leader has been slandering me with the matter I just misunderstood.”

As if she understood everything, the Ice Palace Lord nodded.

“No matter what evidence the Young Cult Leader brings, I trust you, Elder.”

“Thank you, Palace Lord.”

This time, Seo Nak glared at Geom Mugeuk. This time I lost. The words, I truly lost, rose of their own accord.

“Young Cult Leader, I do not know what scheme you have in mind, but you will not be able to destroy the trust between the Palace Lord and me.”

After saying those words, Seo Nak respectfully bade farewell to the Ice Palace Lord and left. He should not have come up in the first place. But that detestable fellow had been waiting at the entrance to hook him, and he had been caught helplessly.

When only the two of them remained, the Ice Palace Lord asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Why are you provoking him to this extent? Are you trying to show me what that man’s true nature is?”

If so, she thought he was using a method that was far too dangerous and extreme.

At that, Geom Mugeuk gave an unexpected answer.

“No.”

“No?”

“It is true that I am trying to make his true nature reveal itself, but I am not trying to show it to you, Palace Lord.”

“Then whom are you trying to show?”

Instead of answering, Geom Mugeuk bade her farewell and left.

“Make sure you go to one of the places written there. They really are dishes your daughter likes.”

Geom Mugeuk descended from the palace lord’s hall.

As he stepped out through the entrance, Ian came running urgently from ahead. Just from his expression, Geom Mugeuk could tell the situation was serious.

“Where?”

“Baekju Snowfield!”

He did not ask what had happened. If an incident had occurred, then getting there even a little faster was the best course. He sprinted there with the Swift Step.

In an instant, he arrived at Baekju Snowfield.

There was a reason Ian had needed to come urgently and fetch him.

The place was a sea of blood. Amid several corpses collapsed in every direction, one person stood. The warriors of the Ice Palace had surrounded him in layer upon layer, pointing their swords at him.

The man standing alone, drenched in blood, was the Drunken Demon.

Chapter 412: Looks Like He Finally Saw It
-----------------------------------------
“Lord Chwima!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s call, Chwima lifted his head and looked over.

Seeing that he was not in a state of madness and rage, Geom Mugeuk felt relieved. If he was in his right mind, there was no way he would have done something like this.

“There’s no need to explain it twice anyway, so let’s wait a bit.”

Chwima understood Geom Mugeuk’s words and nodded. On the other hand, the Ice Palace martial artists surrounding him grew even more tense at Geom Mugeuk’s appearance. Some of them turned toward Geom Mugeuk and stood on guard.

They seemed convinced that Chwima was the one behind this incident.

A short while later, Ian arrived at the scene, followed by Han Seol.

The two women were startled. Among the dead was Jo Myeong, the man they had met at Baekju Snowfield who had been in charge of the liquor ingredients. He was the very person who had told them that the Snowfield Master had drunk with Elder Seo several times.

The two women looked at each other, then each turned her gaze toward a different person. Han Seol looked at Chwima first.

‘No way… did you kill them?’

Given the situation, it looked to anyone as though Chwima had done it.

Ian, on the other hand, looked at Geom Mugeuk first.

‘What do we do? It looks like Lord Chwima has fallen into a trap.’

If the person standing here with her had not been Geom Mugeuk, her heart would have been pounding as if it were about to burst.

But when she saw the calm look in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes, her agitated heart settled down as if it had been a lie. His gaze seemed as though it could resolve any danger in the world. No—even if he could not resolve it, that was fine. He was someone whose mere presence was enough.

How long did they wait like that?

From every direction, martial artists carrying shields and spears appeared and began sealing off the surroundings.

They were the North Wind Unit, the elite of the Northern Sea Ice Palace who obeyed only the orders of the Ice Palace Mistress. Armed with armor and shields, they were composed of those whose martial prowess surpassed any other elite organization within the Ice Palace, and one could tell from the extraordinary look in their eyes just how formidable they were.

The North Wind Unit blocked every direction like an iron wall, and some of its members lined up to the left and right, creating a path with their shields. Through their perfectly ordered ranks, the Ice Palace Mistress walked in. Behind her followed guards dressed in pure white martial robes.

Until now, he had only seen her inside the Palace Mistress’s Hall, where she seemed like someone alone in a castle of ice. But seen outside like this, she was completely different.

The Ice Palace Mistress silently looked at the corpses. Her expression was colder than ever. She had confirmed that among the dead was someone who did not know martial arts.

Only then did Geom Mugeuk ask Chwima.

“Lord Chwima, what happened?”

At last, Chwima opened his mouth.

“That man contacted me, saying he had something to tell me and wanted to meet.”

The corpse Chwima looked at was none other than Jo Myeong, the man Ian and Han Seol had met.

“I was about to speak with him when the blood vessels in his neck suddenly burst. Then the people who came running after hearing the sound burst as well.”

At that, someone spoke as if shouting.

“How are we supposed to believe your words?”

The person who arrived next was none other than Seo Nak. Only a moment ago, he had withdrawn from the Palace Mistress’s Hall with a troubled expression, but his bearing now was different from then.

“You’re covered in blood like that, and you expect us to believe you?”

The visible circumstances were unfavorable to Chwima. Since he was drenched in blood, he looked even more like the culprit.

It was then that Geom Mugeuk stepped forward.

“That is precisely why he can be believed.”

When everyone’s eyes turned to him, Geom Mugeuk calmly asked them,

“If Lord Chwima had truly killed them, would he have gotten blood on himself?”

Geom Mugeuk turned to Chwima and said,

“Even if they burst right in front of him, if he had wanted to, he could have avoided all that blood. And yet the fact that his clothes and hands are stained with blood like that…”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk said,

“Means he was trying to save them.”

Chwima silently nodded. Even without bothering to defend himself, Geom Mugeuk had grasped the situation exactly.

Geom Mugeuk said to the Ice Palace Mistress,

“If the Demon Venerable had truly set his mind on killing those people, he would have killed them without anyone knowing. He would not have ended up surrounded here while covered in blood. You know this, do you not? What kind of people the Demon Venerables of our cult are.”

Of course, the Ice Palace Mistress knew very well. If Chwima had meant to do so, the martial artists who had first surrounded him would already all be corpses.

Seo Nak then asked in agitation,

“Then why is he standing there like that?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze remained on the Ice Palace Mistress.

“He waited out of courtesy toward the Palace Mistress. Believing that you would judge the matter wisely. If he had left this place, the problem would have grown even larger.”

A faint smile formed at the corner of Chwima’s mouth. Yes, this was why he could remain calm. As long as Geom Mugeuk was here, he would not be so easily framed.

“The words of demonic practitioners must not be believed so readily. Naturally, he should be detained until the investigation is complete.”

At Seo Nak’s firm assertion, Geom Mugeuk refuted him.

“That would not be fair.”

“Fair? What do you mean?”

Geom Mugeuk spoke to the martial artists around them.

“You have all likely heard the rumors that we are investigating the death of the Baekhwa Snowfield Master. During our investigation of that incident, we uncovered suspicious circumstances. One of the experts of this Ice Palace had secretly asked the deceased Snowfield Master to make Hyangseol Ice Wine for him.”

In that instant, Seo Nak’s expression stiffened. He had not expected Geom Mugeuk to bring that up here.

“That expert concealed that fact from us. But even after we uncovered it, we did not detain him. Why? Because we respected the Northern Sea Ice Palace and the Ice Palace Mistress.”

The martial artists around them looked at one another and exchanged glances.

Seo Nak could not protest. If the conversation dragged on, Geom Mugeuk would reveal here and now that the suspicious person was him.

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes sank coldly. Since coming to the Ice Palace, he had never once shown anger, but for the first time, he revealed an aura as sharp as frost.

“We respect you, yet you say you will not respect us?”

If the Ice Palace Mistress had not been present, he would have released his force and pressured Seo Nak even more strongly. But because he stood before the Ice Palace Mistress, he observed propriety.

Han Seol was startled by Geom Mugeuk’s sudden change. The man before her now was not the Geom Mugeuk who made frivolous remarks. That cold appearance, rather, suited the Ice Palace all too well.

At last, the Ice Palace Mistress, who had been watching, stepped forward.

“I understand what the Young Cult Leader is saying.”

After looking back and forth between Chwima and Geom Mugeuk for a moment, the Ice Palace Mistress ordered her subordinates,

“Lower your swords.”

The martial artists surrounding them lowered their swords.

Seo Nak did not give up until the end.

“Palace Mistress, you must not believe their words.”

“Yes, we must thoroughly investigate the cause of death of the deceased.”

The Ice Palace Mistress said to Chwima,

“Do not leave the palace without this palace’s permission.”

She would not confine him, but he was not to leave recklessly either. That was the compromise the Ice Palace Mistress had reached.

Seo Nak added to it,

“Until the results of the investigation come out, the palace’s experts should be made to watch them.”

The Ice Palace Mistress accepted that suggestion. In exchange, she added one condition for Geom Mugeuk’s sake.

“Only until the investigation is over.”

Chwima waited for Geom Mugeuk’s reaction. In this matter, he had to move according to Geom Mugeuk’s decision. Geom Mugeuk said nothing. It meant he should accept the compromise.

Chwima readily accepted the proposal.

“Very well.”

When Chwima left the place, Ian followed behind him.

Before leaving, Geom Mugeuk said to the Ice Palace Mistress,

“You asked why I was provoking him to this extent.”

Earlier, she had asked Geom Mugeuk. Why was he provoking Seo Nak so far? Why was he trying to show her his true nature?

Geom Mugeuk had answered that he was indeed provoking him, but not to show the Ice Palace Mistress. Now Geom Mugeuk answered that question.

“It seems he has finally seen it.”

It meant there was someone behind him. The reason he had provoked Seo Nak was to provoke and draw out the one behind him.

“Then I will see you later.”

After greeting the Ice Palace Mistress and turning to leave, Geom Mugeuk looked back at Han Seol and said,

“What are you doing? Are you not coming with us? Did you not decide to become one of us? Are you only one of us when the situation is good?”

The Ice Palace Mistress and Seo Nak looked at Han Seol.

The Ice Palace Mistress looked at her daughter without revealing any emotion, while Seo Nak was the opposite.

Seo Nak shook his head with a firm expression that told her absolutely not to follow. She had never defied him until now.

“I will go with you.”

Han Seol followed Geom Mugeuk.

After the three of them left, Seo Nak looked at the Ice Palace Mistress and said,

“The Young Palace Mistress must not be allowed to associate with the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.”

At that moment, the Ice Palace Mistress could not clearly tell whether his attitude was shamelessness or a sincere concern for the Ice Palace. That was how many long years she had spent with him.

He would betray all those long years? That thought clouded her cold judgment.

“I will keep a close eye on it.”

The Ice Palace Mistress turned and left the place.

Once she had gone, the North Wind Unit and the guards withdrew from the area in an instant.

To the martial artists who were about to examine the corpses, Seo Nak said with a grief-stricken face,

“Leave me alone for a moment.”

Everyone there knew well how much Seo Nak cherished the people of the Ice Palace, so they all quietly withdrew and gave him space.

Seo Nak looked down at the corpses alone. Instead of sorrow or regret, he was lost in other thoughts.

‘He is helping me.’

Just as he had been driven into a corner because of the Hyangseol Ice Wine, an incident like this had occurred.

It was at that very moment.

Drip, drip.

Blood began to flow from the pool that had gathered thickly on the ground. The blood flowed toward Seo Nak’s feet. Thinking that such a thing could happen, Seo Nak stepped aside.

Then, as if it were alive, the blood changed direction and headed toward Seo Nak.

Startled, Seo Nak moved to the opposite side and avoided it.

Then the blood changed direction again and flowed toward Seo Nak.

Stepping backward, Seo Nak asked,

“Is that you?”

At Seo Nak’s question, the blood that had been flowing stopped. It was as if it had understood his words.

Seo Nak said in a trembling voice,

“Thank you for this matter. I will not make another mistake. I will deal with them.”

As if it had understood him, the blood that had stopped for a moment slowly began to return to the pool it had originally come from. Astonishingly, no bloodstain remained on the floor where the blood had flowed.

In that way, the blood returned as though being absorbed back into its original pool.

Seo Nak slowly walked over and looked down at the pool of blood. Before he knew it, the blood had returned to being ordinary blood.

Perhaps this matter had not been help for him, but a warning to him.

His face was reflected on the surface of the blood. It was the resolute face of a man who had made a decision from which he could no longer turn back.

* * *

“Stop swallowing your anger and just drink instead.”

Despite Geom Mugeuk’s words, Chwima drank tea.

“That makes you even scarier.”

Their opponent had trapped Chwima. In other words, he had trapped a Demon Venerable. He must have been furious to the top of his head, yet Chwima was still only drinking tea.

Geom Mugeuk said to Ian,

“I do not know who it is, but they chose the wrong opponent.”

Ian, too, could tell that Chwima was truly very angry. What was astonishing was the way he restrained himself. She had thought that among the Eight Demon Venerables, he would be the one most likely to lose his temper. Yet he was controlling himself more admirably than any Demon Venerable.

“I suppose this is why you were able to become Lord Chwima.”

When Ian sincerely expressed her admiration, Chwima gave an awkward smile. Since he was a man who drank, he did make efforts to regulate himself, but he was an emotional person. The reason he had been able to remain so cool-headed after coming here was because of one desire.

The desire to show Geom Mugeuk a fine side of himself. He wanted to show that throwing himself into a lake while drunk was not all there was to him.

Geom Mugeuk said to the three of them,

“Thanks to this, we now know for certain. Seo Nak is definitely connected to the force behind him.”

At last, the Blood King had begun to move. As soon as Seo Nak was in trouble, he acted immediately. He was not far away.

The one startled by those words was Han Seol.

“A force behind him?”

“I believe someone has joined hands with him and is plotting a major conspiracy.”

Unlike Chwima and Ian, who already knew that fact, Han Seol did not know about any of it.

“Why did you not tell me beforehand?”

At her question, Geom Mugeuk answered,

“That sounds like a question you should ask the Palace Mistress.”

“My mother knows as well?”

“I believe so.”

When he sensed Han Seol’s expression stiffening, as if she felt excluded, Geom Mugeuk said,

“It is likely not merely a problem on the Palace Mistress’s side.”

“What do you mean?”

“I mean it is time for the Young Palace Mistress to have a conversation with her mother.”

What he had said to the Ice Palace Mistress, he now said to Han Seol as well. Because he thought what the two of them needed was conversation.

“In my relationship with my father, I was the one who approached him first. If you think about what kind of man my father is, that was truly no easy thing. But if they have raised us to our age, is it not a child’s duty to make them say at least once, ‘So this is how much the child has grown’?”

Of course, Han Seol had never once thought of it that way.

“The problem with your relationship is not whether it is distant or close, cold or warm. The problem is that it has hardened.”

“!”

The word “hardened” struck Han Seol’s heart. She felt that he was right. Now, before she even felt hurt, she would think, “Mother has always been that kind of person.”

She had never thought of drawing what was distant closer, nor of changing what was cold into something warm.

“Why are you saying these things to me?”

“Because I meddle too much.”

There must be some intention behind it. Han Seol could not fathom Geom Mugeuk’s mind. He was someone who naturally made the words “what a strange person” come to mind.

Ian asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Why did they frame Lord Chwima?”

“They were that desperate. In other words, it also means Elder Seo is someone they absolutely need.”

Through this incident, Geom Mugeuk had learned several things about their opponent. Moreover, he was already planning the next step.

“With this incident, they have tied our feet.”

Outside their residence, experts from the Ice Palace were standing guard. It meant they would monitor them wherever they went.

“This actually works out well.”

“It works out well?”

Not only Ian, but even Chwima and Han Seol looked at Geom Mugeuk with expressions asking what he meant.

“Because in reverse, no matter what we plot, it will become something we could not have done.”

“Ah! Because we are being watched here! But I do not think it will be easy to slip out secretly.”

“We will not be leaving. There is someone who can do that splendidly in our stead.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at one person. Chwima and Ian’s gazes followed Geom Mugeuk’s to that person as well.

Facing the three people looking at her, Han Seol shouted before even hearing the words.

“I refuse!”

Chapter 413: Worrying About Lord Drunken Demon Who Only Drinks Tea!
-------------------------------------------------------------------
To begin with the conclusion: Han Seol could not refuse.

It was hard enough to withstand Geom Mugeuk’s persuasion alone, but today he had an ally. Ian.

“When I saw that man’s corpse earlier, this thought occurred to me. If we hadn’t gone looking for him, would he still be alive?”

Han Seol could feel that Ian was angry over his death.

To be honest, until the moment she heard those words from Ian, Han Seol had not thought of the man even once. No, she had forgotten him completely.

He was someone she had seen for the first time here. Could she really be that concerned about him?

If it was pretense, then this, too, would be pretense. Beginning with the very fact that they had come all the way to the North Sea to save the husband of a woman who had given them a cup of water.

“Do you think he died because of us?”

“No. He died because of the bastard who killed him.”

Ian did not indulge in pointless self-reproach. After learning so much from Geom Mugeuk, she ought to be able to show at least this much wisdom.

“I just don’t want his death to become meaningless.”

At times like this, Ian’s belief that kind people lived longer would waver. The dead man, too, had told them what he knew out of kindness.

Ian looked at Geom Mugeuk and displayed another bit of wisdom.

“Please avenge him.”

Because she had sensed that the enemy was not someone she could handle. At times like this, she simply had to move as Geom Mugeuk instructed.

“I will.”

After answering readily, he even looked after Ian’s heart.

“Things like this leave wounds on the heart. Since it’s the death of someone you didn’t really know, it may seem like a shallow wound, but it’s one that remains forever. You’ll probably keep thinking of him. Even seeing a brewery as you pass by will remind you.”

Geom Mugeuk knew. Sometimes, wounds like these lasted far longer than the great injuries one had suffered directly, tormenting a person all the more.

“Don’t try to forget it on purpose. That will only deepen the wound.”

“Then what should I do?”

“It gets cut, and cut again. Another wound forms over that wound, and then another. In the end, you accept it like this: so this is the pattern originally engraved on my heart. Congratulations on the new pattern engraved on your heart this time.”

A bright smile formed on Ian’s lips. The only person in the world who congratulated her on pain—what more needed to be said?

“If my heart is only like this, I can’t even imagine what your heart must be like, Young Master.”

Ian tried to picture Geom Mugeuk’s heart. Was it a fierce heart engraved with dozens, hundreds of scars, and thus able to overcome any situation? Or was it a clean heart, forged from ten-thousand-year cold iron from the start, without a single mark cut into it?

“You know better than anyone what my heart is like, don’t you?”

“What? How would I— Ah!”

Ian lowered her head deeply. She had momentarily forgotten that Geom Mugeuk had called her his heart, and had only just remembered.

Watching the two of them, Han Seol felt a strange sensation.

To blame oneself over the death of a stranger, and a leader who consoled that pain.

Until now, it had seemed like pretense, like a deceptive display. But now her feelings toward that relationship had changed. To her own surprise, she was beginning to feel the slightest bit envious.

This time, Han Seol’s gaze turned toward Chwima. Come to think of it, Chwima had been covered in that blood because he said he would save them, hadn’t he?

‘Am I too inhuman?’

Because of the three people from the Demonic Cult, she kept thinking thoughts she had never once had in her life. To think she would feel such emotions because of none other than demonic practitioners.

Geom Mugeuk said to Han Seol,

“Help us.”

“I said I refuse.”

“There is a reason you cannot refuse.”

“What reason?”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk said,

“I believe their ultimate targets are you and the Palace Lord.”

Han Seol was not surprised. If it was true that Elder Seo was involved in the conspiracy, it was obvious where that blade would be pointed. And yet she still could not believe it. He had been like family since she was young. He was targeting Mother and me?

“Do you truly have definite proof?”

When Han Seol asked once more, Geom Mugeuk answered firmly.

“It became certain when they framed Chwima this time. Seo Nak is one of them.”

For a moment, Han Seol could say nothing. The expressions on Chwima and Ian’s faces looked as if they believed Geom Mugeuk’s words completely.

“A person’s heart can become diamond, and before one knows it, melt softly like sugar candy. He probably did not harbor such intentions from the very beginning.”

Whether the Blood King had dug into him and changed his heart, or whether the Blood King had dug into him after his heart had changed, in the end, he had changed.

Though she had shouted that she would refuse for no reason, Han Seol knew. This was something she had to step forward and handle herself. A person of the Ice Palace had died, and the enemy was targeting her and her mother.

But could she truly trust these demonic practitioners?

The more this distrust faded, the more uneasy Han Seol became in turn. She could not allow her heart to soften.

“There is something I would like you to do once you leave.”

“What is it?”

“Meet the eldest son of the North Blood Sect. I believe the North Blood Sect is also involved in this matter.”

Elder Seo, whom she had regarded like an uncle, had joined hands with some force behind the scenes, and now the North Blood Sect was part of it as well?

Han Seol felt stifled. If all these words were true, the Ice Palace was truly in grave danger.

“The North Blood Sect Leader and Elder Seo are still too much for you to face, Young Palace Lord. So you must target the eldest son of the North Blood Sect. He will certainly know something. Whatever it is, you must find it out.”

Han Seol hesitated no longer. It was a matter to judge while moving.

“Very well. I’ll meet him.”

“Show him a taste cold enough to freeze his blood.”

“I will.”

As she was about to leave the room, Geom Mugeuk said,

“Before you go, make sure you meet your mother first.”

Han Seol, who had stopped for a moment, left the room without a word.

After she left, Ian asked worriedly,

“Will it be all right to send the Young Palace Lord out alone?”

“Are you worried?”

“The enemies aren’t easy opponents.”

“Don’t worry. Why are you worrying about the Young Palace Lord of the Ice Palace in the North Sea? You’d be better off worrying about our Chwima over there, who is just drinking tea!”

At that, Chwima abruptly said,

“You’re planning to go out too, aren’t you?”

Chwima’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

“You’re going to draw their attention to her and then deal with things from behind, aren’t you?”

Geom Mugeuk said with a smile,

“Now that you’ve quit drinking, you’re turning into a genius! Let’s keep you off alcohol from now on. That fine brain would be too much of a waste.”

“It’s already a brain pickled in liquor.”

Ian reproached herself inwardly.

‘And my brain is worse than that pickled one.’

She had not even faintly anticipated that Geom Mugeuk was planning to slip out in secret.

Ian asked Geom Mugeuk,

“What are you going to do once you’re outside?”

“As you saw earlier, the one behind this is a vulgar and merciless bastard.”

They had tried to frame Chwima and killed people who knew no martial arts.

“If they come out like this, we have to deal with them in a way that suits it. I’ll use your method.”

“My method? What method?”

“I’m going to kidnap both Elder Seo and the North Blood Sect Leader. If I cut off both arms, the torso will reveal itself.”

“Really?”

“Of course I’m joking.”

There was a part of him that truly wanted to do just that. Not kidnap them, but simply erase them both.

But he could not. If he did, the Blood King would never reveal himself. In addition, the bastard would kill countless people in retaliation. He was the kind of man who would kill two hundred if those two were killed.

“I’m going mad wanting a drink. Hurry up and bring that bastard here!”

At Chwima’s words, Geom Mugeuk replied,

“Fine. Once we wipe them all out, let’s really drink ourselves senseless. That day, we become dogs.”

Ian shot her hand up.

“One more dog, right here!”

Geom Mugeuk stuffed pillows, clothes, and the like into the bed to make it look as though someone were sleeping there. No one would dare enter this room without permission, but he made it just in case.

“You have to do a good job pretending I’m here. Then I’m off.”

“How are you going to get past those people?”

“You sure ask early.”

No matter how one looked at it, the number of people standing guard outside made it seem difficult to sneak out. Every single one of them was an expert, different from ordinary warriors merely watching someone.

As Geom Mugeuk was about to go outside, he said to Chwima,

“Oh, right, brother. I couldn’t say this earlier.”

Two gazes filled with goodwill and curiosity met in midair.

“It was impressive, how you got covered in blood trying to save those people.”

Leaving those words behind, Geom Mugeuk went outside.

A smile naturally formed on Chwima’s lips.

Geom Mugeuk probably knew better than anyone how hard he was trying right now.

Someone who recognized his efforts.

And someone who said it aloud.

That was Geom Mugeuk. When that thought occurred to him—

‘I really want a drink.’

Ian and Chwima watched through the window as Geom Mugeuk slipped out of the place. Among the Four Steps of the Wind God, the Shadow Concealment Step was displayed.

The Shadow Concealment Step was a footwork technique used when infiltrating somewhere. At first, it allowed one only to evade a single person’s eyes, but as one’s realm rose, the number of people one could avoid increased. The Shadow Concealment Step that had reached great accomplishment pierced through experts who plainly had both eyes open and escaped that place.

“What did we just see?”

At Ian’s admiration, Chwima drank his tea and said,

“Ice Palace experts getting their noses cut off with both eyes wide open.”

* * *

Han Seol entered the Palace Lord’s Hall.

“Palace Lord.”

At Han Seol’s visit, the Ice Palace Lord recalled Geom Mugeuk’s words.

— I wish to tell you that it is time you looked back at your daughter. Fortunately, she still seems to be standing behind you.

This was the first time they were meeting alone since she had heard those words.

“What brings you here?”

“I came because there is something I must tell you.”

“Speak.”

Han Seol newly realized that when she came to see her mother like this, she did not even exchange a light greeting. She had always begun with the main point.

Naturally, Han Seol also recalled Geom Mugeuk’s words.

— The relationship between you two is not a matter of being distant or close, cold or warm. The problem is that it has hardened.

But now that she was actually here, she felt that was not the problem.

‘Do I want to loosen this hardened relationship?’

She realized it was a matter of will. Her mother might not want such a relationship with her.

Watching her daughter, whose eyes flickered with various thoughts, the Ice Palace Lord sensed that she was different from usual.

Amid those thoughts, the conversation continued.

“I intend to meet the eldest son of the North Blood Sect.”

“Why him?”

“The Young Cult Leader believes the North Blood Sect is involved in this matter. I would like to confirm whether that is truly the case.”

It was unfamiliar, yet encouraging. This was the first time her daughter had stepped forward on her own to investigate something.

“Is this something you want to do?”

The moment she spoke, the Ice Palace Lord realized she had chosen her words poorly. What she had just said was the same as asking, “Is the Young Cult Leader perhaps making you do this?”

“It is because I want to.”

A brief silence flowed.

— Do not misunderstand. I only asked because you are doing something you usually do not.

— It is all right. I suppose I cannot help being influenced by the Young Cult Leader.

How nice it would be if such frank conversation could pass between them.

— You will be able to do well.

— Thank you for trusting me.

How nice it would be if such encouragement and gratitude could be exchanged. But reality was like this.

“If you provoke the North Blood Sect carelessly, problems may arise.”

“Yes, I suppose they may.”

Silence flowed once more.

Ordinarily, the Ice Palace Lord would have said, “It is still too soon.”

But with her daughter having come in person like this, saying she would try to do something, the words “You cannot” did not come easily.

“Will you still try?”

“Yes.”

Neither of them had known until now. They had always spoken like this, so they had lived without realizing anything was strange.

But now, wondering whether to open their hearts to the other and conscious of each other as they conversed, it felt truly awkward.

“I shall take my leave now.”

Han Seol offered her farewell and turned.

As she walked slowly toward the stairs, the Ice Palace Lord spoke from behind her.

“Have a cup of tea before you go.”

The one who showed courage first was the Ice Palace Lord. Yes. If someone was to turn their head, she, as the mother, should turn first.

Han Seol was inwardly surprised. When was the last time she had sat and drunk tea with her mother? She could not even remember.

“I will drink it when I return.”

Flustered, she refused, and the Ice Palace Lord did not urge her further. There was no need to feel hurt. Geom Mugeuk had said it: even if one looked back again and again, cruelly enough, seven or eight times out of ten, the other person would not even know they had looked.

And this attempt clearly had an effect. As Han Seol descended the stairs of the Palace Lord’s Hall, she regretted it.

Should I have had tea before leaving?

* * *

Smack!

Yang Seok, the eldest son of the North Blood Sect, struck a maidservant’s cheek without mercy.

“I was wrong, Young Master!”

Yang Seok, who had been walking hurriedly, nearly collided with the maidservant, and the water she had been carrying spilled, splashing a few drops onto his clothes.

“You would make the Young Palace Lord of the Ice Palace wait because of you? Or should I meet the Young Palace Lord in these wet clothes?”

It was not as though the water had splashed enough to be noticeable. He was simply displeased that someone had blocked his path while he was on his way to meet the Young Palace Lord.

“I’m sorry. I’m so sorry.”

Smack! Smack!

Despite her apology, Yang Seok struck her mercilessly. The maidservant’s lip split, and red handprints appeared on her cheeks.

“There is no time for this.”

At his subordinate’s words, Yang Seok hurriedly left.

The one fortunate thing was that he had beaten her without using internal energy. The Young Palace Lord of the Ice Palace had come to meet him, and he could not afford the ill luck of killing someone, so he simply left.

A short while after Yang Seok departed like that.

Someone returned to the place. It was Ha Gyeol, Yang Seok’s bodyguard. He was the very man who had stopped him earlier, saying there was no time.

“Let me see.”

Ha Gyeol lifted the fallen maidservant and examined her wounds. Blood was flowing from the torn wounds on her mouth and cheek.

Ha Gyeol wiped the blood away with his hand.

“I’m all right.”

His touch felt burdensome, but she did not dare shake off Ha Gyeol’s hand. Moreover, if he had not stopped him earlier, she did not know how much longer she would have been beaten.

“Fortunately, it seems your teeth are unhurt.”

The maidservant felt that Ha Gyeol’s hand was truly hot. She did not know what changes were now occurring in her face and in both her eyes.

“If you catch the Young Master’s eye for no reason, it will be disastrous, so stay in your room today.”

“Thank you.”

After returning to her room, the maidservant looked at her face in the bronze mirror.

Judging by the sensation when she had been struck, she had thought a great deal of blood had flowed down, but no blood remained on her face at all.

Not even a trace of where the blood had been remained.

‘He only wiped it roughly with his hand. How could this be?’

Because she was examining her face, she did not see it. A red circle was drawn around her neck and then vanished.

She would never know that her life was no longer her own, nor that she had given up far too much in exchange for a small kindness.

Chapter 414: The Young Cult Leader Is Sleeping.
-----------------------------------------------
“We’ve met three times?”

Han Seol had no memory of seeing Yang Seok, the eldest son of the Northern Blood Sect, three whole times. She only remembered meeting him once before.

“Today makes the fourth.”

“I’m sorry. My memory is lacking.”

“Not at all. You’re an extremely busy person, so it’s only natural.”

Though his pride was wounded inwardly, Yang Seok acted magnanimous, pretending to be broad-minded.

He liked Han Seol. And was he the only one? Among the young men living in the North Sea, who did not like her?

To win a beauty and, at the same time, gain the finest family of the North Sea Ice Palace.

But she was an ice-cold beauty. He had never heard of her giving her heart to anyone.

“But what brings you here today?”

First, Han Seol expressed her condolences.

“I heard about your younger brother. You must be deeply grieved.”

“My heart is not at ease, but I am managing to overcome it.”

Yang Seok was inwardly puzzled.

‘There’s no way this frosty Young Palace Mistress came here because of my brother.’

That was why Yang Seok had a guess as to her reason.

“I heard you’ve been investigating the death of the demonic practitioners and the Lord of Baekju Snowfield. Is it going well?”

“I’ve been looking into various things, but it isn’t easy.”

On her way here, she had considered many things. How should she approach Yang Seok without making him suspicious? So she had decided to make it seem as though she had come as part of that investigation, and thankfully, the other party asked first.

“Do you perhaps know anything about that incident?”

“I’d like to help, but I know nothing.”

Han Seol let out a light sigh and asked,

“Do you know that demonic practitioners are staying at our palace?”

“How could I not? My younger brother died because of them.”

Of course, if he were to speak the truth, it would have to be like this: Thanks to those demonic practitioners, I was able to kill my younger brother, who had been a thorn in my eye.

“The Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult is committing every sort of tyranny. He’s pressuring us to quickly present evidence regarding this incident as well.”

Yang Seok could clearly understand why Han Seol had come to find him.

‘This is actually an opportunity.’

She had never so much as glanced his way, no matter how much he had wanted to grow close to her.

“If there is anything I can do to help, I will do anything.”

While Yang Seok was thus trying hard to make a good impression on Han Seol, Chan was standing guard outside the reception hall where the two were.

Ha Gyeol walked toward him.

Chan looked at him indifferently, but in truth, he was observing Ha Gyeol closely. Given the nature of his profession, he had met countless guards until now.

When he first saw Ha Gyeol, Ha Gyeol possessed every one of the peculiar traits unique to guards.

Footsteps that stayed alert in every direction, eyes that swiftly scanned his surroundings, a tenacity that, when assessing an opponent, even tried to identify concealed hidden weapons, and even bloodshot eyes that revealed chronic fatigue. He was, in every sense, the very embodiment of a bodyguard warrior.

‘And yet he left his post?’

He had come all the way here with Yang Seok, but when Yang Seok entered the reception hall, he briefly left his position.

No matter how much this place was within the Northern Blood Sect, the fact that someone guarding none other than the heir had left his post was truly unexpected.

What was he going to do if an accident occurred in the meantime? What if a sudden fight broke out with the Young Palace Mistress?

Of course, it was something that would not happen, but the sort of person who worried about what others did not was precisely a bodyguard warrior.

Because he appeared to be a perfect guard on the surface, that action felt even more incongruous.

Chan felt like asking what could possibly have been more important than protecting the heir. Of course, Chan could not know. He could not know that Ha Gyeol had retraced the path he had taken and turned the maidservant’s blood into his own.

Just then, Ha Gyeol glanced up at the roof of the building.

“What is it?”

“Nothing.”

He had definitely sensed something.

Chan also examined the surroundings, but there was no one there. Even when he drew up his qi and searched more actively, he could feel no presence nearby.

‘He definitely seemed to have sensed something.’

If he had sensed something Chan had not? That meant he was more skilled than Chan.

Then, if he had sensed something just now, he should have informed Chan. Was this side merely a guest? Was she not the Young Palace Mistress of the Ice Palace?

It grated on his nerves in various ways, but Chan did not show it outwardly.

Meanwhile, the conversation inside the reception hall was in full swing. Of course, the one mostly speaking was Yang Seok, and the one listening was Han Seol.

Until she had arrived here, Han Seol had worried about whether she could act this well. But once she actually tried it, it was not as difficult as she had thought. She even wondered whether she might have a talent for deceiving others.

“There is one thing I’m curious about.”

“What is it?”

“Why did your younger brother become entangled with the Demonic Cult?”

For a moment, Yang Seok’s expression flinched, but he soon spoke calmly.

“As the Young Palace Mistress also knows, those Demonic Cult bastards are like a pack of wolves trying to latch on to anything and tear off a share.”

Exactly. She, too, had thought all demonic practitioners were like that.

“That’s true. On top of that, the ones who came this time are truly strange people.”

To him, it might have sounded like an insult, but the words contained praise.

Although she knew Yang Seok had not spoken honestly, Han Seol arranged for the next step with him.

“Will you meet the Young Cult Leader with me next time?”

The reason she made such a choice was entirely because of Ian.

When they had handled matters before, at the moment of decision, Ian had said they had done all they could and that they should leave the rest to Geom Mugeuk. And the result had been astonishingly good. This matter was in the same vein. Nor was he someone she wanted to continue meeting alone.

Yang Seok interpreted her proposal this way: she wanted him to lend her some strength in dealing with the Young Cult Leader.

“Very well. The sects of the North Sea should join forces.”

“Thank you. Then I’ll take my leave for today.”

Yang Seok regretted parting with her.

To think a day would come when he would have such a pleasant atmosphere with the Young Palace Mistress of the North Sea Ice Palace. Thanks to those Demonic Cult bastards, he had dealt with his younger brother, and he indulged in the pleasant fantasy that perhaps he might even gain a wife.

Han Seol left the place with Chan in tow. As they went, Chan glanced at Ha Gyeol. He was a perfect guard even now, and yet something about him kept feeling out of place.

After the two of them left, Yang Seok said to Ha Gyeol,

“She’s a cold woman, but she has charm.”

“If you put your tongue on ice, it does not come off easily.”

Yang Seok looked at Ha Gyeol coldly.

He neither trusted nor liked him. Ha Gyeol was a guard his father had assigned to him in order to monitor him.

Once in a while, when he looked at Ha Gyeol, he felt displeased. Ha Gyeol always maintained a respectful attitude, and yet there were strangely moments when he provoked him. This was one of those moments. Insolent bastard, who are you to tell me to be careful or not? You should watch your own tongue. Before I cut it off.

Just then, a warrior came running and said to Yang Seok,

“The Sect Master is looking for you.”

* * *

“If things go well, I think we may be able to draw in the Young Palace Mistress.”

Yang Seok reported to the Northern Blood Sect Master about what had happened with Han Seol.

“The Young Palace Mistress came to find out something through you.”

“I suspect as much as well. I will use her in reverse and uncover information from their side.”

The Northern Blood Sect Master nodded.

“Always be cautious.”

“I will keep that in mind.”

After making his report, he was about to withdraw when the Northern Blood Sect Master kept Ha Gyeol behind.

“You stay for a moment.”

Yang Seok went outside first. He hated moments like this. When his father had heard his report in full, yet still intended to confirm it once more through that bastard.

He would likely receive some report from him regarding Han Seol. At any rate, his father was full of suspicion.

‘Trust your son a little!’

But reality flowed in a completely different direction from what he imagined.

Once his son left, the Northern Blood Sect Master opened a secret door at the back of the main hall and brought Ha Gyeol inside. There was a space there meant only for the two of them.

Within that space was a small hall. It was a bizarre and eerie place, decorated entirely in red on all sides. Also, a thick smell of blood hung there.

As soon as they entered the hall, the relationship between the two was reversed.

Ha Gyeol’s aura was different from when he had been outside. From his eyes and body, a blood-red energy faintly flowed. It was an energy that made one feel afraid just by being in the same space.

“You still haven’t found one?”

At Ha Gyeol’s question, the Northern Blood Sect Master answered respectfully.

“No. I received word just a little while ago. They have finally found someone with the Extreme Cold Physique and are escorting them here.”

It was news he had truly been waiting for, so Ha Gyeol’s expression brightened radiantly.

“How long will it take?”

“They will arrive in three days.”

“Three days!”

It was the first time the Northern Blood Sect Master had seen Ha Gyeol look so delighted. That was how important finding the Extreme Cold Physique was to him.

“Now that we have found the Extreme Cold Physique, please keep your promise.”

In that instant, red energy burst from Ha Gyeol’s eyes.

“Ugh! Stop!”

The Northern Blood Sect Master let out a scream filled with fear. Red circles had formed on both sides of his forehead and were swelling as if they would burst.

“Please spare me!”

The Northern Blood Sect Master pleaded desperately. If they swelled just a little more, the blood vessels in his head would burst, and he would die.

The precarious moment, as though they might burst at any second, continued, and at last, the red energy faded from Ha Gyeol’s eyes. Then the swollen skin of the Northern Blood Sect Master also subsided.

“The promise will be kept when you bring the Extreme Cold Physique before me.”

“Understood.”

The Northern Blood Sect Master was inwardly anxious. When Ha Gyeol obtained what he wanted, would he truly keep his promise obediently?

What he had suffered at Ha Gyeol’s hands was because of greed. Greed had created an opening, and that opening had allowed something vile into his blood.

Yes, ruin always came from greed. But it was also because of greed that he had come this far.

The Northern Blood Sect Master considered even this part of the process. Just as he had until now, he would certainly survive.

Ha Gyeol spoke calmly.

“Until then, we must ensure those Demonic Cult bastards cannot move.”

“Do not worry. Have the Demonic Cult bastards not been tied down under the Ice Palace’s surveillance because of this matter with Chwima?”

Then astonishing words flowed from Ha Gyeol’s mouth.

“The Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult came and went from this place today. It was an extremely faint sensation, but if someone could avoid my eyes to that extent, it must have been him.”

The Northern Blood Sect Master quickly said,

“I will contact Elder Seo at once. Perhaps the fact that he left without permission this time may become an opportunity. After all, the time we need is only three days.”

After bowing respectfully, the Northern Blood Sect Master quickly left the place.

In his eyes as he turned away was a fierce will that would not meekly submit to fate. Whether he lived or died would be decided in three days.

No, this would express his heart more accurately: who would die, and who would live?

Meanwhile, left alone, Ha Gyeol manipulated the rear wall. He pressed certain spots, pulled others, and pressed again. When he carefully operated the mechanism.

Srrrrng.

Then the door opened, revealing another space.

A hidden chamber within a hidden chamber.

There was an enormous tank there. It was a special tank made of ice that could only be created in the North Sea, transparent enough to see inside.

The tank was filled with blood. Mixed into that blood were all sorts of medicinal substances.

A bizarre smell that an ordinary person would never have encountered even once burst forth.

That smell was one only Chwima would likely know. He would know that mixed into this scent was even the wine fragrance of Hyangseol Ice Wine.

A man was submerged inside that tank.

Since it was blood, normally nothing should have been visible, and yet, strangely, his body could be seen clearly. It was a truly mysterious sight.

He had long limbs and fantastical muscles, a perfect body as though carved by a master artisan. What especially drew the eye was the man’s hair. His hair was red as blood.

His body, meeting the blood, created a mysterious atmosphere that could not be seen anywhere else in the world.

Ha Gyeol said to the man in the tank,

“Master, we have finally obtained the Extreme Cold Physique.”

Then the red-haired man within the blood abruptly opened his eyes.

The man who opened his eyes revealed an immense presence. It was a presence that overwhelmed Ha Gyeol.

Though he looked young, he was by no means young. The man within this tank was none other than the Blood King.

Ha Gyeol was the sole direct disciple who had inherited the Blood King’s martial arts.

The Blood King laughed. As he rejoiced, the blood also began to boil up with bubbles of blood as if rejoicing along with him.

But there was also unwelcome news.

“The order to return has come down again.”

Then the Blood King spoke. Though he spoke from within the blood, his words could be heard clearly, low yet deeply resonant.

“We will not return. Even if we must dye the entire North Sea in blood.”

* * *

Seo Nak barged into Geom Mugeuk’s quarters with the Ice Palace warriors in tow.

Ian blocked the doorway.

“What is the matter?”

“Where is the Young Cult Leader?”

“He is sleeping right now.”

A sneer crossed Seo Nak’s face.

“I received a report that the Young Cult Leader left his quarters.”

Ian’s heart sank. If it was discovered that he had left his quarters without permission, it would become a serious problem.

“It seems the news was conveyed incorrectly. He is sleeping right now.”

When he tried to close the door, Seo Nak extended a hand and stopped it.

Ian had no intention of stepping aside, but Seo Nak had come determined from the start.

“Step aside. If you obstruct me from confirming an order given by the Palace Lord, that too will be defying the Palace Lord’s command.”

Seo Nak pushed his way inside with the experts. Since Ian could not fight Seo Nak, he had no choice but to step back.

Seo Nak looked at the blanket bulging on the bed.

“See? The Young Cult Leader is sleeping. So everyone, please leave.”

Ian lowered his voice and acted diligently, but it did not work on Seo Nak.

“I don’t see the wooden pillow.”

He meant that a pillow had been placed inside to create the bulge. He looked like a man who had already come certain that Geom Mugeuk was not here.

“You lift the blanket yourself.”

Seo Nak ordered Ian.

Ian looked back at Chwima. Chwima was merely gazing out the window with a carefree expression.

‘This is not the time for that!’

Then again, in this situation, there was no way he would have any clever solution either.

“Hurry! Do you dare insolently defy the Palace Lord’s command?”

Ian had no choice but to walk to the bed.

Whirik.

Resigned to despair, Ian pulled back the blanket.

“!”

In the next moment, everyone there froze.

Geom Mugeuk was lying there, hugging the wooden pillow.

“Are you trying to take even my blanket from me in this freezing North Sea? Even after seeing me sleep while hugging a wooden pillow?”

Seo Nak was so shocked that his face flushed red, and he even stammered.

“Wh-what in the world? Why are you—?”

Everyone was startled, but the person most startled in that moment was Ian. It was fortunate he did not scream.

‘Young Master, why are you coming out from there?’

Chapter 415: I Permit the Drinking Party
----------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk rose from the bed.

“We’ve been seeing each other often lately.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Seo Nak stood there blankly before speaking in a low voice.

“There must have been some kind of mistake.”

Unlike when he had stormed in so forcefully before, he was calm now.

“It seems someone is trying to put Elder Seo in a difficult position with false information.”

‘That someone is you!’

Seo Nak felt his temper flare.

‘This isn’t like me.’

Ever since he had been drawn into the Young Cult Leader’s pace, he had been unable to control his emotions lately.

It had been the same when he burst into this place. He should have checked beneath the blankets more calmly, and more courteously. Even if he had seen him in the bed, he should not have been so startled.

“I apologize for my discourtesy.”

Seo Nak steadied himself by observing proper etiquette.

“This is not something that can end with a simple apology, so I will lodge a formal protest with the Palace Lord’s Hall over today’s incident.”
