“Do as you wish.”

Seo Nak left the room with the warriors he had brought.

Once they were gone, Ian shouted at Geom Mugeuk.

“When on earth did you get here?”

“Just now. When you opened the door for Elder Seo.”

“Through the window?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded. Ian looked at Chwima.

‘Good grief, like brother, like brother. You knew he had come, but you pretended not to!’

Ian let out a sigh of relief and swept a hand over her chest.

“Ahh! You scared me! I thought my heart was going to burst earlier.”

Chwima responded to her words.

“He was probably clinging to the window precisely to make your heart burst. Waiting to come in at just the right moment and scare you.”

At Chwima’s joke, Ian nodded in agreement.

“Exactly. He’s the kind of person who’d hang there in a snowstorm if it meant he could scare someone. Fine, since you scared us like this, I assume you had results from going out?”

She had meant to corner Geom Mugeuk with that attack.

“I found the one behind it.”

Ian let out a shriek of disbelief. She was even more shocked than when the blanket had been pulled back earlier.

“Really?”

“I got lucky.”

“When it comes to scaring people, you truly are the best under heaven!”

Geom Mugeuk had secretly infiltrated the Northern Blood Sect and seen Han Seol meet Yang Seok.

While everyone was focused on that meeting, he had intended to investigate the inside of the Northern Blood Sect. But then he saw the guard warrior who had accompanied Yang Seok leave his post.

Since it was not an ordinary matter, he followed him, and witnessed the man using blood to cast a blood art on a maidservant. The man had acted as though he were taking care of her, then in an instant cast the blood art, so even though Geom Mugeuk had wanted to help, he could not.

“Who was he?”

“The chief guard of the Northern Blood Sect’s heir.”

Chwima and Ian nodded with hardened expressions. As expected, the bastards had hidden themselves somewhere entirely unexpected.

“Then did you reveal your identity to him on purpose?”

Earlier, Seo Nak had been certain that Geom Mugeuk was not here.

Sure enough, Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“I wanted to gauge the level of his martial arts, and also confirm whether the Northern Blood Sect and Elder Seo were connected.”

“And you confirmed both?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“One more thing. We’ll also be released from being detained here. Seo Nak committed a grave discourtesy against the Divine Cult’s Young Cult Leader, so the Ice Palace Lord now has a pretext to release us.”

With a dazed expression, Ian asked Geom Mugeuk.

“Do you know what I think at times like this?”

“What do you think?”

“Ah! How fortunate I am to be on your side, young master. How fortunate I am not to be your enemy.”

Do you know what I think?

How fortunate I am that you are beside me with such a bright face.

“I was so startled that my heart has become sturdier thanks to you.”

Now Geom Mugeuk looked at Chwima. He was silently gazing out the window.

What was he thinking about? Wine? Or the dead Field Master? Or Yeobin, whom he had left behind?

But all of that was wrong. Right now, Chwima was thinking about Geom Mugeuk. How could he be so clever? How could he handle matters without even becoming flustered?

If Geom Mugeuk were not here, these would all be matters he himself had to handle. Would he have been able to produce such results? For the first time in a long while, he found himself thinking again that although Geom Mugeuk was truly his younger brother, he felt like an elder brother.

“Brother.”

I’m not your brother. If anything, call me your younger brother.

Thinking such idle thoughts, he turned to look at Geom Mugeuk.

From the mouth of this younger brother who felt like an elder brother came the words he had been waiting so desperately to hear.

“It’s finally time to hold a drinking feast.”

* * *

After returning to the Ice Palace, Han Seol headed for the Palace Lord’s Hall.

“I came to drink tea.”

Though she had said she would drink it after returning, the Ice Palace Lord was flustered, not having expected her to come find her as soon as she came back.

“Come here.”

Han Seol sat at the tea table prepared in the Palace Lord’s Hall, and the Ice Palace Lord brewed the tea herself.

“I’ll do it.”

“It’s fine. Just sit there.”

The Ice Palace Lord began steeping the tea herself.

While she did, Han Seol silently watched her mother’s back.

She wanted to say something, but nothing came out. Should she ask what kind of tea it was? Or should she bring up the Northern Blood Sect first? Should she at least talk about the weather?

It had been far too long since they had exchanged anything but official words, so she had no idea what she was supposed to say.

She thought that speaking was similar to martial arts. No matter how extraordinary a technique might be in one’s head, it was useless if it had not been ingrained in the body; likewise, no matter how earnest one’s heart might be, if the words did not cling to the mouth, they would not come out.

It was a moment when she realized anew that words were spoken not with the head or the heart, but with the mouth.

In the meantime, the Ice Palace Lord brought over the tea.

“How does the tea taste?”

“It’s good.”

The conversation did not continue, and only an awkward silence flowed between them.

The Ice Palace Lord was no less awkward.

What should she say to her daughter? This, too, was a worry the Ice Palace Lord had never once had in her life.

And so the two of them simply drank tea quietly. They said that one could tell how close a relationship truly was by how unawkward silence felt, but the two of them still had a long way to go. It was a suffocating silence that made one wonder what they would have done if not for the tea before them.

Even so, the Ice Palace Lord did not want to break this silence by saying just anything.

In some sense, she rather liked this awkwardness. It felt as though a new relationship was beginning.

The one who broke the long silence was Han Seol.

“Palace Lord, who do you think is more likely to survive longer—a ruthless person or a kind person?”

It was an abrupt question, but for now, she answered it first.

“A ruthless person.”

At that, Han Seol smiled faintly.

“That’s the same answer as mine.”

“Wouldn’t any warrior think the same?”

“Ian, who came with the Young Cult Leader, said this. She thinks a kind person would survive longer.”

Ian. For some reason, she had been a child who had drawn her gaze when they first met.

“I was just curious. What you thought, Palace Lord.”

Was it because of that question? For the first time, the Ice Palace Lord spoke the words that had been in her heart.

“I wanted to raise you strong. As a child who would not waver even in the raging north wind.”

The tea in the cup Han Seol was holding trembled once, ripples spreading across its surface.

So? What about now? In Mother’s eyes, did I grow into such a child?

Only after speaking with her daughter did the Ice Palace Lord finally understand.

The reason she had tried to create such a moment, the reason she had said those words a little while ago, was not because of a sudden change of heart or because of Geom Mugeuk.

Her instincts had known. That if things went on like this, she would lose her daughter. That she had already almost lost her. She had only been turning away under the excuse that she was busy with work. If things continued this way, she would merely be the Palace Lord who handed over the Ice Palace, not a mother.

Perhaps she had been living while thinking it might already be too late. Turning away with all her might because she did not want to confirm it.

And then Geom Mugeuk had appeared. His words that she might still be standing behind her had flown into her heart and lodged there, and in the end, she had come all the way to this moment.

And she truly wanted to say these words. They were a little embarrassing, but she wanted to say them to her daughter.

“You grew well into such a child.”

Silence flowed.

It was only a single sentence. Yet Han Seol felt as though part of the wall of ice frozen solid in her heart had shattered and come pouring down. The wall was still enormous, but the fact that it had broken for the first time surely had meaning.

After finishing the tea she held, Han Seol set the cup down.

“Next time, I’ll brew it for you.”

* * *

If one wished to hold a drinking feast in another person’s house, the owner’s permission was necessary.

“You seem to be in a good mood.”

At my words, the Ice Palace Lord smiled faintly.

“My daughter came by.”

The look on the Ice Palace Lord’s face told me what that meeting had been like.

“Congratulations.”

“It is thanks to you.”

“I merely spoke. You were the one who turned back, Palace Lord.”

The Ice Palace Lord felt deep gratitude toward Geom Mugeuk. Now she understood. The unbelievable movements of the heart written in the reports about Geom Mugeuk.

“But what shall we do? I may have to spoil this good mood of yours, Palace Lord.”

“What is it?”

Geom Mugeuk spoke seriously.

“I intend to deal with Elder Seo. Please grant me permission.”

At the shocking statement, the Ice Palace Lord said nothing. Perhaps she had expected matters to turn out this way.

“I saw a master using blood arts in the Northern Blood Sect.”

At the words blood arts, the Ice Palace Lord’s gaze flashed coldly. As far as she knew, there was no master in the North Sea who used blood energy. In other words, it meant some kind of scheme was underway in the Northern Blood Sect.

“He was connected to Elder Seo.”

“Evidence?”

“None.”

“Then you mean to kill an elder of the Ice Palace based only on circumstances?”

“I believe this is enough. We must judge it together with the several mistakes and lies Elder Seo made earlier.”

In particular, the decisive point was that he had concealed even from the Ice Palace Lord, to the very end, the matter of asking the dead Field Master to store the Hyangseolbingju. In the process, the Ice Palace Lord had also seen another side of Seo Nak.

“We won’t be able to find clearer evidence from them. They are all far too seasoned for that. Even if we take the time to find it, by then it will already be too late.”

A brief silence flowed. I gave her enough time to think.

“The one you should be paying attention to now, Palace Lord, is not Elder Seo. Because you have known him for so long, he must weigh most heavily on your mind, but he is nothing more than an arm attached to the body. What you must pay attention to are the head and the body.”

The decisive words that moved her were these.

“I am not the one making this decision.”

“What do you mean?”

“It was your bad premonition, Palace Lord, that called us here. I am telling you that your instinct was correct.”

At last, the Ice Palace Lord made her decision. It did not take very long.

“I will deal with Elder Seo myself.”

“Of course. You should. However, regarding Elder Seo, I would like you to give Chwima the first opportunity.”

Judging by the current circumstances, there was a high possibility that the one who killed the Field Master of Baekju Snowfield was Elder Seo.

There was another round of contemplation, and this time as well, the decision came quickly.

“I will permit the drinking party. However, invite me as well.”

* * *

Seo Nak was arranging ornaments in the main hall.

Whenever his mind was complicated, he would take out all the ornaments and decorate the place anew.

He lined them up precisely once again. When he aligned them without even a hair’s breadth of error, there was a pleasure that came from that perfection.

While he was deeply absorbed in it, he heard the door to the main hall open behind him.

The sound of the door opening was different from usual.

Ordinarily, before the door opened, a subordinate should have reported that someone had come.

But the fact that the door opened by itself like that meant an uninvited guest had arrived.

The subordinates guarding outside must all have been subdued.

Without turning around, Seo Nak said,

“Wait a moment. I’m almost done.”

Only after Seo Nak had lined up even the very last piece perfectly did he turn around.

The person looking at him from the main hall was none other than Chwima.

When his eyes met Chwima’s, he instinctively understood.

‘He has come to kill me.’

Seo Nak sat at the table for receiving guests.

“Thank you for waiting. Now, let us sit.”

Chwima took the seat across from him.

Seo Nak placed one of the cups on the table in front of himself, and the other in the seat across from him.

“Perhaps because I’ve been working, I’m thirsty. Would you pour me a drink?”

Chwima lifted the Hyeollu hanging at his waist and opened the stopper.

The fragrant scent of ice wine spread out. It was the ice wine the Field Master had made before his death.

Chwima inhaled the aroma deeply, then poured the wine into the cup before him.

But he did not pour any for Seonak.

The Drunken Demon drank alone. It had truly been a long time since he had drunk. As the liquor’s warmth spread through his body, he gently closed his eyes and wore an expression of pleasure. Drinking the ice liquor made longing for the original brew well up within him.

Even with the man closing both eyes right before him, Seonak could not attack. The liquor aura rising around his body like a haze was rippling as if alive.

Slowly opening his eyes, the Drunken Demon asked,

“Why did you kill that person?”

The Drunken Demon poured the second cup only into his own cup as well.

Seonak toyed with the empty cup before him and said,

“You could at least give me a drink before asking. How heartless.”

Even in this situation, Seonak showed no fear at all.

“You’re confident you can kill me, aren’t you?”

The Drunken Demon neither confirmed nor denied it, merely looking at him in silence.

“That’s all because you’ve grown complacent. I was the same. Here in the North Sea, there was no one who could make me tense.”

Seonak, who had always lived with an easy, affable laugh, began to reveal his true self. He unveiled his innate presence. The aura that stretched out from him was cold and intense enough to freeze the entire world. He was more of an expert than his known abilities suggested.

“Complacency is a frightening thing. Since you’re a Demon Venerable, you must have lived getting whatever you wanted, haven’t you? You must have defeated enemies you normally couldn’t have beaten. Winning and winning again. But if you look closely, your opponents weren’t devoured by fear of you, but by fear of the Demonic Cult.”

The Drunken Demon was not shaken in the slightest by Seonak’s momentum. He drank another cup of liquor.

Before they knew it, the Drunken Demon’s liquor aura had settled around them like fog.

“I’m the one drinking, yet you’re the one spouting nonsense.”

At the Drunken Demon’s composure, Seonak bared his teeth and laughed. He, who had always worn a good-natured smile, now laughed like a beast baring its fangs.

“That woman was weak. It should have ended once she secretly made it for me, but she kept letting it weigh on her mind. One day, she came to me and said she thought she had to tell the Palace Lord the truth. Does that satisfy your curiosity now?”

Then the Drunken Demon gave an unexpected answer.

“I wasn’t curious at all. The reason people like us kill good people is obvious, isn’t it? It must have been some petty, selfish reason.”

“Then why did you ask?”

Just as Seonak looked as though he could not understand, a voice came from behind him.

“I said I was curious.”

The person who walked through the dense fog and sat down was the Ice Palace Lord.

Gazing at Seonak, she asked calmly,

“Was that also for the sake of the Ice Palace?”

Chapter 416: To a Drunkard, Anything at Hand Is a Weapon.
---------------------------------------------------------
Seonak was shocked by the Ice Palace Lord’s appearance.

More shocked than when he had sensed that the Drunken Demon had come to kill him.

“Palace Lord.”

At first, he tried to make excuses for what he had said moments ago. Even if he had revealed his true colors, they had still spent years together.

But from the Ice Palace Lord’s cold gaze upon him, he knew that any excuse was already too late.

“Was this your doing, Palace Lord?”

“You still haven’t answered my question. Was killing Wonju also for the sake of the Ice Palace?”

If things had come to this anyway—

“As always. Every choice I make is for the Ice Palace. I am the Ice Palace.”

The Ice Palace Lord’s eyes sank even colder. To say he was the Ice Palace in front of the Ice Palace Lord herself went beyond insolence; it was a challenge.

The air around them naturally grew colder. Amid it, the Drunken Demon silently tipped back his cup.

Seonak had already overturned the drinking table, but the Ice Palace Lord still treated him with courtesy. After all, there were the many things he had done in the past.

“Was joining hands with those who use blood arts also for the sake of this palace?”

After a brief pause, Seonak spoke in a low voice.

“You knew even that much?”

She had known, and yet pretended not to? Seonak’s expression instead hardened, revealing his displeasure.

The Ice Palace Lord found this face of his unfamiliar. The thought that he had lived so long hiding this true face was horrifying.

When she imagined Seonak forcing the delicate Wonju to write a suicide note in front of him, goosebumps rose all over her body.

“I won’t ask why you joined hands with them.”

She repeated exactly what the Drunken Demon had said to Seonak earlier.

“Because it must have been for some petty, selfish reason.”

At that, Seonak shouted furiously.

“Shut your mouth! How dare you! Even if everyone in the world curses me, you have no right to.”

He revealed raw anger and resentment.

“Whose grace do you think put you in that position?”

This was what Seonak had wanted to say all along.

“When you ascended to the seat of Palace Lord, if I had harbored other intentions!”

The Ice Palace Lord understood. The root of this betrayal had begun there. He regretted it. He regretted not usurping the position of Palace Lord back then.

“So? Did they promise you the seat of Palace Lord?”

Seonak gave no answer, but the Ice Palace Lord understood. It was the silence of affirmation. If he had not received such a promise, he would never have joined hands with them.

When had his heart changed?

Because he was someone she had watched for so long, she thought many questions would come one after another, but when she actually faced it, that was not the case.

Rather, she had no desire to talk with him. It was obvious they would only scrape at each other’s emotions with all manner of sordid words. The bridge back had already been cut; what use was arguing over what lay beyond it?

The Ice Palace Lord looked at the Drunken Demon. As Geom Mugeuk had requested, she had decided to leave Seonak’s disposal to the Drunken Demon. She thought that would be better for herself as well.

Setting down his cup, the Drunken Demon said,

“It won’t take long.”

“I’ll wait outside.”

The Ice Palace Lord walked out into the mist from which she had come.

“A selfish bitch who doesn’t know how to consider another’s heart! A bitch like a block of ice!”

At Seonak’s curse, the Drunken Demon laughed contemptuously.

“Why are you laughing?”

“Because you’re pathetic. Now I see why they chose you.”

Killing intent loaded itself into Seonak’s cold laugh. He still was not afraid. It seemed he thought this was a fight he could take against the Drunken Demon.

Even so, the Ice Palace Lord was outside. What on earth was he relying on?

With that question in mind, the Drunken Demon closed the stopper of Blood Tear.

Seonak stared intently at Blood Tear.

“Is that your signature weapon?”

The next moment, the Drunken Demon flipped over the table in front of him.

Bang!

The instant Seonak’s palm strike shattered the table—

Crack.

A chair flew into Seonak’s shoulder and smashed down. The Drunken Demon had struck him with the chair he had been sitting on.

Seonak was flustered. It was too unexpected an attack. Since there was no way a wooden chair could harm him, only the innocent chair had shattered and scattered—but could the Drunken Demon have not known that?

The Drunken Demon’s next words answered his question.

“What else would a drunkard’s weapon be? Anything that comes to hand is a weapon.”

The Drunken Demon laughed like a scoundrel, but Seonak did not laugh.

What scoundrel in the world could strike him with a chair grabbed at hand? If anything, one could say it displayed greater skill than stabbing him with a treasured sword.

If one were to choose the Demon Venerable most feared by the martial world, the Poison King, the Vile Little Demon, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable, and others would fiercely compete. No one would choose the Drunken Demon first.

But if that question were asked of Seonak now? It had become a matter he would have to think about.

Swaaaak!

This time, Seonak launched a surprise palm strike, releasing palm force.

The Drunken Demon lightly twisted aside and evaded it.

Boom!

The wall of the great hall behind him broke apart, shattering the display cabinet placed there. Seonak’s gaze flicked, for the briefest instant, toward the ornaments that had broken and rolled across the floor.

He would no longer care.

For it was a scene announcing that a new life had begun.

Swaaaang!

When the second palm force came flying, the Drunken Demon vanished into the mist. Since no scream was heard, he must have avoided this one as well.

Suddenly, Seonak felt that his body was different from usual.

‘This isn’t ordinary mist. It’s wine qi.’

That was why his body grew tipsy and languid. His vigilance naturally loosened. This was not a matter of mental strength, nor of how much internal energy he possessed. His body was naturally reacting to the intoxication.

As Seonak drew up his internal energy, a cold chill rose from both his hands.

‘Get a hold of yourself! If you get drunk, you die!’

Seonak’s signature martial art was the Cold White Divine Palm.

Even if one was lightly struck, cold energy would enter the organs and torment them for life; if struck properly, the whole body would freeze solid and die on the spot. It was a terrifying martial art.

“See here, Demon Venerable. Don’t hide cowardly in the mist. Come out and face me fair and—”

Swaaaak!

Seonak twisted his body aside to avoid what flew at him from the front.

Bang!

What flew on and smashed through the rear wall was an iron bead that had been rolling on the floor. It had fallen from one of the ornaments broken earlier.

As he had said, the Drunken Demon was fighting while using everything that came to hand as a weapon. But the trash he picked up from the floor flew in more fearsomely than the martial world’s golden-grade hidden weapons.

“For a Demon Venerable under heaven to be so cowardly—”

Swaaaaaak.

This time, an attack flew from behind him. It was too late to turn. Seonak drew up his protective aura and blocked it with his back.

Thud!

The moment he was struck, he knew. It had been the Drunken Demon’s fist.

Seonak was flung forward as if hurled, then whipped around. His back hurt as if it might break, but it was bearable.

The Drunken Demon stood where Seonak had been standing, drinking.

He was looking at Seonak as he drank, and the instant Seonak saw those eyes, a chilling wind blew through his chest. Those languid, decadent eyes held a kind of killing intent and madness he had never seen before.

At that very moment—

Whoooooosh!

Seonak’s sudden palm force tore through the mist and surged forward.

But the Drunken Demon lightly avoided it with nimble steps, then concealed himself in the mist. This place, overflowing with wine qi, was the Drunken Demon’s world.

The stronger the expert, the more they would feel how terrifying this mist was. They were people whose victory and defeat were decided by the smallest of differences.

Just then, something gleaming cut through the smoke and flew in.

Shik! Shiiik!

Puk! Puk!

The swiftly flying objects pierced Seonak’s shoulder and arm. In the Drunken Demon’s hand, revealed from within the mist, was a dagger. It was too fast for him to avoid in time.

Seonak swung a palm strike a beat late, but the Drunken Demon had already disappeared into the mist.

‘He’s strong. Stronger than I expected.’

Seonak also moved quickly, trying to hide himself in the mist. But the mist was not on his side. The mist around where he launched himself scattered, revealing him.

Swaak!

Kwaaang!

He blocked the palm force flying toward him, but he was pushed back in internal power, and his arm hurt as if it might be torn off.

‘Even palm techniques!’

And that was not all.

Swaaaaaak!

The Drunken Demon’s fingers shot out from the mist and raked past him. Skin was torn away and blood sprayed.

‘This time it’s claw arts!’

Now Seonak knew for certain. The Drunken Demon had mastered all kinds of martial arts.

Because a drunkard’s martial arts had to be this way.

Striking and exchanging blows, stabbing, rolling, throwing.

This was the Drunken Demon’s martial art.

The martial art of a drunkard fighting wildly was a skill that broke his bones and tore off his flesh.

The Drunken Demon revealed himself again. Up ahead, he was drinking as if to show off. The more he drank, the stronger he became.

Seonak drew up all of his internal energy. The energy rising from both his hands had been blue, but now it turned pure white.

He was about to unleash White Annihilation Palm, the most powerful technique among the forms of the Cold White Divine Palm.

‘He’s not dodging? He’ll take White Annihilation Palm right there? Good!’

Just as he cried out in delight inwardly—

Seonak saw it.

The surrounding mist rushed toward the Drunken Demon in an instant, then formed a single shape. It was the back of a Wine God, standing with his back turned.

Among the many martial arts he had learned, this was the Drunken Demon’s shining signature martial art.

Swaaaaaaaaaak.

White Annihilation Palm was unleashed. Tearing up the floor and ripping apart the air, a massive aura-force flew toward him.

At the same time, the Wine God standing behind the Drunken Demon turned around and launched itself forward.

Among the Drunken Demon’s signature martial art, the Wine God Art—

The Fourth Cup, Wine God’s Rampage.

The wine qi flying in the shape of the Wine God converged and became a single streak of light.

Kwaaaaang!

As the two enormous aura-forces collided, a tremendous explosion burst out.

The impact completely blew away the great hall.

When the rising dust settled, the two men were revealed.

The Drunken Demon stood where he had been from the beginning.

Seonak, on the other hand, had been pushed back more than ten steps.

Wine God’s Rampage had overwhelmed White Annihilation Palm and driven it away completely.

Seonak’s entire body was in tatters.

“Kheeeek.”

He vomited a gush of blood. It was a fatal wound beyond recovery.

The Drunken Demon had defeated him without using Blood Tear, his signature weapon. Seonak had been strong, but the Drunken Demon was stronger.

The Drunken Demon drank. The final mouthful was a drink for Wonju.

Before the death that would soon come, Seonak was furious. He was not angry that he had lost, but furious at those who had broken their promise.

“They said they would protect me…”

Blood flowed down from Seonak’s mouth. What he had trusted in was not only his own ability.

Then a new voice was heard there.

“They didn’t break their promise.”

Something flew in front of Seonak. What was flung down before him were two corpses.

The one who entered was none other than Geom Mugeuk.

“You must have been important to them after all.”

The two martial artists had been lying in ambush in a secret place within Seonak’s residence.

They were no ordinary experts. Their martial skill was one thing, but their stealth and lightness arts were also outstanding; they had been prepared to take Seonak and flee if the need arose.

As Geom Mugeuk walked over to the Drunken Demon, he asked,

“Are you all right?”

“I’m fine. You?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded to say he was fine, then looked to the other side.

“Something’s coming!”

Beyond the mist, something was running toward them.

Piiing!

The Drunken Demon instinctively twisted his body. Something grazed past his face.

It was a drop of blood.

What had flown like a hidden weapon was unmistakably a drop of blood. But it was no ordinary drop of blood. It was blood energy in the shape of a droplet.

Soon after, the one who had sent the blood droplet revealed himself as if pouncing upon them.

His eyes, face, neck, and hands were bizarrely red.

“Dodge!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s shout, the two men avoided to either side.

Kwaaaaang!

The man who had charged in exploded, and blood sprayed in every direction. The spurting blood pierced through the collapsed walls. It was a self-destruction art. The power of the blood energy released upon death was tremendous.

This was why Geom Mugeuk was wary of the Blood King, and why he hated him. He was someone who used his subordinates’ lives in this way.

This was not someone who had merely learned a self-destruction art in a short time. He was a martial artist raised through long training in order to use this self-destruction art effectively. And what awaited at the end of that effort was this single instant of self-destruction.

That was why fighting the Blood King became a revolting battle.

This time, he had to ask.

“Are you all right?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, the Drunken Demon nodded and was about to answer when—

Both men’s gazes turned once again to one place.

Someone else was running toward them. And it was not just one or two.

“There are many this time!”

The Drunken Demon cleared away the mist spread out before them to secure their field of vision.

At that moment, the two of them saw it.

The dozen or so people running toward them seemed to leap into the air all at once—

Kwaaang-aang!

Then exploded together, unleashing their self-destruction arts. Whether they had been raised solely for this moment, they showed not a single instant of hesitation.

It was a self-destruction art made so that the blood energy would concentrate only forward.

Piiiiiiiiiiiiik!

Hundreds of streams of blood energy flew toward the two of them.

With an ear-splitting sound as they tore through the air, they came densely enough that there was nowhere to evade.

They flew in over too wide an area to dodge.

The Drunken Demon stepped in front of Geom Mugeuk.

“Young Cult Leader! Behind me!”

The Drunken Demon blocked Geom Mugeuk’s front and drew his protective aura up to its limit. At the same time, he crossed both arms to shield his face.

In that moment of crisis, the Drunken Demon called Geom Mugeuk Young Cult Leader. At this moment, he was not a younger brother, but the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, whom he had to protect at all costs. And he himself was the Demon Venerable who protected him.

The Drunken Demon raised his internal energy to the extreme. Judging by the power of the blood droplet that had flown earlier, injury was unavoidable. Even if he himself was hurt, he would absolutely not allow a single hair on Geom Mugeuk’s head to be harmed.

Because this was the pride of a Demon Venerable who had come out accompanying the Young Cult Leader.

Thudthudthudthudthudthudthudthudthudthudthud!

He heard the sound of the incoming blood energies striking something.

The next moment, a momentary silence passed.

The Drunken Demon slowly lowered the arms he had crossed before his face.

With a startled expression, he looked up ahead. The blood energy that had flown in with terrifying force had not reached him.

Something had been erected before his eyes.

A wall of aura-force, brilliantly shining as it reflected the sunlight.

The blood energy had poured in densely with no place to escape, but not a single strand of that blood energy had pierced through this shining wall of aura-force.

A massive wall of aura-force, the greatest wall to all demonic practitioners.

The Great Demon Wall of the Guhwa Demonic Art had been erected.

Chapter 417: They Say Some Can Even Open Hell's Gate.
-----------------------------------------------------
Endless waves of blood qi flew in and struck, but they could not pierce the Great Demon Wall.

Thudthudthudthudthud!

The blood qi that flew in to the very last, as though wrung dry, was all dispersed.

The dozen or so martial artists who had leapt into the air and used most of their own blood for the Art of Self-Destruction all became corpses with gaping holes in their chests, strewn across the ground.

The Great Demon Wall did not vanish immediately, but remained there in its majestic form, glittering in the sunlight.

The Drunken Demon slowly turned to look beside him.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been behind him, was now standing at his side, the Black Demon Sword thrust forward.

“I can’t let an attack like that injure you, brother.”

It was not an ordinary attack, nor was it some final ultimate technique unleashed at the end of a fierce battle. This was the Art of Self-Destruction, used the moment they saw them. Though the word “art” was attached to its name, it was an attack that could not be called martial arts.

Geom Mugeuk had deployed the Great Demon Wall because he did not want even a single hair on the Drunken Demon’s body harmed by such an attack.

This scene was something like a symbol.

—The only existence in murim capable of eliminating vile and vicious evil in an even viler, even more vicious way. I believe this is where we must find the reason for our existence. Because we can stop evils that ordinary people cannot.

The Drunken Demon looked at the Great Demon Wall and bowed his head respectfully in salute. It was courtesy toward the Nine Flames Demonic Art. Only then did the Great Demon Wall slowly disappear from that place.

Only after that did he look at Geom Mugeuk and speak solemnly.

“Thank you, Young Cult Leader.”

At this moment, when he had been saved by the Nine Flames Demonic Art, theirs was not the relationship of elder brother and younger brother, but of Demon Venerable and Young Cult Leader. Geom Mugeuk also conveyed his gratitude.

“I will never forget that the Demon Venerable stepped forward to protect me.”

He meant it sincerely. When the Drunken Demon had tried to block the Art of Self-Destruction with his own body, Geom Mugeuk had truly been moved.

After sharing their hearts in that way, Geom Mugeuk drew up his qi and examined whether there was anyone else. There was no one. Those who had been hiding near the ruined great hall were all of them.

Just then, the gate leading outside opened, and the Ice Palace Lord entered.

Far behind her, three or four corpses could be seen collapsed on the ground. All of them were frozen solid.

“A few of them caught my eye, so I dealt with them.”

She had tried not to involve herself in this fight if possible. She had stayed outside because she had promised to give the Drunken Demon priority.

Her gaze turned toward the dozen or so corpses lying fallen.

‘They used the Art of Self-Destruction all at once.’

She looked at the wall of qi-force that had towered up beyond the wall. How could she not know what an extraordinary realm it must have taken to create such a thing?

‘The Young Cult Leader has properly learned the Nine Flames Demonic Art.’

If the Young Cult Leader’s martial arts were like this, how astounding must the Heavenly Demon’s martial arts be? A chilling sense of awe filled her.

Geom Mugeuk said to her,

“They are people who won over an elder of the North Sea Ice Palace, joined hands with the North Blood Sect, and do not hesitate to use the Art of Self-Destruction.”

The Ice Palace Lord felt that the situation was more serious than she had thought. She had always paid attention to murim affairs so as not to fall behind, but she had never once heard of people like this before.

The Ice Palace Lord walked over to Seo Nak, who lay fallen on the ground.

Struck by the Drunken Demon’s attack, his entire body was a wreck, and only the barest thread of life remained.

‘Just who did you join hands with?’

The Ice Palace Lord looked down at him with cold eyes, then turned away without a word. She did not even want to offer him a single word of farewell.

As she turned away, Seo Nak spoke.

“……Han Igyeong.”

In that instant, the Ice Palace Lord flinched and stopped moving.

She slowly turned back again and looked down at Seo Nak.

Lying on the ground, Seo Nak smiled. Because his mouth was full of blood, the sight was ghastly.

Han Igyeong.

She was the Ice Palace Lord’s elder sister.

Long ago, she had fought with her sister over the succession, and her tenderhearted sister had left the North Sea for her sake. She had yielded the position of successor.

After that, she had not looked for her sister.

She had told herself that being entangled with her would only be bad for her sister.

But if she were to be honest, it was closer to saying she had deliberately left it buried. She had been afraid that if their relationship continued, her sister might once again grow ambitious for the position of Palace Lord. Back then, she had truly worried about such a thing.

So she had lived on, forgetting her. At first consciously. Later, she forgot naturally.

The reason she thought of her sister again was none other than that Geom Mugeuk.

When she received the report that Geom Mugeuk had concluded the succession struggle without shedding blood, she thought of the sister she had forgotten.

Looking back now, there must have been a way. Could she not have chosen a successor peacefully, just as Geom Mugeuk had done?

Back then, what had she been so afraid of? Thinking about it now, her sister and she herself had been perfectly fine, yet the people around them had gone into hysterics.

In any case, the Ice Palace Lord felt indebted to her sister.

Because all that she now enjoyed could have been enjoyed by her sister.

And now, the dying Seo Nak had mentioned that sister.

‘Why?’

Under her cold gaze, Seo Nak said,

“I chose you, not Igyeong.”

It was not the delirium of a man on the verge of death. Seo Nak’s eyes, lit by the final flare before death, were clear.

“Do you not want to see her?”

She did not want to hear any more, so the Ice Palace Lord’s hand shone white.

Just as she stretched out her hand to end him—

“She may have family.”

Seo Nak smiled with an expression that said he knew something. The blood filling his mouth spilled down.

‘Is this it? Your final revenge?’

Knowing some secret, yet refusing to tell.

He knew that at this moment, matters concerning her sister would deal her the greatest blow. No matter what else he said, she would not even blink.

“What if she has a daughter? She would live her whole life without knowing that her aunt is the Ice Palace Lord, wouldn’t she? Losing her parents early and suffering somewhere as a maidservant, perhaps. Or maybe she became a courtesan and sells her smiles.”

The Ice Palace Lord gazed into his eyes and said calmly,

“I am not curious. Whether she lives as a maidservant or as a courtesan.”

The Ice Palace Lord did not speak rudely to Elder Seo. As if to say none of his provocations would work, she remained courteous.

“I have lived all this time forgetting her. Did you think I would grow curious now? Did you think I would feel some pang of conscience at words like these? You are foolish to the very end.”

The corners of Seo Nak’s eyes twitched, and the smile vanished from his face.

Pure white energy extended from the Ice Palace Lord’s hand and engulfed Seo Nak’s body. Terrible pain spread through him.

“Aaaaaargh!”

His entire body froze as he writhed in extreme agony. He screamed for her to please kill him.

“Even if you know, do not tell me where she is. It will only bother me for no reason. Keep your mouth shut!”

Even then, Seo Nak writhed in pain.

He died struggling in agony more intense than any he had ever felt in his life.

The Ice Palace Lord looked down at the corpse with complicated eyes. Inwardly, she had hoped. She had hoped he would beg her to spare him, to stop the pain and he would tell her everything he knew.

But seeing him die without speaking even through this pain, she thought it must have been something he had brought up at the end simply to torment her. Even if it was not, she would think so. She had no intention of dancing to his tune.

The Ice Palace Lord said to Geom Mugeuk,

“I have unintentionally exposed my palace’s shame before you.”

“Shame? Palace Lord, you too concluded a succession struggle splendidly, without bloodshed between kin.”

“My sister was the splendid one.”

“If she was such a wise elder sister, then wherever she is, she must be living happily.”

She knew he was saying it out of courtesy, but even so, Geom Mugeuk’s words gave her a little comfort.

The Ice Palace Lord changed the subject.

“What do you intend to do now?”

“Next, I intend to strike the North Blood Sect.”

In her heart, the Ice Palace Lord wanted to lead her subordinates there and sweep away the North Blood Sect at once, but if she did, it would cause many sacrifices.

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“If you permit it, Palace Lord, I would like to visit the North Blood Sect with the Young Palace Lord as a reconnaissance.”

Send her daughter to the North Blood Sect? The Ice Palace Lord was silent for a moment. The enemy was so powerful that she hesitated.

“Do so.”

She judged that when war broke out, it would actually be safer for her daughter to be beside Geom Mugeuk. She had seen the wall of qi-force beyond the wall, and she had drunk tea with her daughter. She trusted him because he was the person who had made that possible.

“Please leave the sweeping away to yourself, Palace Lord. This time, I will give you priority.”

The Ice Palace Lord nodded. The reason the Drunken Demon and the Young Cult Leader had come was to uncover the truth behind Wonju’s death. Now that they had revealed that death and even taken revenge, they could simply return.

And yet they were saying they would fight the enemy together with her. The Ice Palace Lord was grateful to Geom Mugeuk, and reassured by him.

“Then I will see you later.”

After bidding farewell to Geom Mugeuk, the Ice Palace Lord also exchanged a courteous farewell with the Drunken Demon.

Once she left, Geom Mugeuk turned to the Drunken Demon.

The Drunken Demon handed the Blood Tear to Geom Mugeuk.

“Drink.”

He never handed the Blood Tear, his absolute signature weapon, into another person’s hands.

The only one to whom he allowed it was Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk drank the ice wine in the Blood Tear refreshingly.

“Ah, that’s good. The most delicious ice wine in the world!”

Taking back the Blood Tear, the Drunken Demon drank the wine.

“It really is delicious!”

The two of them deliberately spoke loudly. Because the person who had filled it with liquor would be listening from somewhere in the sky.

After they drank all the wine left in the Blood Tear, the two stood side by side and looked up at the sky.

The Drunken Demon smiled brightly.

Geom Mugeuk had such a thought. Perhaps, right now, Wonju was looking down at him from that sky and smiling.

* * *

“Do not worry.”

At the words that came from behind her, Ian, who had been standing by the window, turned her head. The one who had spoken to her was Han Seol, sitting on the edge of the bed.

“Mother went with them.”

Since Ian had been staring blankly out the window, it seemed she had looked as if she were worried.

Ian answered honestly.

“To tell you the truth, I was thinking about something else. I don’t worry about what the young master does. I already have a mountain of worries of my own, so why would I worry about whether the sun will rise tomorrow or not?”

Han Seol stared at her for a moment, then said,

“Because he is a transcendent being?”

Ian thought she was teasing her. Of all things, she had used that expression, so now she would suffer for it forever!

“Yes, to me, he is a transcendent being.”

Because she wanted to talk about this matter someday, Han Seol asked again.

“If the Young Cult Leader told you to die, could you die?”

It was a question she thought Ian would not be able to answer easily, but Ian answered without hesitation.

“Yes. I could die. I would throw the gates of hell wide open and say this: My young master sent me here! Ah, if it were the young master, he would become friends with King Yama and be exempted. I would be the one suffering in the pit of fire, wouldn’t I?”

Han Seol shook her head and barely held back the words that had risen to her throat. They had only spent a few days together; she could not say it outright.

Ian said those words in her stead.

“You were thinking I’m a crazy bitch, weren’t you?”

At Ian’s words, Han Seol made an expression that said, If you know, then fine.

Ian smiled brightly and looked back out the window.

“Actually, that question was the easiest one to answer. Because he is absolutely not someone who would tell me to die. It’s a question where I can boast loudly that I could die.”

Han Seol looked at her back. Could such a deep bond truly form between people?

“Wouldn’t the Young Cult Leader feel burdened if you were like that?”

If it were her, she thought it would feel burdensome. If someone looked at her with such expectation, she would think she had to do better, had to show an even better side of herself. The pressure would be no ordinary thing.

“He won’t.”

“Why are you so sure?”

“Because he is not foolish enough to take words like that from me as a burden. He will simply be happy. He will think, So all my efforts were worthwhile.”

Honestly, Han Seol envied Geom Mugeuk. Gaining someone’s absolute trust and support was truly no easy thing.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk and the Drunken Demon returned to the residence. Seeing the two of them, Han Seol asked urgently,

“What about Mother?”

“The Palace Lord went to the Ice Palace.”

The news was conveyed that Elder Seo had truly been a traitor, and that he was dead.

Han Seol found it unfamiliar. What felt unfamiliar was not Elder Seo’s betrayal, but the fact that there was someone behind him.

Since childhood, she had treated him like an uncle, so to her he had been like a great elder. And yet there was someone else who moved such a person. He had seemed so strong.

Han Seol grew worried for her mother. Even so, her mother must have trusted and relied on Elder Seo greatly.

Suddenly, she realized this was the first time she had felt such an emotion.

‘I am worrying right now.’

To her surprise, she realized that until now, she had not once worried about her mother. Her mother had always been strong. And nothing had ever happened that required such worry. But just moments ago, she had asked after her mother first, and now she was worrying that her mother might perhaps have been hurt. For the first time in her life, she felt a kind of heat in her heart.

Could a person’s heart change this easily? No, was it all right for it to change like this?

If it could change like this, did that not mean it could also change to harbor even greater hatred?

Han Seol suddenly felt that she wanted to ask Geom Mugeuk about such matters. Somehow, she thought that if it were Geom Mugeuk, he would give her a clear answer, saying it was this or that. Well, that did not mean she asked.

“What do you intend to do now?”

“By killing Elder Seo, we have declared war on them.”

Now the true fight had begun.

“The next target is the North Blood Sect. Before striking them, the most urgent priority will be to find out why the North Blood Sect sought the Extreme Cold Physique. It would be good if we could enter their inner ranks.”

At that, Han Seol recalled one fact and, even though Geom Mugeuk had not asked, spoke first.

“When I met the second young master of the North Blood Sect before, I told him I would visit again with the Young Cult Leader. Let us go together.”

Geom Mugeuk honestly told her the current situation.

“It will be extremely dangerous. Someone you were speaking with just moments before may suddenly use the Art of Self-Destruction right in front of you.”

Was it because she felt in her heart a burning heat she had never felt in her entire life? Strangely, Han Seol was not afraid. Since this was the Ice Palace’s affair, she only thought that she should take the lead in resolving it.

“Someone said she’d open the gates of hell.”

Han Seol looked at the smiling Ian and added vigorously,

“I will open the North Blood Sect.”

Chapter 418: The Drunken Demon Must Die.
----------------------------------------
The Ice Palace Lord was silently gazing out the window.

Seeing her so agitated, Seo Nak's final act of spite had succeeded.

Into that place entered Han Seol.

"Palace Lord."

"You have come?"

The Ice Palace Lord turned toward her.

"I am planning to go to the Northern Blood Gate with the Young Cult Leader."

At her daughter's words, the Ice Palace Lord nodded.

She knew it was a dangerous matter given the circumstances, but she had already resolved to send her, trusting in Geom Mugeuk. Her daughter would grow into a true martial artist through such experiences as well.

In truth, Han Seol had not come solely to report that she was going to the Northern Blood Gate. She was worried about her mother. That she might have been hurt because of Elder Seo.

"Make sure to express it in words."

These were the words Geom Mugeuk had said to her when she told him she would go report to her mother. His perception was so sharp that he had known the reason she was going to see her mother.

Expressing it in words.

To her, those words felt like a gate of life and death.

In the past, this meeting would have ended with her mother's reply of "Very well, go and return safely," without them ever once properly meeting each other's gaze.

But this time was different.

Han Seol looked straight at her mother.

"Why do you look at me so?"

After a brief pause, Han Seol spoke.

"I was wondering if you were alright."

The Ice Palace Lord sensed it. How much courage her daughter had mustered to say those words. Even within the awkward atmosphere, emotions were being conveyed clearly between them.

"Let us have a cup of tea."

"Today, I will make it."

Han Seol brewed the tea herself. The Ice Palace Lord sat at the tea table and silently watched her from behind. It was the first time her daughter had brewed tea for her. Thanks to that, her depressed heart eased considerably.

"You have grown up."

The moment she heard those words, Han Seol's hand, which had been brewing tea, paused for a moment. Once again, Geom Mugeuk's words came to mind.

"Had she raised us to this age, wouldn't it be a child's duty to make her say something like, 'You've grown so much,' at least once?"

A short while later, Han Seol brought the tea and set it down.

The two drank tea in silence. It was less awkward than when they had first begun.

"I am fine."

She was truly fine. Elder Seo's betrayal did not affect her particularly. She had already suspected him, which was precisely why she had accepted the cult's people into the palace.

Since she did not seem to be deliberately hiding her wounds, Han Seol was relieved.

"That is a relief. Then I shall go and return."

She tried to get up before it became awkward for no reason.

"Wait a moment."

The Ice Palace Lord rose from her seat and brought something she had prepared. It was a set of protective armor.

"This is the Heavenly Palace Guardian Armor. Wear it when you go."

The Heavenly Palace Guardian Armor was protective armor passed down as a treasure of the Northern Sea Ice Palace.

Greater emotion was conveyed to Han Seol, who was surprised by the unexpected protective armor.

"If they use the Art of Self-Annihilation right before your eyes with no room to evade, trust the Heavenly Palace Guardian Armor and protect only your face with the Winter Wind Ice Barrier technique. Then you will be able to avoid critical injuries."

The heart worrying for her daughter was conveyed to Han Seol. That was why Han Seol's voice trembled slightly as she took her leave.

"I will handle it as you have said. Then I shall go and return."

Han Seol descended from the Palace Lord's quarters carrying the Heavenly Palace Guardian Armor. She should have properly conveyed her gratitude. But she had come down without being able to offer a proper word of thanks, being too flustered, surprised, and moved.

The change in their relationship was joyful, yet at the same time, it was worrying.

Even Elder Seo, whom she had watched for so long, had betrayed her mother. A human's heart when sitting differs from when they stand.

She was afraid she might hurt her mother. And that she might be hurt too. It was because she had lived for so long without considering the other person's heart.

* * *

Ha Gyeol entered the secret room containing the water tank.

Today too, he felt the calculated cold that could only be sensed in this space.

The Blood King was still submerged in the bloody water of the tank. Once the one with the Extreme Constitution arrived, he would finish the grand technique and emerge from that place. Now there were only two days left, and he had to deliver such news.

"Elder Seo is dead."

At Ha Gyeol's report, the Blood King in the tank opened his eyes wide.

"He fell to Chwima."

Then, a low and deep echo sounded from within the bloody water.

"What of Chwima?"

He likely thought Chwima would not be safe either, since blood men who used the Art of Self-Annihilation were guarding him.

"Chwima is safe. The Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult and the Ice Palace Lord were together at that place. It is presumed that the three of them blocked the Art of Self-Annihilation through a combined attack."

The expression of the Blood King submerged in the bloody water stiffened frighteningly.

Raising a single blood man capable of using the Art of Self-Annihilation required tremendous money and time.

Moreover, Seo Nak was a person who had long invested effort to completely take over the Ice Palace. Thus, the Blood King's fury was only natural.

He felt the bloody water of the tank growing even colder.

The submerged Blood King began to slowly rise to the water's surface.

Swiiiiish.

He revealed his body from within the bloody water.

Blood water flowed down his outstretched arms and body. The blood aura flowing from his two eyes was so intense that one could not dare meet his gaze.

"I will not leave Chwima alive."

The moment he tried to go outside immediately.

Ha Gyeol prostrated himself before the water tank as if performing a deep bow.

"Please endure, Master!"

The Blood King's movement paused for a moment. His cold gaze turned toward Ha Gyeol.

"You have the great enterprise right before you. You need only endure for two more days now."

The bloody water flowing down the Blood King's body dripped.

Ha Gyeol knew. Even if only one hour remained rather than two days, his master was not one to endure. His master was a man like blood. That blood boiled like lava and froze like a glacier.

"Please wait just two days. They have not yet discovered us. Seo Nak died because he was discovered in the process of those bastards tracking down the murderer who killed the Lord of the Daylight Snowfield. Please postpone the punishment until two days later."

Ha Gyeol desperately dissuaded his master.

The Blood King silently looked down at Ha Gyeol.

Swiiiiish.

He submerged back into the bloody water.

He closed his eyes and said nothing. Now, in two days, the fury he had endured today would accumulate and erupt, so Chwima would certainly die.

Ha Gyeol revealed his determination, putting strength into his eyes.

"Even if it means sacrificing everyone in the Northern Blood Gate, I will endure for two days."

* * *

Ha Gyeol, who had come out of the secret room, returned straight to Yang Seok's quarters.

As he was about to enter the building, Yang Seok was waiting at the entrance.

"Where have you been again?"

"I apologize. I went to see the Gate Lord."

Whenever he left his post, he always used the Gate Lord as an excuse.

Yang Seok found him displeasing. It was not merely because he frequently left his post to meet his father.

His father treated Ha Gyeol somehow cautiously, and the two were somehow secretive. Therefore, seeing them made him feel unpleasant.

"It is fine. I understand you."

Now Yang Seok had made up his mind to change his strategy.

"Here, I give this considering your troubles until now, so take it."

Yang Seok handed over an envelope. Ha Gyeol received it and opened it to see three promissory notes worth one hundred nyang each. It was a large sum for money given as reward for hard work.

"I cannot accept it."

"It is my sincerity, so take it."

Yang Seok insisted, and in the end, Ha Gyeol accepted the notes, pretending he could not overcome the gesture.

Yang Seok intended to make Ha Gyeol his person and use him in reverse to figure out his father's heart.

'I should have done this from the start.'

Where is there a fellow who hates money? Yang Seok regretted it. He had been angry at his father, who was watching him, and could not act rationally. If his father gave one thousand nyang, he would give two thousand.

"I ask for your goodwill in the future."

"I will serve you with full loyalty."

At that moment, a subordinate came and reported that a guest had arrived.

"The Little Palace Lord of the Ice Palace has arrived."

"She has already come?"

She had said she would come again, but he had not known it would be this soon.

"Is there anyone accompanying her?"

"Yes, she came with a young man."

Yang Seok intuited that the young man was the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult. She had said she would bring the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult when she came again. They said the Young Cult Leader acted arrogantly; it seemed she truly wanted to resolve the conflict with him even a day sooner.

"Let us go."

Yang Seok took the lead and walked. He wanted to hurry and see what kind of person the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult was.

Ha Gyeol's expression as he followed him was stiff.

'They came from the Cult already?'

The Cult's movements were faster than he thought. It had not been long since Elder Seo was killed.

'Moreover, are they trying to target Young Master Yang?'

They had properly grasped the situation. Because he would have done the same.

Ha Gyeol too was curious about the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult, who moved this quickly, so his steps following Yang Seok quickened.

* * *

Han Seol was waiting in the guest hall with Geom Mugeuk.

Unlike when she had come before, today a clear plan and strategy had been established.

Han Seol was someone who did not easily get nervous over most matters, but today she was anxious.

She looked at Geom Mugeuk. He showed no sign of nervousness at all.

"Aren't you nervous?"

Geom Mugeuk nodded and asked in return.

"Do you know why you are so nervous?"

"Why?"

"Because you are trying too hard to do well."

He was right. She clearly had the desire to make the plan succeed with perfect acting.

"So what if you are a bit lacking? Even if I make a mistake, the Young Cult Leader over there will handle it. Try adopting such a mindset. Then you will not be nervous at all."

Even without deliberately adopting such a mindset, hearing Geom Mugeuk's words alone made Han Seol feel a bit more at ease.

"This martial artist said: You will never give the order for me to die. If you do not give that order, you will die. Will you still not give it? Even if the Demonic Cult perishes?"

Geom Mugeuk smiled and gazed at her.

"That is correct. Still, I will not give it. What is the point of living a life where I must kill a subordinate who likes me so much in order to survive? The Main Cult is the same. If its rise or fall depends on a single woman's life or death, then it is better to perish."

Han Seol looked at Geom Mugeuk, then turned her head to the window and averted her gaze.

"It is not bluster!"

I know. That is why I feel even more upset.

Then the door opened, and two people entered.

Ha Gyeol stood guarding near the entrance, and Yang Seok walked toward the two, greeting them brightly.

"Little Palace Lord, welcome."

"There is a precious person I wish to introduce, so I have come to see you again like this."

She introduced Geom Mugeuk.

"He is the Young Cult Leader of the Divine Cult."

Yang Seok politely greeted him with courtesy.

"I am Yang Seok of the Northern Blood Gate. It is an honor to meet you."

"I am glad to meet you. I am Geom Mugeuk."

Yang Seok cautiously sized up Geom Mugeuk.

He was the type to mercilessly beat someone up over a quarrel from nearly bumping shoulders on the street, but toward those stronger than him, he displayed a polite and cautious demeanor.

'What the, this guy?'

Geom Mugeuk was far younger and more handsome than he had thought. He had expected him to look rugged and fierce.

"I heard that the Young Cult Leader is personally investigating the incident where the Lord of the Daylight Snowfield passed away."

"The investigation into that matter is finished."

"Finished?"

Yang Seok had not yet heard the news regarding it.

"The culprit was Elder Seo."

"Surely you do not mean Elder Seo Nak?"

"Why would I not?"

Yang Seok was startled. But Geom Mugeuk could tell from that reaction that he had not known this fact. It was a point that revealed the Lord of the Northern Blood Gate did not share all information with his son.

"What happened to Elder Seo?"

"He is dead."

Yang Seok could not believe it.

"If so, the Northern Sea should have been turned upside down."

But why is it so quiet?

"Because we are not people who work while making rumors."

Unable to believe Geom Mugeuk's words, Yang Seok looked at Han Seol. When he asked with his eyes if this was true, she nodded once.

Starting with the announcement of Seo Nak's death, Geom Mugeuk gradually began to set the mood.

"Your younger brother found the Extreme Constitution; do you know why?"

Yang Seok was flustered because he had not expected Geom Mugeuk to ask so openly.

"I do not know about that matter."

"You seem like a very lucky person. You did not know what your younger brother, with whom you were competing for succession, was doing, yet you became the successor."

Knowing it was sarcasm, Yang Seok hardened his expression, but Geom Mugeuk continued to provoke his nerves.

"Indeed. You became the successor thanks to us."

Suppressing his anger, Yang Seok spoke in a low voice.

"Whatever the reason, my younger brother died because of a man from your cult. Yet how can you speak to me like this?"

It was courteous, but there was an edge to it. As if to say, You killed a man, and you think this is acceptable?

“Are you certain he died because of us?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned cold.

“When we released him, he didn’t look like a man about to die at all. His desire to live was remarkable. And yet he killed himself merely because he had been kidnapped? Don’t you find that a little strange yourself?”

Geom Mugeuk stared fixedly at him, then added another question.

“Could it be that you killed him?”

Naturally, the atmosphere sank into a chill.

Han Seol stopped Geom Mugeuk.

“That is an accusation without evidence.”

“When looking for a culprit, isn’t it customary to first consider who benefited most from the death?”

“That does not mean it should be handled on mere suspicion.”

As Han Seol took his side in this situation, Yang Seok’s favorable impression of her naturally grew.

All of this was something Geom Mugeuk had planned in advance. He would press the attack, and Han Seol would defend him.

Geom Mugeuk now moved on to the next stage.

He walked toward Ha Gyeol, who was standing at the entrance to the reception hall.

Since Geom Mugeuk had witnessed Ha Gyeol using a blood art on the maidservant, he knew that Ha Gyeol was someone directly connected to the Blood King.

When Geom Mugeuk suddenly approached him, Ha Gyeol secretly raised his internal energy. He had observed Geom Mugeuk from a distance, but he could not get a sense of what kind of person the other man was. Surely he could not be someone whose skill overwhelmed his own. And so all he felt was a strange unease.

Geom Mugeuk came right up before him and asked,

“What is your name?”

“I am Ha Gyeol.”

Geom Mugeuk quietly gazed at Ha Gyeol.

“The smell of blood is thick on you.”

Has he seen through my identity? Ha Gyeol tensed, ready to strike at any moment.

“You lived as a wandering warrior before becoming a bodyguard, didn’t you?”

At that question, Ha Gyeol felt relieved. Saying he smelled of blood did not mean Geom Mugeuk had realized he practiced blood arts; it meant he did not seem like a bodyguard.

“Among all martial artists, I have a real fondness for bodyguards and wandering warriors. You happen to be both.”

“Thank you for looking on me favorably.”

While Geom Mugeuk kept Ha Gyeol occupied, Han Seol was carrying out the most important part of today’s plan.

She let out a light sigh and sent a voice transmission to Yang Seok.

—I’m sorry. I shouldn’t have brought that man here.

Those words sounded as if she were saying, I thought you would be able to handle that young cult leader of the Demonic Cult, but I suppose there is nothing you can do either.

Yang Seok’s pride was wounded. But that did not mean he could curse at the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult or challenge him to a fight.

When his emotions had grown complicated, Han Seol cautiously sent another voice transmission.

—And I’ve debated several times whether I should tell you this…

—What is it that makes you say that?

—I happened to overhear what the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult was saying to the Drunken Demon.

Because she had defended him earlier, Yang Seok now subconsciously felt that Han Seol was on his side.

—Tell me.

But Han Seol continued to hesitate.

—It’s all right, so please tell me quickly.

As if agonizing until the very end, Han Seol finally spoke with caution.

—Listen without being shocked.

But what flowed from her mouth was something Yang Seok could not possibly hear without being shocked.

—According to the information they obtained, the Northern Blood Sect Leader is considering someone other than Young Master Yang as his successor.

His father was considering someone else as his successor? It was truly a bolt from the blue.

—They said the purpose of finding the Extreme Cold Body was also related to the new successor.

As truth and falsehood became mixed together, Yang Seok fell into confusion.

He could not dismiss it as nonsense. He had killed his younger brother right in front of his father. And his father had watched without stopping him. Because that was the kind of family his was.

—Did they say who it was?

At that, Han Seol’s gaze slowly turned toward the entrance of the reception hall. Toward the man who had been caught by Geom Mugeuk and was being made to listen to all sorts of things.

—They said it was Ha Gyeol.

Chapter 419: The Most Terrifying Borrowed Blade.
------------------------------------------------
Yang Seok was deeply shocked.

‘Father is considering that man as his successor?’

The reason his astonishment was so great was because it carried the shock of betrayal. One suspicion naturally came over him.

‘Is that why he summoned him so often? Not to receive reports about me?’

Just then, Han Seol sent him another sound transmission.

—The Demon Cult’s information could be wrong. Look into it properly.

Geom Mugeuk had told her to say this. He had told her not to try to deceive him, but to sympathize and think along with him. She still did not know why she had to do that, but she followed Geom Mugeuk’s instructions.

—The Demon Cult’s information is unlikely to be wrong.

Yang Seok knew very well how exceptional the Demon Cult’s intelligence network was. His father had always emphasized, many times, that the Northern Blood Sect also needed an intelligence organization like the Demon Cult’s Tongcheongak.

Of course, there was this possibility.

‘What if Han Seol is lying?’

Why would she? What could she gain by sowing discord between Ha Gyeol and him?

And one other possibility.

‘The Demon Cult may have intentionally fed false information to the Young Palace Mistress.’

He hoped it was the latter rather than the former. Because he did not want Han Seol to be on the same side as the Demon Cult.

Including the possibility that the information truly was fact, nothing was certain, and so Yang Seok was confused.

‘Whatever trick this is, and whatever the reason, they’ve underestimated me. I will never fall for such petty schemes.’

Yang Seok’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk and Ha Gyeol.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk looked over and spoke loudly.

“This friend here is quite the fellow. Come, let’s go together.”

Geom Mugeuk led Ha Gyeol over to where Han Seol and Yang Seok were.

Naturally, Yang Seok and Ha Gyeol came face to face.

Seeing Ha Gyeol made Yang Seok’s mind grow complicated.

‘It’s really you? Father made you his successor?’

He wanted to grab him by the collar and ask, but he forced himself to calm his excitement.

Ha Gyeol noticed that Yang Seok was looking at him with eyes different from usual.

But that the reason was because he suspected him of being the successor was something he truly could not even imagine. Rather, he thought it was antipathy toward the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader, who was acting as he pleased. Besides, he knew Yang Seok had feelings for the Young Palace Mistress of the Ice Palace. This seat would not be easy.

Geom Mugeuk alone was in high spirits as he raised his voice.

“It’s fate that the four of us have gathered like this, so let’s all have a drink together. I had been abstaining from alcohol for a while, but that ended when Elder Seo died. Come, let us drink merrily, like foals with the reins cut loose.”

If the Young Cult Leader said they should drink, what could Yang Seok say? In any case, they had come as guests, so he had to entertain them. Even without Geom Mugeuk’s suggestion, perhaps because his heart was in turmoil, he felt drawn to drink.

“Very well. I’ll have the wine prepared.”

He called a maidservant and ordered wine to be prepared in the reception hall.

But Ha Gyeol declined politely.

“I am to meet the Sect Master, so I’m afraid I cannot. Then I will take my leave.”

If he sat down to drink with Geom Mugeuk, his identity might be exposed. With the great undertaking only two days away, he had to be wary even of falling snowflakes—let alone when the other party was the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader.

How could he not know that the man chattering before him like a village woman was not all there was to him?

Yang Seok watched coldly as Ha Gyeol withdrew.

When they had met earlier, Ha Gyeol had said he was on his way back from meeting his father. After that, they had been together the entire time, and now he had to go meet his father? Fine, he could lie to the Young Cult Leader. But was Yang Seok not also present here?

‘He’s leaving without even glancing at my reaction?’

Geom Mugeuk began placing kindling in the fire blazing within him and blowing on it.

“Did I not say so? He’s an amusing fellow. A guard warrior who leaves behind the person he ought to protect? Young Master Yang, you must understand. Is this friend not from a wandering warrior background?”

Yang Seok instead spoke in Ha Gyeol’s defense.

“In our sect, the Sect Master’s orders are absolute.”

Though he was smiling outwardly, smoke began to rise in his heart.

‘Is he truly ignoring me because he considers himself the successor?’

Even normally, he had been different from ordinary subordinates. There was a strange air about him that made it difficult to treat him carelessly. His father had treated him differently from the other subordinates as well.

‘Was all of that truly because of this?’

If Yang Seok had not killed his own younger brother with his own hands, he would not have suspected this far. Because he had done something absurd, he now thought absurd things might also happen.

There was one fastest way to confirm this matter.

To meet his father directly and confirm it.

But he did not readily feel inclined to do so.

What if the information the Demon Cult had found out was true? How would his father react? His father had not even blinked when his second son died before his eyes—would he be unable to kill the eldest?

While Yang Seok was sinking into dark thoughts, Geom Mugeuk sent a sound transmission to Han Seol.

—You did very well.

Han Seol had performed her role splendidly. She had lied without so much as batting an eye. Relationships with people were difficult, but deceiving people was not difficult.

—Was that really doing well? When I deceived someone so easily.

—You deceived him well not because you are a wicked woman, but because you are clever.

—It truly feels like you have an answer prepared for anything anyone says.

—That is because I am clever too.

Geom Mugeuk truly left her with nothing to say.

In the meantime, a drinking table was prepared in the reception hall. Fine wine and dishes were set out, and the three sat around it.

As Yang Seok poured wine into Geom Mugeuk’s cup, he thought inwardly.

‘If that information is true, then this man knows that I cannot become the successor, yet he is not showing it at all. As expected, he is no easy opponent.’

Yang Seok emptied two cups of wine in succession.

“Oh, Young Master Yang. You can drink quite well.”

Originally, Yang Seok did not have a particularly strong tolerance. But today, the wine went down like water.

After they had drunk several cups that way, Geom Mugeuk and Han Seol began the conversation they had agreed upon. Geom Mugeuk released his qi to prevent the conversation from leaking outside. Now it was beginning.

“Young Cult Leader, when will you be returning to your Divine Cult?”

“As you know, I killed Elder Seo and avenged the Drunken Demon, so our business is finished. All that remains is to return, but the Ice Palace Master asked me to look into the Extreme Cold Physique, so I stayed.”

Geom Mugeuk revealed why he spoke of such an important matter so lightly.

“To be honest, I have no interest in the Extreme Cold Physique.”

Pouring wine for Yang Seok, Geom Mugeuk asked,

“Young Master Yang, tell me plainly about the Extreme Cold Physique. Then I will relay it to the Ice Palace Master and return to the cult. If you do not wish to tell me, you may tell the Young Palace Mistress.”

“I would need to know something first.”

Yang Seok answered politely. Come to think of it, he truly knew nothing.

He had only searched for those who possessed the Extreme Cold Physique together with his younger brother; he did not actually know what those who possessed it were to be used for.

Suddenly, he recalled what Han Seol had said earlier.

—They said the purpose of obtaining the Extreme Cold Physique was related to the successor as well.

But I am the successor, am I not? If I know nothing?

Then does that mean Ha Gyeol is related to the Extreme Cold Physique?

Geom Mugeuk summarized what was currently happening in the North Sea with a single phrase.

“In any case, the greed of old men is something else.”

It was clear that the old men he referred to here were Elder Seo and the Northern Blood Sect Master.

Though it was an insult to his father, Yang Seok could not refute those words. No, he did not even feel like refuting them. His father was clearly harboring a desire he had not even told his son about.

Until now, he had trusted only his father and followed him without asking a single word about it. An unbearable sense of betrayal kept raising its head.

“Come, let us drink.”

Geom Mugeuk kept urging him to drink. Before long, Yang Seok’s face was flushed with intoxication.

The North Sea affair had begun with the matter of the Drunken Demon, and even at this moment, alcohol was playing a major role.

Geom Mugeuk did not care what means he used. Since the other side had come at them using a self-destructive art, he had no intention of dealing with them by fair means either.

Han Seol sent a sound transmission to Yang Seok.

—Ask the Young Cult Leader directly whether that information is true.

—Then will it not be revealed that you told me that?

—I am fine. Since I brought it up in the first place, I must be prepared for at least this much. Are you going to hand over the position of successor to a subordinate just like this?

Yang Seok asked suspiciously.

—Why are you trying to help me?

Geom Mugeuk had expected that he would certainly ask this. Now was the moment for her to play the second most important role she had today. A short but powerful sentence.

—I want you to become the Northern Blood Sect Master.

At those words, Yang Seok felt a surge of emotion. Since he already had feelings for Han Seol, and since he was drunk as well, the moment he heard those words, he felt a delight he had never experienced before.

Just from Yang Seok’s expression, Geom Mugeuk could tell what sound transmission Han Seol had sent. He pretended not to know and simply drank, and at last Yang Seok drew his blade.

“Young Cult Leader, I ask you as one martial artist to another.”

“Why are you acting so frighteningly?”

“Did Father truly choose Ha Gyeol as his successor?”

The smile vanished from Geom Mugeuk’s face. Instead of making excuses, he looked at Han Seol with cold eyes.

“It was you.”

Han Seol did not deny it.

“Yes. I happened to overhear the Young Cult Leader speaking with the Drunken Demon, and I told this man.”

“You did not happen to overhear it. You eavesdropped.”

In truth, considering their martial arts levels, it was impossible for Han Seol to eavesdrop on the conversation between the two of them.

But at present, Yang Seok did not have precise information about the three people’s martial prowess, and even if he had, he was not in a state to judge rationally. All his nerves were focused on whether or not that matter was true.

“I’m sorry. I only wanted to help Young Master Yang.”

Han Seol lowered her head deeply. In her own way, she was feeling a strange thrill. It was a dangerous mission she had even come wearing the Cheongung Hogap for, and yet, astonishingly, this moment was fun. She found it fascinating that she could carry out such acting so shamelessly.

Yang Seok, who had no way of knowing such thoughts, stepped forward when he saw her cornered.

“Do not seize on someone innocent. This is my problem, so settle it with me.”

He held out his wine cup. His expression said that they should speak man to man, martial artist to martial artist.

“Did Father truly make Ha Gyeol his successor?”

Geom Mugeuk glared at Yang Seok with cold eyes. It was a chill incomparable to when he had been smiling and speaking frivolously before.

Han Seol could feel it. Such a contrasting reaction increased the credibility of the words he was about to say. She realized that overwhelming and deceiving an opponent was not accomplished by words alone. A single expression, a single gaze, changed the impression entirely.

“You have learned a fact you should not have known.”

With Geom Mugeuk responding this way, all of it seemed true to Yang Seok. He was gradually being swept up in the atmosphere Geom Mugeuk created.

“This is my affair. It is a fact I ought to know. I will ask again. Did he truly make Ha Gyeol the successor?”

Now wearing an expression as if he had no choice, Geom Mugeuk answered calmly.

“He did.”

“Do you have proof?”

“We deployed all of our intelligence capabilities for this matter. My word is the proof.”
