Geom Mugeuk, wearing a serious expression, truly did not look like a man who was lying. Yang Seok was confused. Had his father truly abandoned him?

“If your sect master finds the Extreme Cold Physique, you may find yourself in a difficult situation.”

Because he was saying it to his face, he expressed it as difficult situation, but those words meant he could be eliminated. There was an intention behind them to keep him from going to the Northern Blood Sect Master to confirm it.

“That is why our cult’s Tongcheongak is also keeping watch over this matter. It is not common for someone to eliminate their own blood kin in order to make another person their successor.”

This time, he directly used the words eliminate their own blood kin. Geom Mugeuk’s calculations were contained in each and every word.

Suddenly, Yang Seok thought about why his father had so easily allowed his younger brother to be eliminated. Because we are not Extreme Cold Physiques, and therefore have no use?

The flames of suspicion that had caught fire in his heart now began to give off black smoke.

“When someone searches for a special constitution, is it not usually because they intend to perform some Great Art? It may be a Great Art that should never appear in the world, so our cult is on edge in various ways. Thanks to that, I am the only one being bothered.”

His half-believing, half-doubting heart tilted further. Geom Mugeuk had no reason to lie about something like this. What would the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult lack that he would stay in this freezing North Sea?

“Now, I’ve been honest with you, so tell me honestly everything you know about the Extreme Cold Physique.”

Yang Seok hesitated for a moment, but in the end, he opened his mouth.

“I don’t know the details either. My father only said it was an important matter and had to be handled in secret. Ah, he did say this: that the Extreme Cold Physique would make our sect the foremost sect in the North Sea.”

A chill passed through Hanseol’s heart as she listened. Those words meant they intended to pull down the North Sea Ice Palace. Judging by what they had done so far, their methods would never be righteous.

‘If that is your plan, then you won’t be erased by the Demonic Cult, but by our hands.’

Geom Mugeuk casually offered a solution.

“If you find the Extreme Cold Physique first, the matter will be easily resolved.”

Still acting as though he had no great interest in it, Geom Mugeuk added offhandedly,

“If you find out, be sure to contact me. Either me, or the young palace master here.”

“I will.”

The three of them drank again.

At that moment, Yang Seok was making a decision that would change his fate.

‘Seize the Extreme Cold Physique and get rid of Hagyeol.’

If his father truly intended to make Hagyeol the real successor, then of course he had to kill him. And even if this information wasn’t true, it didn’t matter.

It would simply mean that one subordinate who had been an eyesore would die. If he was right, he would be saving himself; if he was wrong, it would only be the death of one subordinate.

The problem was his father. If he learned that Yang Seok had seized the Extreme Cold Physique and killed Hagyeol, he would not let it go. Then how could he avoid responsibility?

Yang Seok raised his head and looked at Geom Mugeuk.

‘What if the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult seizes the Extreme Cold Physique? What if Hagyeol dies at the young cult leader’s hands?’

He meant to use Geom Mugeuk as the blade for killing with a borrowed knife. Among all the blades belonging to others in the martial world, this was the most terrifying and powerful one.

If that much was merely petty scheming, then he was now entertaining an even more dreadful thought, one he should never have had.

‘What if even Father dies at the hands of the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult?’

Now a blaze was roaring in his heart. It was a flame in which suspicion, rage, and desire were all mixed together.

‘All of this will become your doing. Yes, keep smiling like a fool.’

As if he had truly read his mind, Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly and held out his cup.

“Seeing you, it feels as though the North Blood Gate has opened wide.”

Yang Seok did not understand the meaning behind those words, but Hanseol did. She had said it herself: Ian could open even the gates of hell, so she would try opening the North Blood Gate.

And now Geom Mugeuk was telling her that the gate had opened.

“Now, let us toast to the North Blood Gate!”

Between desire and mourning as they collided, a toast of admiration was added.

Chapter 420: I’m the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader, So I Should Be Fearsome.
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
As the drinking party neared its end, Geom Mugeuk was acting as though he and Yang Seok were old friends.

“Young Master Yang, come with me to the Central Plains.”

“I would be delighted. Please broaden my terribly narrow horizons.”

If they drank just a little more, they looked ready to start calling each other sworn brothers.

Throughout the drinking party, Han Seol tried hard to play her role faithfully. She sympathized with Yang Seok, and from time to time took his side. It was meant to seem like she was trying to understand him, but in the end, it became an opportunity to learn about herself.

So this is the kind of person I am.

She could say things she did not mean, lie without trembling, and in the midst of it all, observe what intention Geom Mugeuk had in saying such things. She had thought herself a truly cold person, but she realized there was another side to her as well.

Until now, she had looked into herself alone. Naturally, she had reached her conclusions alone too.

This is who I am.

But the conclusion she had reached then and the one she had learned today were clearly different.

Perhaps coming to know oneself was not a conclusion reached by gazing into a mirror alone. Wasn’t the moment one discovered one’s true self when one saw what form one took among other people?

As Han Seol was thinking such thoughts, her eyes met Geom Mugeuk’s.

“Young Palace Lord, you should return now.”

“What about you, Young Cult Leader?”

“I should drink with Young Master Yang all night long.”

The moment he heard those words, Yang Seok was overwhelmed by Geom Mugeuk’s presence.

He really is no ordinary man.

Right now, at least the Young Palace Lord of the Ice Palace was with him, but what exactly was he trusting in to say he would drink here alone, without a single subordinate? What if poison was mixed in? What if he was ambushed while drunk? Did it not mean he was confident he could handle all of that?

Yes, that’s how it should be. He has to be at least this much for me to cut everything down with this blade.

Yang Seok spoke politely.

“We have drunk a great deal today, so how about we drink again another time?”

Yang Seok, who was trying to restrain him, was also very drunk. He thought he was not drunk, but in Han Seol’s eyes, he was completely intoxicated. He was merely so excited by the fact that he was with the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult that he did not realize how drunk he was.

“Another time? We’re only getting started.”

To Geom Mugeuk, who was expressing his regret, Yang Seok wanted to shout, Are you the Drunken Demon?

“I will arrange another gathering.”

“Ah, what a pity. Very well. Let us meet again soon.”

Geom Mugeuk, who had been about to leave after saying farewell, turned back and said,

“Ah, and if you find the Extreme Cold Body, be sure to contact me.”

“Of course.”

Geom Mugeuk left the Northern Blood Sect together with Han Seol.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been laughing and chatting lightly at the drinking party, had returned to a calm demeanor as if none of that had ever happened.

“Were you really thinking of drinking all night?”

At Han Seol’s question, Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“Of course not. Drinking is only enjoyable when you drink with good company. I nearly died of frustration.”

She had thought as much. She had only asked because his acting had been too good.

“I never had any intention of sending you away alone.”

“Really?”

“In the current situation, when we don’t know how the enemy will act, how could I leave you alone? From now on, you must never move alone. I even told Ian, who is in the Ice Palace, to stay close to the Drunken Demon.”

Until this matter was over, Geom Mugeuk would not let his guard down. Because their opponent was the Blood King, they had to be even more tense.

“To think you would worry about the daughter of the Northern Sea Ice Palace’s Palace Lord while in the Northern Sea.”

“The Palace Lord of that Northern Sea Ice Palace even had his daughter wear protective armor.”

She could not refute that.

In any case, walking together with Geom Mugeuk somehow made her feel reassured.

Today, she had clearly seen from beside him how Geom Mugeuk handled Yang Seok. The important thing was not that he had deceived him. The important thing was that he had made Yang Seok believe he was using Geom Mugeuk.

Han Seol stopped walking. Geom Mugeuk stopped along with her.

Their gazes intertwined beneath the moonlight.

“The more I see of you, the more frightening you are.”

She was not joking.

She had never imagined she would say such a thing to someone. Even if she had felt such fear, she was the type to hide that emotion instead.

She did not know why, but with Geom Mugeuk, she became honest.

Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“Isn’t it only natural that I should be frightening? I am the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.”

Seeing a faint smile form on Han Seol’s lips as well, Geom Mugeuk continued.

“I have to be frightening. Think about the enemies we are facing. To find the constitution they wanted, they gathered people, experimented on them, and then killed them. They use the Self-Destruction Art without hesitation, and they even kill their own blood kin. If I were not frightening, how could I face such people?”

Even while laughing, chatting, and drinking, this man had been thinking such things. When she thought that, Han Seol once again felt that Geom Mugeuk was more frightening than the enemy. Because it seemed preferable to face those enemies rather than face Geom Mugeuk.

“If he finds the Extreme Cold Body, will he contact you?”

Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“He will absolutely not contact me. He will think the Extreme Cold Body is the one and only move that can protect him. He will also be hoping that it might make him stronger.”

“Then why did you keep mentioning the Extreme Cold Body?”

“Because it is an important card for us as well.”

Geom Mugeuk had anticipated how Yang Seok would move.

“His desire to survive will guide us to the Extreme Cold Body.”

* * *

Early the next morning, Yang Seok headed to the Sect Master’s Hall.

He was suffering from a hangover after drinking too much, but the thought of meeting his father had his nerves on edge.

I must steal away the Extreme Cold Body no matter what.

He did not know why his father wanted the Extreme Cold Body so desperately, but he had to secure it unconditionally for now. He could not hand it over to Geom Mugeuk either. The Extreme Cold Body would become the hidden card that would protect him.

Yang Seok entered the Sect Master’s Hall.

His father was seated on the grand chair ahead.

His heart pounded with a tension he had never felt before. He had never entered this place with such feelings.

“Did you sleep well last night?”

Yang Seok walked up beneath the grand chair and greeted him politely.

Seeing his father made all sorts of thoughts rise within him, and his mouth itched to speak.

Have you truly chosen Ha Gyeol as your successor?

He wanted to ask. He wanted to ask why, even to the point of abandoning his own son, he had made that man his successor.

Normally, he should have asked. He should have been scolded, told, How dare you see me in such a way and misunderstand me like that?

But his father and he were not in a relationship where they could have such a conversation.

Perhaps when an incident occurred and a relationship broke, it was not because of the incident itself. At least in this case, he thought it depended on what kind of relationship it had been until the moment that incident occurred.

“Yesterday, the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult and the Young Palace Lord came to see me, and I drank with them until late.”

Naturally, the Northern Blood Sect Master already knew that.

To be honest, Geom Mugeuk’s visit was not very welcome. In just one more day, the Extreme Cold Body would arrive at the Northern Blood Sect. After tomorrow passed, not only his own fate but the fate of the Northern Sea would change. Once tomorrow passed.

“How was he?”

“As the rumors say, he was an extraordinary figure.”

“Why did he come?”

Yang Seok did not tell the truth.

“He spoke about Elder Seo. He seemed to suspect that there might be something connected to us.”

Inwardly, the Northern Blood Sect Master was actually pleased by Seo Nak’s death. The two people the Blood Men had joined hands with were himself and Seo Nak. Now that Seo Nak was dead, his own importance had risen even further.

Like the martial world beyond the borders, the Northern Sea’s martial world was extremely conservative.

For someone new to establish themselves without the help of existing powers was several times more difficult than in the Central Plains. Moreover, neither the Demonic Cult nor the Martial Alliance would simply sit back and watch a new power grow. Therefore, Seo Nak’s death was an opportunity for the Northern Blood Sect Master.

Yang Seok cautiously asked the Northern Blood Sect Master,

“By the way, have you found the Extreme Cold Body?”

“Not yet.”

At that moment, Yang Seok was deeply disappointed on two counts.

First, his father had lied. He already knew that his father had discovered the Extreme Cold Body and was escorting it here.

Second, that lie had come out without the slightest hesitation. That disappointed him even more than the fact that his father had lied to him.

Do not resent me later.

Yang Seok concluded that his father had abandoned him. It was the moment when the tower of distrust Geom Mugeuk had built late into the previous night was completed.

Because of that, Yang Seok actually felt relieved. There was no need to think in a complicated way anymore. All that remained now was to fight and survive.

“Do not get involved with the Young Cult Leader if you can avoid it. Nothing good will come of entangling yourself with him.”

“I will keep that in mind.”

Yang Seok bowed politely and turned to leave.

Today, his father should have told him the truth. The Extreme Cold Body is on its way here now, so we must make full preparations. I am only telling you now, but the Extreme Cold Body will be used for such and such a purpose. I am sorry for telling you so late.

You should not have done this to me, Father.

In that way, Yang Seok steadily piled up justifications for the betrayal to come.

* * *

The next day, a single carriage was racing toward the Northern Blood Sect.

Around the carriage driven by a coachman with cold eyes, two martial artists followed on horseback. They had run without rest, changing horses from time to time along the way. Now, their destination, the Northern Blood Sect, was less than a hundred li away.

The speeding carriage slowed. A tree had fallen and was blocking the road.

The carriage slowly came to a stop, and the three martial artists, including the coachman, surveyed their surroundings tensely.

The martial artist on the driver’s seat, who was in charge of this escort, warned the two martial artists.

“Prepare for an ambush.”

Swish! Swish!

At that moment, even though they had prepared for an ambush, an attack they could not avoid came flying in. A man who had been hiding behind a rock on the other side sprang up and launched a surprise attack.

The two martial artists could not dodge the hidden weapons that flew in at high speed. They twisted and collapsed.

The one who had launched the surprise attack was none other than Yang Seok. In his hand was Seven-Step Certain Kill, a forbidden hidden weapon whose use was banned in the murim. It was known as a hidden weapon that, if launched within seven steps, would certainly kill the opponent.

Before he knew it, the martial artist from the driver’s seat was rushing toward Yang Seok and slashing with his sword.

Because Yang Seok was late in launching his hidden weapon, he met him with his sword.

A battle broke out between the two. The martial artist from the driver’s seat was an expert, but not enough to handle the successor of the Northern Blood Sect.

Before even twenty exchanges had passed, the martial artist instinctively realized that he could not defeat Yang Seok. He did not hesitate in making his final decision.

The martial artist’s face flushed red. When he tried to use the Self-Destruction Art right in front of him in the middle of the fight, Yang Seok was greatly flustered.

Should I dodge? Should I block it? The very moment he hesitated—

Crack!

The martial artist’s face was smashed apart, and he collapsed on the spot. Before he could unleash the Self-Destruction Art, someone had sent out a blast of ganggi and killed him.

Startled, Yang Seok turned around. Ha Gyeol was standing there.

“You!”

Yang Seok was shocked. He had never expected him to appear here.

“Now I have finally done my job as a guard.”

But Ha Gyeol had not killed the subordinate who was about to use the Self-Destruction Art for Yang Seok’s sake. It was because Yang Seok could not be killed yet. If they were to continue using the Northern Blood Sect Master, they had to keep the son, who could become his weakness, alive.

“How did you get here?”

“Why, I am Young Master Yang’s guard, am I not? Wherever Young Master Yang goes, I must go.”

“Enough nonsense!”

Now the smile vanished from Ha Gyeol’s face.

“Young Master Yang, how did you know the carriage would come this way?”

“That is what I should be asking.”

Yang Seok could tell.

The dead men were his subordinates.

If not, there was no way Ha Gyeol would have appeared here. He used people who employed the Self-Destruction Art as his subordinates? Just who are you?

Ha Gyeol, on the other hand, had a different question. How had Yang Seok found this place?

When the news that the Extreme Cold Body had been found came in, the escort mission had been assigned in haste, but even so, the three who had died were people Ha Gyeol trusted.

He had used them to escort the Extreme Cold Body in true secrecy, yet Yang Seok had discovered that they would pass through this place at this time.

Ha Gyeol wondered if there might be a traitor within their ranks.

But Yang Seok had learned the carriage’s route by an unexpected method.

“As you know, my younger brother and I were in charge of searching for the Extreme Cold Body for a long time. What we focused on then was creating the shortest transport routes from various places in the Central Plains to the Northern Sea.”

They had to gather people, bring them to the Northern Sea, and experiment on them. Experiment and discard, then experiment again. The experiments themselves were over in a few days, but bringing the subjects took far too long.

“This time, you used your own people, but you used the transport routes we created. You ran along the routes we made and changed horses at the stables prepared in each region.”

Only then did Ha Gyeol understand. He had figured out which route they were taking through the records of horse changes. They had used them because they had to arrive as early as possible, but Ha Gyeol had not expected him to notice that.

“You are smarter than I thought.”

“If I were smart, I would have gotten rid of you long ago.”

Ha Gyeol laughed as if it were absurd. That is something even your father cannot do.

“Because of this matter, Young Master Yang will receive a severe reprimand from the Sect Master.”

Yang Seok aimed his sword at him and said,

“You will be the one who caused this matter.”

He thought this was actually for the best. He intended to make it look as though Ha Gyeol and these escorting martial artists had fought and both sides had been grievously wounded. Of course, he thought that only because he did not know Ha Gyeol’s hidden martial prowess.

“Before that, let us see just what kind of person possesses the Extreme Cold Body.”

As Yang Seok tried to walk toward the carriage, Ha Gyeol warned him in a low voice.

“If you open that door, you will die by my hand.”

A cold energy flowed from Ha Gyeol’s body.

Yang Seok also raised his aura and turned toward him. As Ha Gyeol began to reveal the true face he had been hiding, that presence was extraordinary.

He had not even revealed a quarter of his aura, yet Yang Seok was already being suppressed by it. Then again, if he had been an ordinary man, his father would not have chosen him.

Shocked by his ability, Yang Seok once again felt that his father had abandoned him.

This man must die by the Young Cult Leader’s hand.

Should he throw down his sword even now? Judging from the fact that Ha Gyeol had saved him earlier, it did not seem like he intended to kill him.

Just as the two were confronting each other, it happened.

Fog began to thicken around them. They thought it was something that sometimes occurred early in the morning.

Ha Gyeol caught a scent within the fog.

“The smell of alcohol?”

Yang Seok also smelled the alcohol imbued in the fog. Realizing that it was not fog but alcohol qi, Ha Gyeol shouted,

“It’s the Drunken Demon!”

Ha Gyeol’s gaze turned toward the carriage. Before he knew it, the carriage was enveloped in the fog.

“Protect the carriage!”

At Ha Gyeol’s shout, Yang Seok drew his sword and launched himself toward the carriage.

It was then.

Whooooosh.

The fog that had wrapped around the carriage scattered and vanished along with the wind that blew through.

With his sword aimed forward, Yang Seok cautiously opened the carriage door. When he checked inside, his heart sank. A person was sitting there, leaning back against the seat, and it was someone he truly had never expected.

Geom Mugeuk stared straight at Yang Seok and said,

“I told you to contact me if you found it, didn’t I?”

Chapter 421: I Can Handle the Cold Too.
---------------------------------------
A cry of “Oops” involuntarily burst from Yang Seok’s mouth.

He was so startled he nearly cursed. Who could have imagined that Geom Mugeuk would be inside the carriage?

For a brief moment, silence hung in the air as if time had stopped.

“Why didn’t you contact me?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Yang Seok made a belated excuse.

“Well… I only just found out myself. I didn’t have the leisure to contact you. I was planning to reach out immediately after I discovered it.”

So flustered was he that Yang Seok’s voice trembled and he even stuttered.

“But how did the Young Cult Leader know to come here?”

“How did I know?”

Looking at Geom Mugeuk asking back as if posing a riddle, Yang Seok understood.

*How else? He followed me. Damn! He must have been watching me because he couldn’t trust me.*

He himself was the one who had guided Geom Mugeuk to the Extreme Cold Physique.

*I should have been more careful.*

He had grown too excited after discovering the movement route.

Yang Seok wasn’t the only one surprised by Geom Mugeuk’s appearance.

Ha Gyeol, standing behind him, was even more surprised. Not because Geom Mugeuk was inside the carriage.

*They smuggled away the one with the Extreme Cold Physique.*

Something that should never have happened had occurred. No doubt about it—Chwi Ma had smuggled them away when the intoxicating energy spread earlier. He had to chase after Chwi Ma right now.

*They didn’t come this way. Behind that carriage. The opposite side.*

He strode toward the carriage. Pretending to approach Yang Seok, he planned to leap over the carriage in one breath and pursue Chwi Ma.

They would be escaping while carrying a person, so with luck, he might still catch them.

Whether it was the Young Cult Leader or Yang Seok, only the Extreme Cold Physique existed in Ha Gyeol’s mind at that moment.

Geom Mugeuk saw through his inner thoughts.

“You’re not going anywhere anyway. Don’t even try.”

The moment Geom Mugeuk’s warning fell, Ha Gyeol instead launched his body.

*Swish!*

Ha Gyeol’s qinggong, lightly stamping the ground and leaping, was excellent enough to disregard Geom Mugeuk’s warning.

Just as he was about to soar over the carriage in an instant—

*Boom!*

An explosive sound burst from the air, and Ha Gyeol plummeted as if crashing back down to his original spot.

Geom Mugeuk had blocked his path in an instant as if shifting through space and unleashed a palm strike.

Ha Gyeol’s left arm, which had blocked the attack, throbbed.

*He’s strong.*

This time, Yang Seok was more surprised than the struck Ha Gyeol. If Geom Mugeuk had broken through the carriage roof to block him, he wouldn’t have been this astonished.

But Geom Mugeuk had vanished in the blink of an eye and had somehow intercepted Ha Gyeol in the air above the carriage. When had he gotten out of the carriage and flown up?

Even if his martial arts were the lowest among the three here, wasn’t he still the heir to Bukhyeolmun? He had lost sight of Geom Mugeuk’s movement with his eyes wide open. It meant that if Geom Mugeuk had meant to kill him, he would have died without ever knowing why.

Geom Mugeuk, having sent Ha Gyeol back to his original spot, sat astride the carriage roof.

“Would it be fitting for a bodyguard to jump over the head of the person he serves?”

He wasn’t speaking about himself, but referring to Yang Seok standing beside the carriage.

Yang Seok shouted as if tattling.

“That man tried to kill me.”

He realized the time had finally come to execute the borrowed-knife murder scheme. Though the Extreme Cold Physique he had tried to smuggle away was gone, the opportunity to kill Ha Gyeol had arrived.

“Young Cult Leader! I humbly beseech you to teach that bastard the solemn lesson that in this martial world, there exists a strict hierarchy between superior and inferior!”

Ha Gyeol sneered coldly. He had saved him from dying to the Self-Destruction Technique, yet here the man was acting so shamelessly.

“I told you from the first time I saw you that you reeked of blood.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s leisure, Ha Gyeol fell into deep thought.

*It’s already too late.*

If an ordinary martial artist had taken the Extreme Cold Physique, that would be one thing, but if Chwi Ma had taken them, they would already be too far away to track.

He truly hadn’t imagined such a variable would occur. It was because he had let his guard down, assuming Geom Mugeuk and Chwi Ma were focused solely on Wonju’s death. Regardless, there was only one thing to do in this situation.

*I have to settle things with the Young Cult Leader and retrieve the Extreme Cold Physique.*

There were two methods. First, step back and slowly plan for another day. It was a safe and wise method, but the chances of finding the Extreme Cold Physique would grow increasingly slim with time.

The problem was his master.

*Will my master truly wait?*

His master was reaching the limits of his patience inside the water tank. If he reported that the Extreme Cold Physique had been stolen by Geom Mugeuk, the master would emerge from the water tank immediately.

*In the end, I must settle this right here.*

Could he really defeat this Young Cult Leader?

The move shown moments ago was truly no trivial matter. Furthermore, there was another problem.

He couldn’t recklessly kill this Demonic Cult Young Cult Leader. If he did, it would cause a bigger problem than failing to obtain the Extreme Cold Physique.

But the stronger one became, the harder it was to subdue someone rather than kill them.

While he was caught in conflict amid this vexing situation, Geom Mugeuk looked up at the sky from the carriage roof.

Time was on his side. He held the hilt of the knife too. He didn’t need to press first; he only needed to watch how his opponent moved and respond accordingly.

The biggest reason for ruining things even in an advantageous situation was failing to overcome impatience. Because one held the advantage, one felt victory was certain, that things would go even better. That simple desire to see good results faster.

Precisely because it was simple, that powerful desire had to be endured.

Because once one began to crumble in an advantageous situation, it became uncontrollable. Because one held the advantage, one grew more impatient, more frustrated, more furious. Moreover, the opponent would ride the momentum of turning the tables. In the end, everything would be ruined.

This was why the heavens existed—to look upon during times like this.

Watching Geom Mugeuk look up at the sky, Yang Seok felt a strange sensation.

“Don’t look at me. Look at the sky.”

“Ah, yes!”

Surprised by Geom Mugeuk’s words, Yang Seok looked up at the sky. But in this situation, there was no way the sky could catch his eye.

*Please kill him!*

Eliminate Ha Gyeol first and think later. He thought he was orchestrating a borrowed-knife murder scheme, failing to realize that he was now nothing more than a guide leading Geom Mugeuk to the Blood King.

Then the surrounding air began to change. Ha Gyeol revealed the intent he had kept hidden.

He completely unleashed the intent he had previously shown Yang Seok at only a quarter of its true extent.

His intent, hot yet heavy, began to dominate the surroundings. When he revealed his true form, he became a completely different person.

Truly, the smell of blood emanated from his intent.

At this terrifying intent, Yang Seok felt a fear that made him want to vomit. He had known Ha Gyeol was a master, but to think he was this strong!

*Could it be that the Young Cult Leader might lose?*

In truth, with his insight, he couldn’t tell who was stronger. It was simply that the presence Ha Gyeol revealed was that overwhelming.

What frightened Yang Seok wasn’t only that.

The Blood Men waiting some distance behind Ha Gyeol revealed themselves. Their number was seven. One could tell from their gazes and stances alone that they were masters, and all of them could use the Self-Destruction Technique.

Ha Gyeol asked Geom Mugeuk. The courteous tone he had maintained was also gone.

“Where is the person who was in the carriage?”

He had concluded that he would pressure the Young Cult Leader here and find the Extreme Cold Physique.

From his choice, Geom Mugeuk could tell for certain. The opponent needed the Extreme Cold Physique badly enough to reveal his full true form.

“I’m quite good at enduring the cold too, so how about taking me instead?”

A joke in this situation? Ha Gyeol’s gaze toward Geom Mugeuk grew even colder.

“You think I can’t kill you just because you’re the Demonic Cult Young Cult Leader?”

“No. You people are the type to kill even your own flesh and blood, aren’t you? If you could kill him, you’d probably try to kill my father too.”

At those words, Yang Seok standing beside him inwardly flinched. Because the one Geom Mugeuk spoke of—one who would kill even his own flesh and blood—was precisely himself.

Ha Gyeol asked Geom Mugeuk.

“What do you want?”

The fact that he had remained by the carriage meant he wanted something. Indeed, Geom Mugeuk had a purpose.

“Let me meet the person you serve.”

Ha Gyeol didn’t show it outwardly, but inwardly he was shaken.

*How does he know I have a master?*

For a moment, Yang Seok mistakenly thought that person was his father.

*Perhaps this is the opportunity.*

Now he entertained thoughts he shouldn’t without hesitation. The moment Ha Gyeol appeared here, he thought his father had completely abandoned him.

*Which side should I take?*

Learning that Ha Gyeol was such a tremendous master, he fell into worry.

Joining Ha Gyeol’s side wasn’t the worst choice either. Seeing how Ha Gyeol had saved him by even killing his subordinate at the very beginning, it seemed unlikely he would kill him.

*Should I join Ha Gyeol even now? No. What if he loses to the Young Cult Leader?*

He would be considered a traitor to the Young Cult Leader.

But soon Yang Seok came to this conclusion.

*At least they wouldn’t fight here, right?*

Think about it. With the precious status of a Demonic Cult Young Cult Leader, would he recklessly gamble his life? It wasn’t even directly related to the Demonic Cult.

But the situation did not unfold as he expected.

“If I refuse?”

Ha Gyeol had no intention of letting him meet his master.

Then Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly.

“Then the only reason to keep you alive will disappear.”

Ha Gyeol could feel that Geom Mugeuk’s words were no simple threat.

*He truly intends to kill me.*

Did that mean he had the confidence to do so?

Ha Gyeol raised his energy and surveyed the surroundings. He was checking if Chwi Ma had returned. But there was no one around. Geom Mugeuk meant to kill him alone.

He didn’t know what the outcome would be, but at least in spirit, he was confident he wouldn’t lose to Geom Mugeuk. No matter that he was the son of the Heavenly Demon—he was still too young. Even if he had inherited the Heavenly Demon’s martial arts and possessed internal energy beyond his years, experience was something he couldn’t compensate for.

Moreover, he had probably never experienced the blood techniques that Ha Gyeol had mastered.

*Very well.*

The Demonic Cult Young Cult Leader wouldn’t stake his life on the Extreme Cold Physique.

*If only I can subdue the Young Cult Leader!*

For his own survival, he would surely hand over the Extreme Cold Physique.

If—by any chance—Geom Mugeuk died in the process? Then he would have no choice but to shift the blame to the Lord of Bukhyeolmun. If he could only find the Extreme Cold Physique, it was worth trying.

“Our Young Cult Leader needs to have a taste of blood.”

Seven Blood Men stepped forward.

Red energy flowed from the swords they simultaneously drew. Masters capable of wielding blood qi like sword qi.

Ha Gyeol estimated that with their combined attack, they might not kill Geom Mugeuk, but could at least wound him. If luck was on his side, he might even find an opportunity to subdue Geom Mugeuk in the process.

He truly thought so, but—

In a flash, Geom Mugeuk charged at the Blood Man on the left with Flicker Step.

*Swish! Slash!*

The sound of a gust of wind and the sound of a neck bone being severed rang out simultaneously. It couldn’t be blocked.

Before the attack of Geom Mugeuk, who appeared in a flash, could even be transmitted to instinct or mind, the blade was already passing through the neck. It was truly overwhelming speed.

The Blood Man closest to him reflexively swung his sword, but the blade only cut empty air. Yet he paid a massive price for that miss.

Before the second Blood Man, stabbed through the heart, could even collapse to the ground, Geom Mugeuk was already performing Shadow Step.

Just when he seemed to vanish before everyone’s eyes, Geom Mugeuk revealed himself between the two Blood Men standing in the center.

*Stab! Stab! Stab! Stab!*

Geom Mugeuk consecutively pierced the necks and hearts of the two Blood Men to his left and right. Once to the left heart, once to the right neck, once to the left neck, once to the right heart.

Those four attacks were so fast they couldn’t be seen with the eye.

The two Blood Men collapsed on the spot, and right at the moment when the fifth Blood Man’s face and body turned red to use the Self-Destruction Technique—

*BOOM!*

The one who fell from the blood qi was another Blood Man.

Geom Mugeuk had instantly subdued the Blood Man using the Self-Destruction Technique and spun him around like a doll, pointing his body toward another Blood Man.

The sixth Blood Man fell victim to the Self-Destruction Technique. The blood qi emitted by the Self-Destruction Technique was a power even they themselves could not avoid or block.

The seventh Blood Man also tried to use the Self-Destruction Technique. But this time, Geom Mugeuk was one step faster.

*Stabstabstabstabstab!*

The Black Demon Sword pierced his entire body in succession. The moment all his major meridians were severed in an instant, his reddened face returned to its original color. Geom Mugeuk’s sword had been faster than the speed at which the Self-Destruction Technique could be unleashed.

Thus, in the blink of an eye, all seven had become corpses.

Yang Seok trembled with his eyes wide open. He had experienced actual combat many times before and had watched others fight on numerous occasions.

And now he thought: The fights he had had until now weren’t even fights.

Geom Mugeuk shook off the blood on his sword. *Shwaaa.* Blood sprayed in a straight line on the ground.

“The taste of blood is rather lacking, no?”

Ha Gyeol’s expression had completely stiffened. He had known the Demonic Cult Young Cult Leader would be strong. But this strong?

He had boasted of showing the taste of blood, but it seemed he himself had tasted Geom Mugeuk’s blood.

“As expected, it’s the Nine Transformations Demonic Art.”

He had naturally assumed it was the Heavenly Demon’s martial art. Since he had never seen it before.

“It’s not the Nine Transformations Demonic Art. Could you be overestimating yourselves? Did you think I would need the Nine Transformations Demonic Art to deal with a mere handful of your subordinates?”

“His strength is at this level without even using his unique martial art?”

Ha Gyeol’s shock was natural. They weren’t ordinary subordinates. He didn’t know how much time and effort it had taken to raise those seven. They weren’t men who should have died like this.

“If you want to see the Nine Transformations Demonic Art, have your leader guide me to him.”

If those words were true, he felt he couldn’t defeat Geom Mugeuk. Of course, it could be a bluff. A ploy to instill fear and seize the upper hand.

Regardless, unless he spoke about his master, Geom Mugeuk wouldn’t let him go alive. But even if it meant dying, he had no intention of revealing his master.

Ha Gyeol drew his sword. A stream of red energy flowed along the blade. A strength of a completely different dimension from what had flowed in his subordinates’ swords could be felt.

“Whether it was the Nine Transformations Demonic Art or not, I shall confirm it myself.”

Through the cold gaze directed at him and the hot blood qi swirling around, Geom Mugeuk understood. That Ha Gyeol had resolved to die.

“Then I’ll show you just a small taste.”

From Geom Mugeuk’s mouth flowed a question that Ha Gyeol could not understand.

“Which direction do you like among east, west, south, and north?”

Chapter 422: You Like the West?
-------------------------------
Have you ever been asked such a question right before a life-and-death battle?

Asking which direction you like among east, west, south, and north?

‘This is psychological warfare.’

Naturally, Hagyeol thought Geom Mugeuk’s question was a ploy to confuse him. So what? He would play along with composure.

“I like the west.”

Then, once again, incomprehensible words came from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“Oh dear. Why the west, of all things? There are handsome ones, clever ones, and mysterious ones too.”

What on earth was he talking about?

Unable to understand, Hagyeol merely snorted.

“Young Cult Leader, enough with your pointless tricks. They won’t work on me.”

Though he appeared relaxed on the outside, he was extremely tense. The seven subordinates killed earlier weren’t slain with mere words. The moment he let himself be swayed by that tongue, a sword would pierce his heart.

‘What trick is he trying to use?’

Geom Mugeuk was attempting something he had never tried before.

He would unleash the Annihilation Formula of the Guhwamagong while summoning only the western evil phantom. A flashy appearance, without cutting! He didn’t intend to kill Hagyeol right away; he needed to keep him alive to find out where the Blood King was hiding.

‘Is it possible?’

His realm in the Guhwamagong had risen, and he could now control the evil phantoms quite freely, so he had been about to try. But soon, Geom Mugeuk sheathed his sword.

“I’ve changed my mind.”

An instinct related to martial arts stopped him. It was possible to make only one evil phantom appear, but he felt that not killing the opponent wasn’t what the evil phantom desired.

Yes, summoning the evil phantoms of the Guhwamagong without killing the enemy was discourteous.

Geom Mugeuk realized anew.

‘So now I’m feeling a bond with them.’

When he had called them forth while training in Wind Valley, the evil phantoms hadn’t disappeared immediately and had looked at each other. At that time, he had felt that they possessed emotions.

The one that looked clever among them had even shown reluctance to part before vanishing.

“I was rash. I’m sorry.”

Hagyeol thought those words were spoken to him, but they were actually Geom Mugeuk speaking to the evil phantoms.

No matter what Geom Mugeuk said, Hagyeol dredged up his true nature that had been sleeping deep within his heart—the nature he had buried while serving as Yang Seok’s guard at the Northern Blood Gate.

He was a rough and cruel man. He had planted the Self-Destruction Art in his subordinates, mercilessly detonated those who lacked ability during training, and brainwashed the survivors with fear. He was someone who achieved his goals by any means necessary.

“You should have killed me when you had the chance!”

Hagyeol flew toward Geom Mugeuk. He would make him pay the price for underestimating him.

Their two swords clashed in the air.

Beyond the crossed blades, Geom Mugeuk’s eyes held no emotion whatsoever. He was the Young Cult Leader who spoke incomprehensible words and joked around, but once the battle began, his eyes changed.

The demonic energy erupting from the clashing Black Demon Sword made breathing difficult. Resisting it, an even stronger blood energy surged from Hagyeol’s sword.

Their swords continuously clashed, creating sparks.

Hagyeol was fast and strong. His martial arts combined swordsmanship and blood arts, but even without the blood arts, his swordsmanship alone was excellent.

“Die! You demonic cult bastard!”

Hagyeol’s eyes tore into long slits, emitting killing intent. His emotions intensified reacting to the demonic energy. An enormous amount of blood energy poured down on Geom Mugeuk.

But unfortunately for him, the endlessly surging blood energy was being blocked by the completed Heavenly Demon Guardian Divine Art. In swordsmanship too, Geom Mugeuk held the advantage.

Hagyeol increased his attack speed even more. But no matter how quickly he swung his sword, Geom Mugeuk blocked everything.

Each and every move carried powerful internal energy, so the more their swords clashed, the more Hagyeol’s arms hurt as if they would fall off. The more they collided, the more he realized he was being overpowered in internal energy as well.

Unable to endure any longer, Hagyeol leaped backward. Instead of quickly pursuing, Geom Mugeuk calmly asked.

“I told you to let me meet the person you serve. If you die here, who will protect that person?”

The moment Geom Mugeuk said those words, he saw the corners of Hagyeol’s eyes tremble.

‘He’s not just a simple leader.’

A blood relative or a master.

The blood energy erupting from Hagyeol’s body grew even fiercer. As the fight progressed, his blood boiled more and more.

Shwaaaaak!

Blood energy erupted from Hagyeol’s sword.

Geom Mugeuk likewise didn’t evade and unleashed sword energy to meet it.

Kwaaang!

Sword energy and blood energy collided, raising a cloud of dust.

Hagyeol unleashed blood energy like a madman.

Clashing again and again.

“Die!”

Hagyeol gritted his teeth and poured out blood energy.

It was an attack with enough power to shake the earth and overturn the topsoil, but Geom Mugeuk stood his ground and blocked all attacks.

Hagyeol, who had been pouring out blood energy, stopped his attack and panted roughly. In contrast, Geom Mugeuk calmly approached him.

There was a reason Hagyeol allowed Geom Mugeuk to get so close. Soon, red energy spread out silently behind Geom Mugeuk like wings unfolding.

Huuuuuk.

That red aura wrapped around Geom Mugeuk in an instant, as if snatching prey, and covered him completely.

It was the secret technique of Hagyeol’s unique martial art, the Myriad Evil Blood Technique — the Blood Coagulation Membrane.

‘Got him!’

It was a hidden trump card: if he succeeded in trapping his opponent in the Blood Coagulation Membrane, the opponent would suffocate and die inside. There was no way to tear through the blood membrane no matter what method was used.

Sure enough, trapped like an egg, Geom Mugeuk squirmed inside the blood membrane.

“Die in there, Young Cult Leader!”

He was half-dominated by blood energy. More than his purpose of finding the Extreme Limit Body or the aftermath of killing the Demonic Cult’s Young Leader, he was swept up in the desire to simply kill his opponent.

Hagyeol looked at Yang Seok with eyes thirsting for blood.

“Please spare me! I was wrong!”

It wasn’t a situation to assert pride or not. Hagyeol had the look of someone who would rush over and kill him at any moment.

It was at that very moment.

A blue light seemed to shine from within the blood membrane.

Puhuk.

Blue light tore through the membrane. It was the Black Demon Sword imbued with sword force.

The moment the membrane tore, Hagyeol let out a scream.

“Uwaaaaah!”

The Black Demon Sword containing blue sword force tore the blood membrane straight across.

Scriiiiiitch.

Hagyeol screamed, and the blood membrane writhed as if alive.

Geom Mugeuk poked his head out through the torn blood membrane.

“I hate stuffy places.”

And then he roughly grabbed the torn blood membrane with both hands and ripped it apart. Once torn, the blood membrane was ripped to shreds by Geom Mugeuk’s hands as well.

When Hagyeol’s screams reached their peak.

With a pop, the Blood Coagulation Membrane disappeared. With a pained expression, Hagyeol asked.

“How on earth? It couldn’t even be torn by sword force.”

“From now on, when you pass down that martial art, make sure to pass on this message: it doesn’t work on the Demonic Cult’s Young Leader.”

At those words, the smell of blood from Hagyeol’s body grew stronger, and blood pooled in both his eyes. It wasn’t red energy settling in; it was actual blood.

Now the blood energy was devouring him. He felt that unless he became a perfect Blood Being, he couldn’t defeat Geom Mugeuk.

Blood orbs began rising from his palms. Dozens of blood orbs rose and transformed into the shape of small daggers.

Shwishswishswishswish!

Another one of his secret techniques.

Flying Dagger Blood Rain.

Dozens of hidden weapons poured down on Geom Mugeuk. Geom Mugeuk began to run.

Papapapapapapak!

Geom Mugeuk displayed astonishing movement art between the pouring blood-red daggers.

Yang Seok, who was watching, opened his eyes wide.

The thought, *How does he dodge that?* only followed after Geom Mugeuk had already dodged.

His figure dodging the pouring blood-red daggers was almost beautiful.

When he had dodged all the daggers and landed, a new secret technique was waiting for him. The earlier Flying Dagger Blood Rain had been a method to make him fall for this move.

Swish! Thud! Swiiish! Swiiish!

Red lines appeared all around Geom Mugeuk. The blood lines trapped Geom Mugeuk and surrounded the area like a spider web. It was as if he were imprisoned, enclosed within the red lines.

Blood Dismemberment Net.

“Don’t move. If you touch the blood lines, you die.”

“Can I still move my mouth?”

Yang Seok nearly burst into a hollow laugh. Making jokes even in this situation?

Meanwhile, the blood pooled in Hagyeol’s pupils grew even redder.

Ssssssssh!

The blood lines began tightening around Geom Mugeuk.

“I’ll cut you into dozens of pieces so they can’t even find your corpse!”

The blood lines from all directions approached with the momentum to cut Geom Mugeuk apart.

Even in this hair-trigger situation, Geom Mugeuk was calm. When he injected internal energy into the Black Demon Sword, blue sword force condensed.

“I don’t think your martial art can overcome my internal energy and the Black Demon Sword.”

It was words born from absolute confidence in himself.

Papapapapak!

The Black Demon Sword cut in all directions.

Chichichik! Thuk! Thuuk!

The blood lines in all directions began to be cut down.

In this unbelievable moment, Hagyeol unleashed his final secret technique. The final move of the Myriad Evil Blood Technique that required using half of his innate true qi.

A drop of blood rose from his hand and became a butterfly flying away.

In front of Geom Mugeuk, who was cutting through the blood lines and emerging.

Puk.

The butterfly burst.

Flying Butterfly Blood Poison.

His master had said: The Flying Butterfly Blood Poison possessed power rivaling formless extreme poison. That no one could block it. However, one had to use half of their innate true qi.

As the blood butterfly burst,Geom Mugeuk’s surroundings were completely enveloped in red mist.

Hagyeol was certain the Young Leader could no longer endure. That was why his gaze finally turned to Yang Seok.

“The Demonic Cult’s Young Leader died because of you.”

Yang Seok felt aggrieved. Why was he shifting the blame here when he was the one who killed him? But he didn’t dare talk back. Hagyeol’s two eyes were still filled with blood.

“Now the demonic horde will swarm in and wipe out the Northern Blood Gate.”

Then Yang Seok put on a shocked expression.

“You did this without even expecting that?”

But the reason Yang Seok was surprised wasn’t because of Hagyeol’s words. Geom Mugeuk was walking out from the red smoke behind Hagyeol.

Hagyeol was horrified seeing that figure belatedly.

‘He survived the blood poison? How on earth?’

It was the moment when all the absolute truths he had learned from his master crumbled.

It was the first time in his life he had felt so powerless.

He felt ashamed of himself for having worried even briefly about what would happen after Geom Mugeuk died. The Geom Mugeuk standing before him felt like a massive wall.

Then he suddenly arrived at one thought.

“You know I won’t open my mouth, right?”

“Yes.”

“You could have killed me earlier, right?”

“Yes.”

“Why did you take the risk?”

He felt that it wasn’t just to ask where his master was. Truly, there was a reason Geom Mugeuk had dragged out this fight for so long.

“To get used to blood arts. Because whoever is behind you uses the same martial art.”

“!”

In that moment, Hagyeol’s expression completely stiffened. He joked, spoke incomprehensible words. So he had forgotten. Just how meticulous and fearsome this man was.

“I have lost.”

Hagyeol lowered his head as if resigned. His tone became polite as well.

“If you spare me, I shall tell you who is behind this and where they are now.”

“You should have done so from the start.”

Geom Mugeuk slowly approached him.

As Geom Mugeuk walked until he was five steps away, Hagyeol’s expression changed.

“You fool! Did you think I’d tell you willingly?”

A satisfied smile formed on Hagyeol’s face.

“At this distance, you can never dodge my Self-Destruction Art.”

He was certain he could kill Geom Mugeuk.

Nevertheless, Geom Mugeuk wasn’t very surprised. It was an expression as if he had expected this situation.

“Then did you think I’d believe your words obediently?”

“What? Then why did you approach?”

“Why else? I’m still practicing. I don’t know who it is, but wouldn’t whoever is behind you try tricks like this too?”

“You bastard! Let’s see if you can remain so relaxed while dying!”

Hagyeol’s face turned red in an instant, and he used the Self-Destruction Art. It was faster than the other subordinates’. Its power would also be incomparably stronger.

At that very moment.

Geom Mugeuk disappeared from before his eyes.

The surprised Hagyeol stopped the Self-Destruction Art. Forcing it to stop caused his energy and blood to tangle, making him vomit blood.

Then Geom Mugeuk revealed himself. He had disappeared from sight in an instant by opening the Space-Time Transference Art.

If one considered compatibility of martial arts, they were two martial arts at complete extremes.

“No matter how quickly you use the Self-Destruction Art, you can’t be faster than me.”

Hagyeol’s face turned red again. This time he tried to use the Self-Destruction Art even faster than before.

But by the time he had finished chanting all the oral formulas, Geom Mugeuk had already disappeared.

“Just what are you….”

Unable to continue speaking, Hagyeol vomited blood again. Once again forcing the Self-Destruction Art to stop, he vomited even more blood than before.

But he couldn’t die meaninglessly like this alone. In the end, the martial art that had inflicted the greatest injury on him was the Self-Destruction Art.

“Uaaaaaaaah!”

Hagyeol screamed, gritting his teeth.

Before he knew it, Geom Mugeuk had appeared before his eyes again.

“A martial artist who has lost should just die. How dirty and cowardly is this?”

Geom Mugeuk looked into Hagyeol’s two eyes. His own face was reflected in the bloody pool filling the pupils. Gazing into that abyss, Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly.

“Do you know why I search so hard for those behind you? Because I don’t like you people. Because I don’t like people who don’t know how to accept defeat and try to drag others down like water ghosts.”

Who in this martial world can stop those who use the Self-Destruction Art without hesitation? They avoid it because it’s dirty, cowardly, and terrifying—who would chase after them so desperately to stop them?

Those who make people incomparably fearful to seize their own gain. They too are evil. Absolute evil that must be trampled and judged in the name of the Demonic Cult.

“…You need to keep me alive to find out who is behind me.”

Hagyeol tried to seize an opportunity by dragging out the conversation somehow. But his opponent was Geom Mugeuk.

“You have no intention of telling me, do you? I’ll find out myself.”

Geom Mugeuk no longer spoke with him.

“You said you like the west?”

The instant those words ended.

Hagyeol felt chilling killing intent from the side.

And he saw it. The most terrifying thing he had ever witnessed in his life. The single slash that that terrifying thing swung without a moment’s hesitation.

Shwiiiiiik!

Swhack!

Hagyeol’s severed head flew into the air.

In the blood pooled in his pupils, various figures were reflected. The sky was reflected, and so was the figure of Geom Mugeuk looking down coldly. Yang Seok watching with a surprised face, and the frightening evil phantom disappearing were reflected too.

Then the ground, grass, and sky flashed by quickly, then slowed, and soon darkness came.

Chapter 423: I’ll Need Stronger Liquor Now.
-------------------------------------------
Yang Seok stared blankly at Ha Gyeol’s head as it rolled across the floor.

He could not believe that the man had been such an extraordinary master. And Geom Mugeuk’s skill, treating that extraordinary master as nothing more than a sparring partner and killing him, inspired awe beyond mere astonishment.

In truth, Yang Seok had not properly seen the clash the two had unleashed. There had been flashes, booms, and thunderous crashes—and then the fight was over.

The only thing he had clearly seen was the final move Geom Mugeuk had shown.

‘What in the world was that demon?’

He had known the martial arts of the Demonic Cult were terrifying, but he had never imagined an actual evil spirit would manifest. When it appeared, he had been so shocked and frightened that not even a scream had come out.

Yang Seok cautiously looked at Geom Mugeuk. The man who had said they should drink together all night and the man who had just been fighting seemed like completely different people.

And Geom Mugeuk, now silently looking up at the sky, also felt like another person entirely.

That sight seemed as though he were debating whether or not to kill him, and Yang Seok was seized by fear.

‘Even if I have to kneel and beg, I need to survive first.’

The fact that he had tried to spirit away the Extreme Cold Physique on his own without contacting Geom Mugeuk was certainly something worthy of death.

‘If I’d succeeded, the Extreme Cold Physique would have become my trump card.’

Yang Seok felt terribly regretful. He had lost the one hidden move that could have let him negotiate with both his father and Geom Mugeuk. An extra life had gone up in smoke.

“Young Master Yang.”

“Yes, Young Cult Leader.”

Yang Seok’s voice trembled violently as he answered. Please, let him not say, Sorry, but you have to die too!

“Now the matter of finding the one behind this depends on you, Young Master Yang.”

Yang Seok let out a sigh of relief. But once he thought he had survived, his body trembled even harder.

“Do you know anything about who is behind this?”

“No, I don’t.”

No. Now was not the time to lie.

“In truth, the Sect Master of my sect is involved.”

He used the title Sect Master instead of father.

“I only found out this time as well.”

Geom Mugeuk could tell that Yang Seok was laboring under that misunderstanding. He could also tell something else: Yang Seok wanted to kill his father. That was why those words had come out so readily.

“The one behind that man is not your father.”

“He isn’t?”

Geom Mugeuk firmly shook his head and said,

“Instead, your father will know about the person Ha Gyeol serves.”

Indeed. Thinking about it, the terrifying blood art Ha Gyeol had displayed earlier seemed, if anything, stronger than his father’s, not weaker.

Only now did Yang Seok understand the relationship between his father and Ha Gyeol. Why his father had been so wary of Ha Gyeol. It was not simply because Ha Gyeol’s martial arts were profound. There had been an even greater master behind him.

‘So that’s why he tried to make Ha Gyeol the successor. Because he couldn’t withstand their threats.’

Yang Seok distorted the situation in that way.

The thought should have led to: Then Father had no choice either. But instead, he resented his father.

‘To yield to outside pressure and cut down his own children!’

He had already resolved to use the blade called Geom Mugeuk to deal with everyone and become Sect Master himself. He interpreted every circumstance in a way that justified his own attempt to defy the natural bond between parent and child.

On top of that, with a mere gesture from Ha Gyeol earlier, his blood vessels had swollen as if they might burst at any moment. Only today had he learned that some damned thing had been planted inside his body.

Rage surged up in Yang Seok. It was an intense murderous intent that made him want to kill every last person involved in this affair.

Because Geom Mugeuk saw through his thoughts, he further fanned the conflict between father and son.

“Soon your father will learn that Ha Gyeol died because of you. Do you think your father will forgive you then?”

Because Yang Seok’s desire to survive would create another opportunity.

“He will not forgive me.”

Yang Seok wanted to protest, Why is it because of me? But he did not dare. Now was not the time to argue; it was the time to get on Geom Mugeuk’s good side.

“Then I must find out who is behind this as quickly as possible, no matter what.”

“As I said earlier, the only person who can find that out is you, Young Master Yang.”

Now that matters had come to this, Yang Seok did not hesitate.

“What should I do?”

“Meet your father and find out who stands behind Ha Gyeol.”

“He won’t tell me.”

“Ordinarily, he wouldn’t. But with Ha Gyeol’s death, the situation has changed. If you approach him in the right way, you will certainly be able to obtain information that will help us.”

“What way is that?”

Then came an answer he had not expected.

“Try approaching him honestly.”

Because Yang Seok had lived a life far removed from honesty, Geom Mugeuk’s words sounded unbearably unfamiliar.

“The outcome will depend on how honest you are.”

Yang Seok was half in doubt. No, he was certain it would not work. He had never been honest, and his father was better than anyone at seeing through lies. The very act of approaching him honestly would itself be a lie.

Even so, Yang Seok did not speak those true feelings aloud. After all, he was far from honest.

“I’ll give it a try!”

At that moment, Yang Seok had only one wish.

‘Fine. I’ll find out somehow, so deal with Father and whoever is behind him, then leave! Please, get out of my Northern Sea!’

* * *

When Geom Mugeuk returned to his quarters in the Ice Palace, Chwima was seated alone at the table by the window, drinking.

As if he had expected Geom Mugeuk to return, there were two cups set out before him.

Seeing the cup that had been prepared in advance put Geom Mugeuk in a good mood. That cup was Chwima’s trust in him.

The liquor Chwima poured filled that trust to the brim.

“Did you find out who was behind it?”

“No.”

They were the sort who used the Self-Destruction Art without hesitation. They were people who guarded secrets thoroughly, at least.

Of course, there was one strong premonition he had.

“I think he’s in the North Blood Sect.”

If they were seeking the Extreme Cold Physique, their purpose was likely to carry out the Great Art.

In such circumstances, if Ha Gyeol, his limb, was in the North Blood Sect, would the Blood King not also be hiding somewhere within the North Blood Sect?

“Whether he’s there or not, I’ll be cleaning up the North Blood Sect anyway.”

Under the pretext of developing medicine, they had examined people’s constitutions and lured them with promises of great sums of money.

Since most people were not of the Extreme Cold Physique, they had all met their deaths. For this alone, the North Blood Sect Master and Yang Seok had committed acts that could never be forgiven.

“By the way, what about the man we saved?”

Chwima had rescued the person in the carriage with truly fantastic skill.

“For now, I hid him in a safe house of the main cult.”

“What kind of man was he?”

“A father who volunteered to earn money for his child’s treatment.”

Chwima had already taken care of everything Geom Mugeuk would have done.

“I sent word to the branch near where he lives. Told them to treat the child before it was too late.”

“You’re amazing, Brother.”

“Amazing, my foot.”

If he were to confess honestly, he had done it to look good in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes.

Chwima knew very well what kind of person he was. He was not the sort to save someone and then send word for the sake of the saved person’s child.

Whether others lived or died had nothing to do with him. His had been a life spent drinking alone, paying no mind to anyone.

And yet now he was acting for others. To look good to Geom Mugeuk.

When he had first gotten involved in the succession struggle, he truly had never imagined he would come to have this kind of relationship with Geom Mugeuk.

“If it weren’t for you, would I have saved that man at all, let alone sent word?”

“It’s the same for me. If Ian hadn’t come to save the husband of the woman who gave him water, would I have cared about the Extreme Cold Physique?”

Chwima was about to ask, And who was it that made that Ian enter the martial world and do that? But he stopped himself. He knew very well that Geom Mugeuk was unexpectedly embarrassed by boasting and receiving praise.

“What do you plan to do now?”

“He lost both the Extreme Cold Physique and his limb.”

“He’ll come out for revenge, then. Or hide forever.”

There was no way to know which of those two paths the Blood King would choose. But the Blood King he knew was someone who always repaid what he suffered.

“He’ll definitely try to see blood.”

Chwima’s gaze grew intense as he looked down at his cup.

“Then I’ll need stronger liquor.”

* * *

The North Blood Sect Master was somewhat excited.

‘At last, it is today.’

It was the day the Extreme Cold Physique would arrive. Once the Great Art was completed using the Extreme Cold Physique, he would reveal himself to the world.

He recalled what Ha Gyeol had once said to him.

—Once we find the Extreme Cold Physique and complete the Great Art, the Northern Sea Ice Palace is finished. The Ice Palace Lord’s martial arts will be neutralized, and we’ll be able to enter the Ice Palace Treasury, which is so cold that only the Ice Palace Lord can enter it.

For the Ice Palace to disappear from the martial world of the Northern Sea also meant that the North Blood Sect would become the greatest sect in the Northern Sea. That had been his long-cherished wish.

‘I can’t live my whole life crushed under a woman.’

Astonishingly, he had lived with such thoughts since his youth. He had spent his life feeling inferior to the Ice Palace Lord.

Perhaps that was why he had accepted their proposal so easily.

‘Today, at last, I go from being the head of a snake to the tail of a dragon.’

He did not care if he was merely the tail. He would not live as the tail forever. One day, what had seemed like a tail would open its jaws wide and devour the head, the body, and all.

In any case, his foremost goal for now was to use them to strike down the Northern Sea Ice Palace. Like father, like son. Both men were dreaming of killing with a borrowed knife.

At that moment, Yang Seok entered.

“I greet the Sect Master.”

“What brings you here at this hour?”

“Ha Gyeol hasn’t been seen all day, so I came to find you. I wondered if perhaps he was here.”

Naturally, the North Blood Sect Master knew where Ha Gyeol had gone. He must have gone to receive the Extreme Cold Physique.

“I sent him somewhere for a short while.”

Yang Seok struggled to keep his expression under control. Once trust had been broken, every single word grated on him.

‘You never tell me the truth, not even once.’

And Geom Mugeuk had told him to be honest in a relationship like this?

‘I wonder what expression you’ll make when you learn he’s dead.’

Once news of Ha Gyeol’s death spread, suspicion could fall on him. After all, it was public knowledge that he and Ha Gyeol had not been on good terms.

But that did not mean they would suspect him blindly. Ha Gyeol’s martial skill had far surpassed his own. You know at least that much, don’t you? Sect Master.

“There is one thing I would like to ask.”

“What is it?”

“Why are you searching for the Extreme Cold Physique?”

Because it was an unexpected question, the North Blood Sect Master looked at his son with surprise and asked in return,

“Why do you suddenly ask that?”

“Because I think the time has come for me to know as well. It is something the sect has spent a great deal of time and money on, isn’t it?”

The North Blood Sect Master stared silently at his son. He sensed that something about him was different from usual.

“When the time comes later, I will tell you.”

Just then, a subordinate rushed in and delivered news.

“Urgent report.”

“What is it?”

The subordinate glanced once at Yang Seok, then quickly made his report.

“Ha Gyeol, the Young Sect Master’s guard, is dead.”

The North Blood Sect Master’s eyes flew wide open.

It was the first time Yang Seok had ever seen his father look so shocked. He had not even made that expression when Yang Seok’s younger sibling died.

Yang Seok, on the other hand, acted surprised.

“What in the world are you saying?”

He had to act with all his might. If he mishandled things here, the fact that he had been involved in the matter could be exposed.

“I told you to report in detail!”

Unlike Yang Seok, who flew into a rage, the North Blood Sect Master said nothing. Though he did not reveal his emotions outwardly, two conflicting feelings collided violently within his heart.

First, he was pleased. The man who could swell his blood vessels and kill him was dead.

At the same time, worry surged in. Ha Gyeol was a master even he could not handle.

‘Who on earth killed him?’

His death meant a crisis for the North Blood Sect Master, who had joined hands with him, did it not?

‘The Ice Palace Lord?’

The first person who came to mind was the Ice Palace Lord.

“Where and how did he die?”

“He was found beheaded on the road leading to the main sect. A carriage and more than ten other corpses were discovered nearby as well.”

At the mention of a carriage being found, the North Blood Sect Master understood.

‘The transport was attacked!’

He intuitively realized that someone had stolen the Extreme Cold Physique.

‘Whoever it is, they knew the Extreme Cold Physique was being transported to us today.’

The North Blood Sect Master clenched his molars. How much time, money, and effort had he invested to obtain the Extreme Cold Physique? This was disastrous. Something that absolutely should never have happened had occurred.

Meanwhile, Yang Seok gave an order to the subordinate.

“Track down the culprit! Investigate the dead and determine the cause of death quickly.”

“Yes, understood.”

After the subordinate withdrew, Yang Seok asked,

“What exactly did you order him to do?”

The North Blood Sect Master did not answer.

‘What should I do now?’

There were two choices. First, he could inform the man in the secret chamber of this. He knew that the person Ha Gyeol served was in the hidden chamber within the hidden chamber.

The problem was that he had never once seen him. Ha Gyeol alone had been difficult enough to deal with. To meet that person now, in a situation where the Extreme Cold Physique had been stolen? He could not know how that man would react.

The second method was to find the Extreme Cold Physique himself. Ha Gyeol was dead, but if they had the Extreme Cold Physique, the person in the secret chamber might be satisfied. Perhaps he could even take over Ha Gyeol’s role and turn misfortune into blessing.

Yang Seok, for his part, decided this was the moment to make his move.

“Father.”

He, who had called him Sect Master until now, called him Father.

The North Blood Sect Master gazed at Yang Seok.

“I know that you planned something with Ha Gyeol without telling me.”

Yang Seok accepted Geom Mugeuk’s advice and approached him with honesty. No, to be precise, he was only pretending to be frank.

“Please tell me what that matter is. Please allow me to help you, Father.”

The North Blood Sect Master was hesitating for a moment over how to answer.

At that very instant—

Grrrrrrng.

The secret door in the wall behind the grand chair began to open. It was the door to the very hidden chamber where he had held secret talks with Ha Gyeol. The door to the room that contained yet another hidden chamber within it.

The North Blood Sect Master stared at the opening door with eyes even more shocked than when he had heard that Ha Gyeol was dead. A door that should not have opened first was opening.

“Hrrk!”

Yang Seok grimaced and covered his mouth and nose with his hand.

An atrocious stench of blood, unlike anything he had ever smelled before, was flowing out through the gap in the door.

Chapter 424: Nothing Is Beyond Help
-----------------------------------
When the door opened completely, the Blood King walked out.

Having just emerged from the tank that had been filled with blood, he was naked, crimson droplets falling steadily from his body.

The stench of blood was so overpowering that Yang Seok wanted to bolt outside. Never in his life had he encountered such a thick reek of blood.

If liquor had alcohol essence, then this was surely the smell that would come from blood essence.

The Northern Blood Sect Master, on the other hand, stared at the Blood King without the slightest concern for the smell.

He was younger than he had expected.

His face was handsome, his limbs long, and his body was perfect, as though carved from stone.

And then there was that striking red hair that seized the gaze.

The first impression he gave off was more intense than that of any martial artist the sect master had ever seen.

‘He is insanely strong.’

Even though the Blood King had not revealed his aura, that thought came to him from first impressions alone.

Yang Seok, meanwhile, was so overwhelmed by the smell of blood that he could not properly gauge the Blood King. Still, there was one fact he could grasp with certainty.

‘So he’s the one behind everything.’

The fact that he had been hiding in a secret chamber behind the sect master’s hall was shocking.

‘To think Father was hiding a secret like this. He should have told me, his own son.’

Even at this moment, he was filling himself with resentment toward his father.

Tap tap tap tap!

A dozen or so martial artists sprang out from the doors on the left and right, blocking the way in front of the Northern Blood Sect Master. They too frowned at the appalling stench.

Ordinarily, whenever the Northern Blood Sect Master entered through the secret door, he always sent his subordinates away first. But today, he had not known that the man in the secret chamber would come outside, and so he had failed to dismiss them in advance.

The Blood King looked down at the martial artists with indifferent eyes.

The Northern Blood Sect Master felt many things from that gaze. It was not a cold, emotionless stare. It was hot like blood, sticky like blood, and chilling like blood.

There was another sensation as well. Order and logic seemed as if they would not work on him; it felt as though he lived purely by instinct—no, by the pull of blood.

But the Northern Blood Sect Master knew. He knew that was not all there was to him. If he had been a man faithful only to instinct, he could never have spent that long stretch of time in the secret chamber. He possessed a terrifying patience that overwhelmed all else. That was the most frightening thing of all.

At that very moment.

The Blood King slowly extended both hands forward.

Shwaaaaaak!

From the Blood King’s outstretched fingers, red lines shot out all at once.

Thud thud thud thud thud!

One line from each finger, ten lines in all, pierced through the martial artists’ foreheads.

They were not so weak as to die so pointlessly. Yet with that single move, the martial artists all collapsed and died at once.

It was a martial art even the Northern Blood Sect Master had never seen before. It was not finger wind, nor was it some weapon flying through the air.

The Blood King slowly made his way toward the taishi chair. Even as he walked, blood continued to drip down from his body.

The Blood King sat naked in the taishi chair.

The aura and presence he exuded were truly overwhelming. Added to that was the suffocating smell of blood.

The Blood King gently closed his eyes and recited some kind of mnemonic formula, then extended his hand to the side. Though no wound had been made, blood flowed down from his palm.

Drip.

Red smoke began to rise from the blood that had fallen to the floor.

Kwaang!

The Blood King unleashed a palm strike and blew away the ceiling.

The red smoke rising from the floor continued upward through the open ceiling. It looked as though it were sending a signal to someone.

Hearing the commotion, voices began to murmur outside.

The Northern Blood Sect Master shouted with internal energy in his voice.

“Everyone, withdraw!”

Soon the commotion outside subsided, and silence descended.

Seated in the taishi chair, the Blood King looked down at the Northern Blood Sect Master without a word.

The Northern Blood Sect Master knelt where he stood and bowed.

“I greet my lord!”

After becoming sect master—no, since the day he had been born—this was the first time the Northern Blood Sect Master had ever knelt before anyone other than his parents or master.

But he knew instinctively. If he did not kneel now, he would die by this man’s hand.

Yang Seok, who had been standing awkwardly, quickly knelt as well.

He was truly shocked. He had sensed that the naked man was strong, but he had never imagined his father would kneel. And without even exchanging a single word, at that.

‘My lord?’

The words from his father’s mouth sounded utterly unfamiliar.

Only then did the Blood King speak. His voice was low, yet carried a deep resonance.

“How did Ha Gyeol die?”

Had he heard the conversation outside from within the secret chamber? Or was he connected to Ha Gyeol by blood? He knew that Ha Gyeol was dead.

“We do not know yet either. We only just received the news.”

The Northern Blood Sect Master answered respectfully.

The Blood King rose from the taishi chair and walked forward.

With every step he took, the hearts of the Northern Blood Sect Master and Yang Seok pounded violently. Even the Northern Blood Sect Master, who had experienced every kind of hardship, felt fear.

This man possessed an aura incomparable to that of Ha Gyeol from before. He truly had never imagined that such a terrifying person had been in the secret chamber.

The one the Blood King approached was Yang Seok.

Seen up close, the Blood King’s eyes felt even more terrifying, and the thickened smell of blood made Yang Seok feel as if he would suffocate.

“You were there when Ha Gyeol died, weren’t you?”

It was not a question. It was an interrogation.

Yang Seok’s vision went dark. Terror swept over him, but he desperately denied it. If he told the truth, he felt as if he would truly be killed in a painful manner.

“No.”

But the Blood King was not merely probing.

“I smell Ha Gyeol’s blood on you.”

“!”

Yang Seok’s heart dropped. Since he had watched the fight, perhaps a few drops of Ha Gyeol’s blood might have splashed onto him.

But he could smell that? Was that even possible?

The Blood King glared at Yang Seok with cold eyes.

“Who killed Ha Gyeol? Was it you?”

He should have said he did not know, but the light in the Blood King’s eyes was so terrifying that Yang Seok squeezed his eyes shut and shouted.

“No, no! I didn’t kill him.”

“Then who was it?”

Yang Seok answered truthfully without hesitation.

“The Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.”

A faint blood qi seeped from the Blood King’s body as he placed his hand on Yang Seok’s head. The moment his hand touched the crown of his head, Yang Seok cried out as if begging.

“Please! Spare me.”

He felt a strange energy entering his body through the crown of his head. But he could not resist. His entire body trembled like an aspen leaf, afraid that the energy would burst with a pop inside his skull.

“Please!”

The Northern Blood Sect Master did not step forward to help his son. Even if he did, it would not stop him anyway.

More importantly, he was extremely angry at Yang Seok right now. He had been present where Ha Gyeol died, yet had lied to him with a perfectly innocent face. He had even acted shocked at Ha Gyeol’s death.

The Blood King removed the hand he had placed on Yang Seok’s crown.

“Haa, haa.”

Yang Seok had no idea what that energy had been, but at the thought that he had survived for the moment, he panted roughly in relief.

“What happened to the one with the Extreme Cold Physique?”

When the Blood King asked again, Yang Seok answered without hesitation.

“The Drunken Demon took her.”

At the words Drunken Demon, the Blood King’s eyes narrowed into long slits, and his pupils turned red. When Seo Nak had died, he had made a resolution inside the tank. He would kill the Drunken Demon no matter what. And now that very Drunken Demon had once again blocked his path.

Having lost both his subordinate and the Extreme Cold Physique, the Blood King was unrestrained.

“Tell the Young Cult Leader to meet me.”

The red pupils between those slit eyes were beyond terrifying.

“In the meantime, I will kill the Drunken Demon and retrieve the Extreme Cold Physique.”

* * *
