“He’ll target you, hyung.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, the Drunken Demon, who had been looking out the window, turned his head.

“Since you killed Elder Seo and took the Extreme Cold Physique away. He’ll definitely try to get revenge on you.”

“That’s what I want.”

The Drunken Demon was not frightened at all. He possessed the skill to be that way.

However, he had to fear the Blood King’s Self-Destruction Art. The Drunken Demon’s alcohol qi would not be able to block even that.

“I have a favor to ask.”

“What favor?”

“You have to grant it.”

“No. I refuse.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk said nothing.

“What is it?”

“You said you refuse. Ah! As expected, hyung is hyung. To think you’d refuse this favor.”

In the end, the Drunken Demon could not endure his curiosity.

“I’ll at least hear it first.”

The Drunken Demon said this while filling the cup set before him.

“As long as you don’t tell me to quit drinking, it’s fine. I realized it when I quit this time. Without alcohol, the joy of life disappears.”

“So anything except that is fine?”

“And don’t come to see me last when you return after leaving the cult. Also…”

To think he could be so relaxed even in this situation. Geom Mugeuk smiled and took out the Black Demon Sword at his waist, sheath and all.

“It isn’t a difficult favor…”

For some reason, feeling reluctant, the Drunken Demon shouted loudly.

“I don’t know what it is, but no! I refuse!”

* * *

Watching Geom Mugeuk approach from far away, Yang Seok’s heart pounded as if it would burst.

—You must hold him there as long as possible. If you fail, you die.

He recalled the Blood King’s cold warning.

‘He really will kill me.’

The Blood King was different from Geom Mugeuk. He would truly kill him without the slightest hesitation.

And his father would not help him either. Not if he thought back to the look in his father’s eyes when the Blood King gave the order.

In the meantime, Geom Mugeuk arrived before him.

“You’ve come?”

Yang Seok bowed at the waist. His attitude toward Geom Mugeuk was now completely different from before.

“I apologize for contacting you so urgently.”

“Why did you ask to see me?”

“I found information about the person behind this.”

“What kind of information?”

“Shall we walk for a moment?”

Yang Seok began walking together with Geom Mugeuk.

The place where they met was a sparsely populated field a short distance from the Northern Blood Sect.

As they walked in silence, Yang Seok spoke cautiously.

“I went to see my father. As you told me, I spoke honestly. I said I wanted to be of help to him. Then my father told me. I truly had no idea that speaking honestly could wield such great power.”

“Where is the one behind this?”

“He said that person is at the Ice Sword Sect.”

The Ice Sword Sect was one of the three great powers of the Northern Sea, along with the Northern Sea Ice Palace and the Northern Blood Sect.

‘Even if things go wrong later, I can pin everything on Father.’

The Blood King was truly terrifying, but the opponent here was the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult. He had to dig an escape route for himself.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been quietly watching Yang Seok, suddenly asked.

“Is the idea that he is at the Ice Sword Sect your own, Young Master Yang? Or did that man tell you to say it?”

Yang Seok forced himself to ask back calmly.

“What do you mean?”

Yang Seok could not tell whether Geom Mugeuk was testing him or had truly noticed that he was lying.

“Well, I suppose it would have to be somewhere like the Ice Sword Sect to buy time. Which means the one behind this has revealed himself in front of you.”

Even if he truly tried not to be surprised, it was impossible not to be when he was with Geom Mugeuk.

“You fooled me once, and that should have been enough. Yet you’re deceiving me again.”

He felt that he could no longer hide it. Yang Seok had been so afraid of the Blood King that he had resolved to take his side.

But now that he was actually seeing Geom Mugeuk again, he realized there was another kind of fear. A person who seemed to look straight into his heart; before Geom Mugeuk, he became naked.

“I was wrong. Please spare me!”

Yang Seok immediately prostrated himself on the spot.

‘You sharp-eyed bastard! Even so, I’ll survive no matter what!’

Kneel here, kneel there. He would kneel and kneel again until his knees could no longer bend if it meant he survived.

“But how did you know I was lying?”

“When you asked me to walk with you.”

“Why that?”

“If you had found information, you would have been busy showing off what you learned the moment you saw me. But you asked me to walk with you? That was strange, wasn’t it?”

Geom Mugeuk had grasped exactly what kind of person Yang Seok was.

“That man forced me, so I had no choice.”

“Where in the world is there such a thing as having no choice? There are only things you chose wrongly. Today is the same. The moment you saw me, you could have said, ‘This is the situation.’ You could have told me honestly, couldn’t you?”

Yang Seok had no excuse to offer.

“What else did that man say?”

“He only told me to meet the Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk quietly looked into his eyes and suddenly said.

“He said he would kill the Drunken Demon while you stalled for time, didn’t he?”

Geom Mugeuk saw the unmistakable agitation in Yang Seok’s eyes.

“So in the end, you were stalling like this so he could kill the Drunken Demon.”

Startled, Yang Seok waved his hands.

“No, that’s not it.”

“If not?”

“I only…”

He tried to make an excuse, but the words caught in his throat. This was not an opponent excuses would work on.

Yang Seok knelt again.

“Please forgive me. If you see that bastard, you’ll understand me. He isn’t human!”

With his head lowered deeply, his true thoughts were far from his pleading cries.

‘Forgive me. You high-and-mighty types get pleasure from forgiveness, don’t you?’

He thought that only because he did not know what kind of gaze Geom Mugeuk was looking down at him with. His sins were already too great, and his death had been decided. It was only a question of when.

“Please go quickly. Shouldn’t you go at once and help the Drunken Demon?”

Geom Mugeuk was not worried about the Drunken Demon at all.

“Why are you worrying about a Demon Venerable? The only person in this murim qualified to worry about a Demon Venerable is the Heavenly Demon.”

It was a statement that upheld the pride of a Demon Venerable, and it also meant that he trusted the Drunken Demon that much.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk’s composure and trust were not solely because he believed in the Drunken Demon’s skills. The opponent was far too strong for that.

The supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Thread that had been wrapped around the Black Demon Sword in Geom Mugeuk’s hand was nowhere to be seen.

Geom Mugeuk’s request had been for the Drunken Demon to wrap that very supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Thread around his body.

Though the Drunken Demon had insisted he would not wear it even if he died, Geom Mugeuk had ultimately made him wrap it all around himself. Not only around his heart, but also several times around each vital point. A fight with the Blood King was a fight in which one never knew when or where something might erupt.

And there was one more thing he trusted.

He had the Swift Step that could bring him back before it was too late.

“Get up now.”

Yang Seok rose and tried in any way he could to put Geom Mugeuk in a good mood.

“I believe in you. The Young Cult Leader will eliminate him. You’ll eliminate him in the most satisfying way.”

“You won’t get to see it.”

Geom Mugeuk vanished from before his eyes in an instant.

Yang Seok, who wore a puzzled expression, did not know. He did not know that his face and entire body had turned red in an instant.

Kwaaaaaaaang!

He exploded through the Self-Destruction Art. A tremendous force of blood qi swept forward, but Geom Mugeuk had already entered the Space-Time Transposition Art.

Geom Mugeuk appeared again.

Yang Seok was dead, a huge hole torn through his chest.

The bastard had mercilessly used the young sect master of the Northern Blood Sect as a tool for the Self-Destruction Art. Even though the target was the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.

Geom Mugeuk finally felt that the Blood King had emerged into the world.

He likely had not expected Yang Seok to kill him when even Ha Gyeol had failed. If so, this was the Blood King’s greeting.

Looking down at the gaping hole, Geom Mugeuk said softly.

“Yeah, nice to meet you too, you lunatic.”

Chapter 425: Come Here and Have a Drink.
----------------------------------------
Ian and Han Seol were in the Ice Palace.

There was a special reason for the two of them to visit the Ice Palace today.

“I’m sorry for imposing on you.”

The person Ian greeted courteously was the Ice Palace Lord.

“The Young Cult Leader gave strict orders that I must not leave the Palace Lord’s Hall under any circumstances.”

“The Young Cult Leader has already been here.”

Before leaving the palace, Geom Mugeuk had already asked him to look after Ian and Han Seol while they stayed together for a time. If it had been any other enemy, he would have brought Ian along as well to let her gain experience.

But not when it came to the Blood King. Even if Ian had achieved great mastery of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, he would still have made her stay in the Palace Lord’s Hall.

The Ice Palace Lord did not consider it an imposition at all. It was a matter tied to the safety of his daughter and the Ice Palace, and it was also a request made because Geom Mugeuk trusted him.

Only, there had been a small dispute before he accepted the request.

—Are you not underestimating the Ice Palace far too much?

It had sounded as though he were telling even him to hide inside the Palace Lord’s Hall.

—If it were an ordinary enemy, I would have entrusted the matter to you, Palace Lord, and already left the North Sea. But these are people who use the Self-Destruction Art without hesitation. There will be great sacrifices.

—Even so, they targeted this palace, so we should be the ones to deal with them.

Of course, the Ice Palace had many experts besides the Ice Palace Lord. If they stepped forward, they would be a great help, but many sacrifices would still have to be borne.

—They are not enemies. They are monsters. And we are good at hunting monsters.

To the Ice Palace Lord, who was still hesitating, Geom Mugeuk added one final persuasion.

—Please leave monsters to be hunted by devils.

How could he refuse when the man was humbling himself by calling himself a devil? He was grateful, but there was one thing he was curious about.

—Why are you going so far to help us?

Because the enemy is Hwa Mugi’s subordinate.

—Because I was treated to good liquor.

As Geom Mugeuk requested, the Ice Palace Lord withdrew all the warriors around the Drunken Demon’s residence. Since the Blood King himself was moving, he judged that whoever guarded the place would only become an unnecessary sacrifice.

After briefly recalling his conversation with Geom Mugeuk, the Ice Palace Lord looked at Ian. Ian was standing by the window, looking outside.

She had been a woman who strangely drew his attention from the moment he first saw her.

“You said your name was Ian, correct?”

Ian, who had been looking out the window, turned back to the Ice Palace Lord and answered politely.

“Yes, that is correct.”

“The Young Cult Leader seems to cherish you greatly.”

“He has always been an affectionate person.”

“An affectionate Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult.”

“You are witnessing the birth of a new Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

Ian spoke as though making a joke, but the Ice Palace Lord took it seriously. After all, he was the Young Cult Leader who had restored his relationship with his daughter.

‘Perhaps, years from now, I may think back on today. I saw the Heavenly Demon when he was still the Young Cult Leader.’

Han Seol asked Ian.

“What was the Young Cult Leader like when he was young?”

The Ice Palace Lord looked at his daughter. It was the first time he had seen her show interest in someone else. She had never once been curious about others. Truly, thanks to Geom Mugeuk, he was experiencing many new things.

At Han Seol’s question, Ian hesitated for a moment before answering.

He hadn’t always been like that. He had been a good person, yes, but not this good.

Not until that day when Geom Mugeuk had changed as if suddenly hatching from an egg—the day the Divine-Demon Struggle was held.

From the moment he returned after finishing the spar, Geom Mugeuk was different.

That day, Geom Mugeuk had said this.

—Starting today, I’ve decided to live a changed life.

He had truly kept those words. Geom Mugeuk changed, and that change transformed everyone around him. Herself, the Demon Supremes, and even the Heavenly Demon.

Ian told the two of them what Geom Mugeuk had said that day.

“The Young Cult Leader said this: that he did not believe people changed little by little. When there is some kind of turning point, they change all at once.”

That was why she, too, had tried to change. To become an exception to the saying that people never change.

“He matured, and changed greatly.”

Just as she answered with a smile, something entered Ian’s line of sight.

Far outside the window, red smoke was rising into the sky. It felt ominous. It was unmistakably smoke meant to send a signal to someone.

“Ah! Smoke is rising over there too.”

As if answering that smoke like a beacon, another plume of smoke began to rise from far away in the opposite direction.

* * *

“I’ll raise your monthly wage next month.”

At the owner’s words, the cook smiled faintly in the kitchen. The cook was a man who could not speak.

But acquiring this cook had been the greatest stroke of luck in the life of the owner of the Northern Ocean Inn. His food tasted good. He had no major complaints about his treatment. He did not capriciously say he would quit after a short time. He simply stayed in the kitchen and cooked in silence.

Just then, a customer said,

“Looks like there’s a fire. Look at that smoke over there.”

“Why is the smoke so red? If there was a fire, shouldn’t black smoke be rising?”

The owner was looking at the smoke rising in the distance along with the customers when it happened.

“Good heavens, you startled me!”

The owner, who had casually turned to the side, was shocked. The cook had come out without a sound at some point and was staring at the smoke with them. He had never once left the kitchen during business hours.

“It seems a fire broke out somewhere over there.”

At the owner’s words, the cook spoke.

“That is not a fire.”

Both the owner and the customers were startled by the cook’s words. It was surprising enough that the cook had come out of the kitchen, but it was the first time they had ever seen him speak like this.

“You knew how to talk?”

The man who had opened his mouth felt like a completely different person from usual.

“It is a signal from our lord, calling us.”

“Me?”

Just as the owner opened his eyes wide, wondering what on earth he meant—

Puhk!

The cook’s hand pierced the owner’s heart.

“You need not raise my wage.”

The customer beside them screamed and fled. When the cook waved his hand, the fleeing customer’s body was torn apart, spraying blood as he collapsed.

The cook looked down at the blood streaming from his hand and arm with a smile.

He inhaled, smelling the scent of blood, then walked outside with blood dripping from his hand.

When he recited an incantation, red smoke began to rise from the blood that had fallen from his hand onto the ground.

It was smoke that would awaken someone else.

* * *

The Drunken Demon was sitting on a wooden platform in the yard, drinking liquor.

Dozens of liquor bottles were placed around him, and there was also a large liquor jar. He was drinking with clear intent.

It was not all the same liquor. He was drinking every one of the dozens of varieties of liquor found in the North Sea.

The Drunken Demon drank a cup of this liquor, then that liquor. Each time, he gave his appraisal. This liquor is good because it is light. This liquor is good because it is strong. This liquor is good because it is sweet. This liquor is good because it is bitter.

He judged every liquor not by saying it was bad for this reason, but that it was good for this reason.

Then he looked up at the top of the wall and asked,

“Care for a drink?”

A man was standing on the wall, looking down this way, though no one knew when he had arrived.

The man was the Blood King. He had been naked before, but now he wore a red martial uniform. The thick scent of blood revealed who he was.

On his way here from outside, he had encountered no obstruction at all. There were no warriors guarding the place. It meant they had known he would come and had been waiting.

Far behind the Blood King, red smoke was rising here and there. In that rising red smoke, one could feel the tension of the eve of war.

The Drunken Demon drank another cup.

“Come down and have a drink.”

The Blood King only looked down at the Drunken Demon without a word.

“If you don’t want to, never mind.”

The Drunken Demon drank deeply. Even as he drank, his gaze remained on the Blood King.

Hot and cold.

Cold and hot.

Both men possessed eyes that held heat and coldness alike.

The smell of liquor and the smell of blood.

In the space where the two faced each other, two primal scents that stirred human instinct mingled.

The Drunken Demon behaved as though he was not tense at all, but his body reacted differently. Instinctively, it tightened, preparing for the opponent’s attack.

‘He came to kill me.’

Geom Mugeuk had been right. He had said the one behind this would surely target him, and now that person had come personally.

And the Blood King had not come alone today.

A dozen or so warriors climbed onto the wall where the Blood King stood. The one who climbed up right beside the Blood King was none other than the Northern Blood Sect Master. Those who had come with him were the trusted limbs of the Northern Blood Sect Master.

The Northern Blood Sect Master asked the Drunken Demon,

“Where have you hidden the Extreme Cold Physique?”

Instead of answering, the Drunken Demon rebuked him.

“How does a man who is the Northern Blood Sect Master end up being led around by this filthy stench of blood?”

It was a remark that pricked at his pride, but the Northern Blood Sect Master pretended not to hear and asked again.

“I asked where the Extreme Cold Physique is.”

At that, the Drunken Demon drank his liquor and answered,

“Why do you keep asking? Did you leave it in my care?”

The Northern Blood Sect Master looked at the Blood King. At length, the Blood King spoke in a low, resonant voice.

“He is not someone who will speak obediently. Kill him.”

The Blood King had grasped precisely what kind of person the Drunken Demon was. Though he looked as if he were merely grinning and drinking, that liquor was as thick as blood. He was not an easy man by any means.

The Drunken Demon said to the Blood King,

“There’s one thing I’m curious about. May I ask?”

The Blood King stared at the Drunken Demon in silence, as if telling him to try.

“That Self-Destruction Art you people use—when you explode, does that red head of yours explode too?”

It was a deliberate provocation. Since the Northern Blood Sect Master was present as well, the Drunken Demon thought the Blood King would surely reveal some emotion. But not even the look in the Blood King’s eyes changed.

‘He’s calmer than he looks.’

The Drunken Demon also sensed what sort of person the Blood King was. The opponent was not a murderer who simply went mad and ran wild. When it was time to be hot, his heat operated; when it was time to be cold, his coldness operated. He was truly the most difficult type of enemy to face.

Srrrng!

The one who drew his sword was the Northern Blood Sect Master. Since the Blood King had given the order to kill, they had to step forward. Besides, the Drunken Demon was provoking the Blood King like that; if they simply stood by, they might be killed by the Blood King.

When the Northern Blood Sect Master drew his sword, his subordinates drew theirs all at once. Their momentum was no ordinary thing.

They revealed their killing intent, but the Drunken Demon’s gaze remained fixed on the Blood King.

The fact that he sent them out first while knowing perfectly well they would be sacrificed meant—

‘He wants to confirm my skill?’

And there was likely also the intention of making him consume his inner force. Though the Blood King gave off such a thick scent of blood, he himself was a man with neither blood nor tears.

‘And that smoke! It is surely a signal calling someone.’

Meanwhile, the Northern Blood Sect Master was just as ruthless.

He had come along because he had no choice due to the Blood King, but he knew very well how dangerous a situation he was in. He was being driven out to face a Demon Supreme.

‘Even if I have to sacrifice all my subordinates, I alone must survive.’

First, the Northern Blood Sect Master ordered three of his subordinates to attack.

The three subordinates lightly leaped down from the wall. In any case, the number of people who could launch a coordinated attack was bound to be limited. The best course would be to wage a wheel battle, taking turns to wear down the opponent’s strength.

The three warriors approached the Drunken Demon from three directions.

The Drunken Demon was still sitting in place, drinking liquor.

Whoooosh!

Suddenly, alcoholic qi surged up around them, and in an instant, all of them vanished into the mist.

From within the mist came the sound of swords swinging through the air. There was also the sound of flesh and bone being cut away, and the sound of neck bones breaking.

Ssssst.

When the mist disappeared again, the Drunken Demon was sitting in the same spot, drinking liquor.

The three men who had approached to kill the Drunken Demon lay sprawled as corpses around him. Because it had happened inside the mist, no one had seen how they died.

“If you like the smell of blood better than the smell of liquor, I’ll let you smell your fill.”

The Northern Blood Sect Master’s expression hardened. He had expected the three to be defeated, but he had not expected them to be defeated so quickly.

Moreover, fighting inside a mist that completely blocked their vision was overwhelmingly disadvantageous.

“Pour out sword energy and sword aura!”

At the Northern Blood Sect Master’s command, the remaining subordinates unleashed sword energy all at once. Once again, alcoholic qi blocked their vision.

Swish swish swish swish swish swish swish swish swish!

Only after they had poured out a storm of sword energy did the Northern Blood Sect Master make them stop attacking.

The mist cleared, revealing the ruined state of the courtyard. The ground had been overturned and shattered.

The Drunken Demon was sitting on the edge of the broken platform, drinking liquor.

What was astonishing was not that the Drunken Demon was unharmed. It was that not a single liquor bottle scattered around him had been broken.

In other words, amid the flying sword energy, the Drunken Demon had protected the liquor bottles.

‘This is possible?’

Not only the Northern Blood Sect Master, but the remaining warriors as well were intimidated.

The Drunken Demon looked up at the Northern Blood Sect Master and said,

“I told you to come down and drink.”

When no one answered, the Drunken Demon drank straight from the bottle.

Since the Blood King still did not so much as open his mouth, the one dealing with the Drunken Demon was the Northern Blood Sect Master.

“You seem to be trying to buy time, but it would be better not to expect help from the Young Cult Leader.”

“You’re the ones buying time.”

It was a rebuke asking why the Blood King and the Northern Blood Sect Master did not step forward first and instead sacrificed innocent subordinates.

“Are you worried? That the Young Cult Leader might arrive before you kill me?”

Of course the Northern Blood Sect Master was worried.

He could sense that the Blood King regarded the Young Cult Leader’s martial arts highly. If he had been confident in eliminating both of them together, he would not have had Yang Seok lure the Young Cult Leader away.

“The Young Cult Leader cannot come. He is already in our hands.”

It was a statement meant to shake the Drunken Demon’s composure, but he did not fall for it.

“If that were true, you would be saying right here and now that we should exchange the Young Cult Leader for the Extreme Cold Physique.”

The Drunken Demon was not only more intelligent than anyone, he also had absolute faith in Geom Mugeuk.

That was why he even made this joke.

“Don’t worry. The Young Cult Leader always comes to me last.”

The Drunken Demon picked up a bottle and slowly rose from his seat. It was merely a drunk man standing up, yet that presence made everyone tense.

“The person you should be worried about is not the Young Cult Leader. It’s me.”

Even in this moment when taut tension filled the air, the Drunken Demon gulped liquor straight from the bottle.

“Because instead of keeping me company over drinks, you people keep swinging blades around at me, so I’ve been drinking alone.”

The color of the alcoholic qi wavering around the Drunken Demon’s body began to change.

“And I’m very drunk.”

Chapter 426: Blood Is Thicker Than Wine
---------------------------------------
Purple energy shimmered around the Drunken Demon’s body.

He truly was drunk. Seeing him in that state, one ought to have thought, This is our chance!

Yet no one dared rush at him as he staggered, eyes half-unfocused.

Yes, it was because he was the Drunken Demon.

When the Drunken Demon was well and truly drunk and reeling, how could anyone dare find the nerve to charge him?

Moreover, the languid gaze beneath his half-lowered lids felt even more terrifying than eyes brimming with killing intent.

When the wine bottle in the Drunken Demon’s hand ran dry, he picked up another bottle from the ground. Then he drank again. He drank so much one could not help but wonder if it was truly all right for him to drink like that.

“Ah, I’m drunk.”

The Drunken Demon staggered forward and looked at the Northern Blood Sect Master and the Blood King.

“Why don’t you come down already? My neck hurts from looking up.”

At that, the Blood King sat down. As though he intended to watch this fight comfortably from where he was, he settled into an entirely relaxed posture.

For the Northern Blood Sect Master, this was pressure. Silent pressure that said, I’ll watch from here, so hurry up and go die.

‘Damn it.’

The Northern Blood Sect Master simply could not get a read on this Blood King. Now that Elder Seo of the Ice Palace was dead, he was certain the Northern Blood Sect would be needed if they were to rule the North Sea. And yet this man was trying to use him as a disposable pawn. He seemed more emotional than anyone, yet he had not so much as blinked at the Drunken Demon’s provocation a moment ago.

The Northern Blood Sect Master looked at the smoke rising far off behind them.

‘And on top of that, he’s even summoned his own subordinates.’

Just what is he planning to do? If he made the board this big, there would be no cleaning up afterward.

In any case, now was the time to fight. Offending the capricious Blood King was even more frightening than fighting the Drunken Demon.

And if he joined hands with the Blood King and won, he could fulfill his long-held dream of becoming the greatest force in the North Sea.

“Kill him!”

At the Northern Blood Sect Master’s command, seven martial artists descended below the wall all at once.

Their opponent was the Drunken Demon, so how could they not be afraid? Even so, they pinned their hopes on the fact that there were seven of them.

Sssssst.

The alcoholic qi that blossomed in an instant obscured that hope.

The Drunken Demon’s voice came from within the mist.

“When the mist rises, run west. Then you can live.”

But none of them fled through the path of life the Drunken Demon had opened for them.

They were not ordinary martial artists of the Northern Blood Sect. They were the hands and feet of the Northern Blood Sect Master, men who had spent their lives following the sect master’s orders and committing all manner of evil. Whether in life or death, they were one body.

The alcoholic qi that rose this time was far stronger in drunkenness than before. Just being exposed to the mist for a brief moment was enough for them to begin growing drunk. Even so, trying to expel the alcoholic qi from within the alcoholic qi was a meaningless attempt.

If it had been poison, they could at least have taken an antidote. The most terrifying thing was that because it was not poison, it could not be blocked.

Whoooong!

One martial artist waved his hand, dispersing the alcoholic qi. The mist around his body vanished for a moment, then reappeared.

The instant he waved his hand again.

The Drunken Demon was standing in the place where the alcoholic qi had disappeared.

The startled martial artist thrust out his sword in haste, but it was already too late.

The Drunken Demon’s palm lightly struck the martial artist’s forehead first.

With a thud, the martial artist toppled over.

By the time he fell, the Drunken Demon had already vanished from that spot.

“I told you, if you want to live, run west!”

But instead of running there, they sent sword qi flying toward the place his voice had come from.

Swish! Swiiish! Swish!

Sword qi poured down, but no scream came from the Drunken Demon.

“West is that way. No, is it this way?”

The drunken Drunken Demon’s voice sounded again. This time, one martial artist threw himself in the opposite direction from where the voice had come and thrust out his sword.

‘It’s probably a trick to lure us in!’

It was a choice he had made using his head in his own way, but he could not keep up with a drunken mind.

His sword cut through empty air, and at the same time, as though waiting for him, someone’s arm coiled around his neck from behind.

‘How did he read my thoughts?’

Crack.

With that thought as his last, the martial artist’s neck bone broke, and he collapsed where he stood.

“Ah, I’m drunk.”

Swish! Swish!

Swords flew in from two directions toward that place, but the Drunken Demon was already gone. The Drunken Demon was certainly drunk, but his movements were faster than anyone’s. This was his world, one even the Blood King did not dare rashly leap into.

“All your subordinates are going to die. Are you still not going to step in?”

The Drunken Demon’s voice came from within the mist.

But the Northern Blood Sect Master stood atop the wall and did not move.

‘If I go into that mist without a plan, I’ll die.’

Even here, a little distance away, the smell alone was enough to make him feel drunk.

Inside that mist, his subordinates must truly be drunk. Their bodies and judgment were surely growing sluggish. Even being faster than usual would not have been enough.

Truly, if not for the Blood King, this was an opponent he should never have fought.

He had to find a weakness before all his subordinates were killed, no matter what.

A scream rang out from within the mist. This time too, it was one of his subordinates.

Unable to endure the terror, one martial artist shot upward through the mist. For now, he had chosen to get out of the alcoholic qi first.

Swaaaaaaang!

Something pierced through the mist and flew like a streak of lightning, shattering his jaw.

The dead subordinate would not know what had struck and killed him, but the Northern Blood Sect Master saw it clearly. It was a pebble that had been lying on the ground. In the Drunken Demon’s hands, anything he could grasp became a weapon.

Once more, the sound of a heavy blow burst from within the mist.

Thud!

A martial artist was flung out through the mist and slammed into the wall where the Blood King and the Northern Blood Sect Master stood. He had already died the moment he took that punch to the abdomen.

This time, one martial artist burst out through the western side of the mist. He was truly trying to flee west.

Swiiiiish!

Baaang!

A strand of astral qi flew out and smashed him apart.

The one who sent out that palm strike and killed him was the Northern Blood Sect Master. This fight could not be allowed to become known to the world. He could not permit someone to disobey his order and flee.

The mist scattered left and right, and from the center of the mist, the Drunken Demon appeared. Only the area around where he stood was clear of mist.

The Drunken Demon looked at the Northern Blood Sect Master with drunken eyes.

The emotions contained in that languid gaze were contempt and scorn. The Northern Blood Sect Master hardened his eyes and shouted.

“He died because of you!”

At that moment, the seventh martial artist lowered his head as if in despair and walked toward the Drunken Demon.

“West is not this way.”

The very instant the martial artist lifted his head.

The Drunken Demon hurriedly raised his protective astral qi and crossed both arms to shield his face.

The man had already activated a self-destruction art, his face and body glowing bright red.

Kwaaaang!

The blood energy that poured from his chest engulfed the Drunken Demon. From the impact, the Drunken Demon was hurled backward.

The Drunken Demon rolled across the ground and collapsed. Then the alcoholic qi that had hung thick around the area vanished.

“We’ve taken down the Drunken Demon!”

The Northern Blood Sect Master shouted in delight. The Blood King had not simply driven them into death without reason.

“So this was the arrangement my lord had prepared!”

Although he was displeased that he had not been told beforehand, the fact that he did not have to fight the Drunken Demon was enough for him to endure it.

“Let us go before the Young Cult Leader returns.”

But the Blood King remained seated, staring at the Drunken Demon lying on the ground.

When the Northern Blood Sect Master’s gaze naturally turned that way.

“Uuugh, that hurts.”

Astonishingly, the Drunken Demon was slowly getting to his feet.

The Drunken Demon rubbed his chest and drank wine first. It had exploded abruptly in an unexpected situation, and under ordinary circumstances, he would have been gravely injured.

However, the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk wrapped around his body had prevented the blood energy from piercing through him, allowing him to avoid a serious wound.

‘Little brother, I lived thanks to you.’

Geom Mugeuk had saved his life. He really had been so relentless in making him wrap that supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk around his body.

A peculiar light flashed through the Blood King’s eyes as he looked at the Drunken Demon.

He could not believe the man was this unharmed after taking that attack from so close.

“He’s wearing protective armor.”

The Blood King spoke to the Northern Blood Sect Master with certainty. When the gaze that had been fixed on the Drunken Demon all along turned toward him, the Northern Blood Sect Master understood.

‘He wants me to go out and fight too?’

In the end, it meant that he too should go out and die.

The Blood King’s eyes said this.

‘Will you die here, or die fighting?’

There are moments in life when one suddenly wakes from a dream.

Huh? So that was it? Huh? So that was the kind of person he was? Huh? That was how he thought of me?

That moment when your heart sinks and goosebumps rise over your skin. The moment when you cannot manage your expression and stammer, when your face flushes red with humiliation.

At that moment, the Northern Blood Sect Master was exactly like that. He had thought that, at the very least, he was not such a trivial existence to the Blood King.

But he could tell that the Blood King regarded him the same as the subordinates who had died before him.

“Give me a cup of wine too.”

The Northern Blood Sect Master lightly leaped down below. Even so, the Blood King was more frightening than the Drunken Demon. No matter what, he could not die atop this wall.

The Northern Blood Sect Master slowly walked toward the Drunken Demon. He intended to attempt a surprise attack when he received the wine and drank.

‘There is only one chance.’

If he fought within the alcoholic qi, he would surely lose.

The Drunken Demon spread his arms wide and welcomed him.

“At last, I have a drinking companion. Is sharing a cup of wine together truly this difficult?”

The Northern Blood Sect Master approached the Drunken Demon step by step. Forcing his pounding heart to settle, he aimed for a single instant.

The two of them were now close enough to touch if they reached out.

“There are no cups, so let’s drink straight from the bottle.”

The Drunken Demon was about to offer the entire wine bottle when he suddenly seemed to remember something and asked.

“By the way, Sect Master, are you that afraid of that man?”

It was an unexpected question.

“I respect my lord.”

“What is there to respect about a man who is too afraid of his opponent and pushes his subordinates into the jaws of death first?”

The Northern Blood Sect Master felt bitter. After all, the subordinate being pushed in was himself. He had thought he was not someone who deserved to be treated like this.

“Does that man over there have some weakness of yours?”

The Northern Blood Sect Master did not answer.

A weakness? He had two. The blood art that could swell his blood vessels and kill him, and his own desire to bring down the Northern Sea Ice Palace and stand in its place.

Just which weakness had dragged him all the way here?

“Wouldn’t it be better to join hands with me and fight that bastard? Then even if you died, wouldn’t you have no regrets?”

The moment he said that, a cold sneer formed at the lips of the Blood King sitting on the wall.

“Look at how that man over there is laughing.”

How on earth was he laughing?

The very instant the Northern Blood Sect Master turned back without thinking.

Swish! Stab!

The sound of flesh being torn rang out.

When the Northern Blood Sect Master looked back at the Drunken Demon with a startled face, a bloodied dagger was in the hand that was not holding the wine bottle.

‘What?’

The next moment.

Puaaaak.

Blood spurted from the Northern Blood Sect Master’s neck. The instant he turned to look at the Blood King, the Drunken Demon had ambushed him like lightning and stabbed him in the throat.

The Northern Blood Sect Master had only thought of making a surprise attack himself; he had never imagined that the Drunken Demon would ambush and kill him. All the more so because the Drunken Demon had displayed such extraordinary skill earlier and had fought with such ease.

“I’m sorry. Drunkards’ fights are always ugly.”

The Northern Blood Sect Master wanted to say something in response to the Drunken Demon’s words, but the wound in his neck was too deep.

He clutched his throat, but could not stop the blood flowing out.

The Northern Blood Sect Master collapsed as if crumbling. He died with both eyes wide open, as though aggrieved by this hollow death.

The Drunken Demon crouched down and closed his eyes for him.

He was someone who had to be killed anyway. The Drunken Demon knew everything he had done in the process of searching for those with Extreme Yin Bodies within the Northern Blood Sect. It had only been a question of when to deal with him.

He had sensed that the Northern Blood Sect Master would not betray the Blood King in this fight. He had also sensed that the reason he came asking for wine was to launch a surprise attack.

“There can never be a good end for someone who stands beside a man like that.”

Using the thick scent of blood rising from the Northern Blood Sect Master as his drinking snack, the Drunken Demon drank his wine. Of course, even as he drank, he did not take his eyes off the Blood King for a single moment.

Cold and heat entangled in midair, heat and cold.

The Blood King spoke to the Drunken Demon for the first time.

“And yet beside you, there is not even anyone watching, is there?”

The mockery aimed at Geom Mugeuk did not work on the Drunken Demon.

“He shouldn’t come to a filthy, dangerous place like this.”

Instead, the Drunken Demon went a step further.

“For that, I am truly grateful to you. For keeping the Young Cult Leader from coming here, I mean. He is not someone suited to a place like this, reeking of wine and blood. People like you and me are the ones who should be grabbing each other by the collar and rolling in the dirt.”

The Blood King looked down at the Drunken Demon with a strange gaze.

“You are an odd one.”

“It’s because I’m drunk. No, that’s not it. It’s because you aren’t drunk.”

Rambling incoherently, the Drunken Demon drank again. He drank to what seemed an excessive degree. But the Blood King saw it. The more wine he drank, the more the Drunken Demon’s eyelids drooped, but his pupils shone ever clearer.

The Drunken Demon crooked a finger, beckoning for the Blood King to come down.

At that, the Blood King finally rose from his seat and lightly jumped down.

At last, the Blood King had stepped forward. In that place filled with drunkenness, the Blood King’s blood energy began to swell.

“Blood is thicker than wine.”

The blood energy and alcoholic qi refused to yield even an inch to each other.

“How dare you compare cheap blood to wine?”

As the primal, violent scents of wine and blood mingled, the two energies began to push fiercely against one another.

Chapter 427: The Smell of Blood and Liquor Is Ours Alone.
---------------------------------------------------------
The two energies stood tautly opposed.

If someone had been standing between them, even a master would have found it difficult to endure. It was not simply because the aura the two men exuded was powerful.

It was because of the nature of those two auras. Merely being there made one dizzy, nauseous, even left one’s mind hazy. On top of that, there was the unbearable stench of blood and reek of liquor.

“This is too tiring. I can’t keep this up.”

The Drunken Demon was the first to withdraw his liquor qi. He had already consumed internal energy dealing with the Northern Blood Sect’s subordinates, and though not deep, he had even been injured by the Self-Destruction Art. In many ways, he was at a disadvantage.

So he judged that there was no need to continue this contest of aura and waste his internal energy.

With an expression as if he had completely lost the battle of qi, the Drunken Demon drank a cup in defeat.

“Ugh, bitter. So bitter.”

The Blood King, on the other hand, did not withdraw his blood qi, and the surroundings became filled with it.

It was a blood qi so thick with the smell of blood that it seemed to suffocate, yet the Drunken Demon instead breathed it in deeply and smelled the blood.

“Does your blood taste like this too?”

The Blood King glared at the Drunken Demon with eyes asking what he meant.

“I’m saying my blood would taste like liquor, but does yours taste this vile?”

Perhaps because it was a question related to blood, the Blood King answered.

“There will never come a day when anyone learns the taste of my blood. No one.”

At that, the Drunken Demon provoked him.

He clenched and opened his fist in front of his chest, miming the Self-Destruction Art bursting with a bang. It meant that he would taste his blood then. It was not just any provocation, but a powerful one.

At that provocation, the Blood King felt as though his blood had gone cold.

He was mocking him by imitating the Self-Destruction Art? As much as anger surged within him, he also felt something unfamiliar. No one had ever dared act so insolently toward him.

He knew it was a provocation, but it was one he could not help but take.

Because of whom had he been forced to abandon the Great Art and come out here?

The Drunken Demon’s provocation did not end there. He opened his robe. There was blood on the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk bound around his chest.

“Your blood will get on this too, won’t it? I’ll have to wash it clean before returning it.”

The Blood King could tell that the object the Drunken Demon wore had been given to him by the Young Cult Leader.

“You received a trinket like that and became the Young Cult Leader’s dog?”

It was a counterattack to the earlier provocation.

But at least when it came to Geom Mugeuk, provocation did not work.

“The dog is the Young Cult Leader.”

At the Blood King’s questioning gaze, the Drunken Demon uttered words that were still impossible to understand.

“You won’t believe it, but the Young Cult Leader becomes a dog for our sake.”

After all, there was no one within the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult who did not know that he had barked together with the Poison King in the training ground for the Poison King’s sake.

The Drunken Demon did not add any explanation. He was not confident he could explain it in a short time so that the Blood King would understand. No, even if he explained it all day, the man would not believe him. Unless one experienced him directly, it was impossible to understand a person like Geom Mugeuk.

“But is it all right for you to be this relaxed? Are you trusting that your subordinates will arrive first?”

Even now, red smoke was rising here and there in the far distance.

“If you keep that up, our Young Cult Leader will come too.”

“Are you anxious? Afraid your Young Cult Leader will come here and die?”

“He won’t die. The Young Cult Leader won’t.”

The Drunken Demon was certain. Even so, the reason he hoped he would not come was this.

“I simply don’t want him to fight people like us.”

“People like us?”

“Yes, people like us.”

Without telling him what kind of people that meant, the Drunken Demon drank. Around his body, liquor qi flickered like flames.

“So let’s be the only ones to smell this vile stench of blood and reek of liquor.”

Sssssss.

A thick mist rose around the two men, and the Drunken Demon vanished into that liquor qi.

The Blood King raised his hand and touched the liquor qi that obscured his vision. At a glance it was only mist, but it was such potent liquor qi that he could already feel himself becoming tipsy.

Throughout the entire time the Northern Blood Sect warriors had been fighting, the Blood King had thought of only one thing.

How would he fight within this liquor qi? How would he break through that liquor qi?

And he had thought of one method.

The Blood King, sharpening every nerve in preparation for the Drunken Demon’s ambush, extended one hand forward.

A searing heat began to rise from his fingertips.

His solution was this.

To burn away all the liquor qi with extreme yang qi.

Given the nature of liquor qi, it would be easy to burn, and the liquor qi the Drunken Demon was exuding now was so potent that it would burn even better.

Flames rose from the Blood King’s extreme yang qi.

Fwoosh.

As the liquor qi began to burn from his fingertips, a satisfied light flashed through the Blood King’s eyes.

But that lasted only a moment. The flames that had risen soon went out.

He had expected the fire to spread in every direction, but it burned only where the extreme yang qi had been unleashed and then quickly died.

This time, he raised his extreme yang qi even more strongly.

Fwoooosh.

But it only burned a little more than before; the fire soon went out. The liquor qi gathered back into that spot.

Then, from within the mist, the Drunken Demon’s drunken voice rang out.

“If you play with fire, you’ll wet the bed at night.”

The Blood King pretended not to hear and raised his hand, clutching at the liquor qi flowing around him. It felt as though it should all burn away, so why would it not burn?

“You can try for a hundred days and it still won’t burn. My liquor qi takes after its master, you see. It’s not passionate enough to blaze up in flames.”

Swishhh!

Irritated, the Blood King sent a palm strike toward the place the voice had come from.

Bang!

The sound of a wall breaking apart came from far behind, but there was no scream from the Drunken Demon.

Swishhhhh!

The Blood King turned and caught with his hand what had flown at him from behind. What had shot toward him at a terrifying speed was a pebble.

Rrrrrrk.

The pebble crumbled into powder in the Blood King’s hand and trickled down. Both the internal force sent into it and the internal force that blocked it had been too powerful for it to withstand the impact.

“Do you think you can kill me with a pebble like this?”

“So I prepared something bigger.”

Whoooosh!

What came flying was a large stone lantern that had been standing in the courtyard.

Bang!

The instant the stone lantern shattered under the Blood King’s palm strike—

The Drunken Demon appeared from behind and thrust out his dagger.

Shhhk!

The stone lantern had not been the only thing that flew.

As the Blood King dodged the Drunken Demon’s dagger aimed at his heart, he thrust out a palm strike.

Baaang!

Palm force collided with palm force, and as he was pushed back by that energy, the Drunken Demon vanished into the mist again.

The Blood King’s expression was stiff. The Drunken Demon’s ambush just now had been truly fierce. If he had held the arrogance of thinking, “Could he kill me with nothing more than a stone lantern?” he felt certain he would have been stabbed by the Drunken Demon’s dagger.

The fight that had begun as if they were taking a stroll now began to grow violent in earnest.

This time, the Blood King sensed the Drunken Demon first and charged, spewing blood qi.

Boom!

Fist collided with fist.

Skrrrk. Both men were pushed back, and the number of steps they retreated was exactly the same. They were, in truth, evenly matched.

This time, palm met palm; palm arts clashed.

Once again, they were pushed back by the exact same number of steps.

At that very moment, the Blood King was the first to unleash his unique martial art.

When he was pushed sliding backward, blood beads had already appeared above his other palm. Astonishingly, in that brief instant of collision and retreat, he had unleashed the Flying Blade Blood Rain from the Ten Thousand Deaths Blood Art.

In an instant, the blood beads changed into the shapes of small daggers.

Shk-shk-shk-shk-shk-shk-shk-shk-shk!

They swept through the place where the Drunken Demon had been.

It was a power that could not be compared to the Flying Blade Blood Rain his disciple Hagyeol had used.

Papapapapapapapak!

Dozens of holes were punched through the wall behind. The holes were densely packed, traces that made it clear anyone within that area could never have avoided them.

But there was no trace of the Drunken Demon’s blood anywhere on the wall.

Whoooosh.

For a moment, the liquor qi cleared, and the Drunken Demon revealed himself.

“Wait! Let me catch my breath.”

With an expression as if he had barely escaped danger, the Drunken Demon drank.

“Go easy, will you? I thought I’d never get to drink this good stuff again.”

The Blood King also paused to catch his breath. Neither of them had time to restore internal energy through circulating qi and regulating breath. Even in that brief moment of rest, neither took his eyes off the other.

“I feel bad drinking by myself.”

The Drunken Demon looked at the Blood King and held out the bottle.

“Have just one cup.”

The very instant those words ended—

Craaaack!

A streak of lightning-like astral qi fell onto the crown of the Blood King’s head.

The Blood King, who twisted his body in an instant and barely dodged, had his face contort.

A deep hole had been pierced into the place where he had been standing, and smoke rose from it. In another moment, his skull would have been run straight through.

The Drunken Demon’s ambush had not been an ordinary attack.

The Drunken Demon’s unique martial art, the Wine God Art.

The second cup, Wine God’s Lone Drink.

It was named for the sight of a single streak of astral qi descending from the sky, like one drinking alone.

“Do you hate receiving a cup from me that much?”

Grinning, the Drunken Demon vanished into the rising liquor qi.

The blood qi pouring from the Blood King’s body grew even denser, and red energy began to flow from his eyes as well. Rage surged up at the thought that he had nearly died.

Whether he knew that anger or not, the Drunken Demon appeared behind him again and launched a surprise attack.

Shhk! Shhk!

The Drunken Demon’s dagger sliced through empty air, and the Blood King, who turned swiftly, detonated a palm strike in the air.

“It will hurt, drunkard.”

The Drunken Demon looked down at his own arm, which was trembling faintly. Red energy lingered around it. He raised his internal force and shook off the red energy. That was how heavy the Blood King’s attack had been.

“Wipe off your blood before you talk.”

The Blood King also looked down at his own arm. Blood was flowing from a cut wound.

The Blood King put his mouth to the injury. The instant he sucked, as though drinking the blood, veins bulged all over his face. His two eyes split long like a demon’s, and blood qi surged forth.

The Blood King’s appearance and momentum now were fearsome enough that even facing him was frightening.

Of course, it did not work on the Drunken Demon. Instead, he provoked him further.

“Why are you searching so hard for the Extreme Cold Physique?”

The corners of the Blood King’s eyes twitched fearsomely. Truly, his opponent was focusing only on the points that enraged him most.

“What are you planning to do after extracting the Extreme Cold Physique? Become resistant to cold and settle down here in the North Sea?”

Because the voice was drunken, the Blood King became even angrier. The Extreme Cold Physique was not something to be babbled about while drunk.

“As you know, I took the Extreme Cold Physique away. As an apology, should I buy you a padded coat?”

The Drunken Demon seemed to be saying whatever came to mind, but every single word was calculated.

“So you should have come to us in the first place if you were going to come. Ah, or are you perhaps the weakest among them? Is that why you were pushed all the way out here to the North Sea?”

In the end, curses burst from the Blood King’s mouth.

“Shut up, you drunken bastard!”

He could endure other things, but he could not tolerate being told he ranked low within the organization. He prided himself on being the most imposing of them all. That was why he had been able to defy the order to return.

The liquor qi also played a part in his agitation. As he fought within the liquor qi, the Blood King had unknowingly become drunk.

Puk!

Not missing this chance when he was agitated, the Drunken Demon attempted an ambush. Appearing from within the mist, the Drunken Demon’s dagger stabbed into the Blood King’s side.

Thud!

When he stabbed the side and tried to vanish again, the Drunken Demon was struck hard in the shoulder by the Blood King’s blow. At that moment, the Drunken Demon saw it. The Blood King’s eyes were colder than anything.

The Blood King who had shouted curses had been hot, but the one counterattacking was cold. It made him think that perhaps half of the man’s excitement had been deliberately displayed.

Daggers flew and fists flew. Blood qi and liquor qi collided again.

They struck, battered, and rolled. The two men were truly locked in a fierce battle between dragon and tiger.

Every single move was imbued with emotion. They fought as if they had met an irreconcilable enemy.

It was a first for both of them. The first time they had fought a life-or-death battle against an opponent this evenly matched.

After a fierce bout of grappling, the Drunken Demon vanished into the mist again.

He had expected it, but the Blood King was even harder to deal with than anticipated.

The Drunken Demon, who had been moving while drinking within the liquor qi, stopped walking.

A single red line had been drawn in front of him. The Drunken Demon looked to the side. There was a line there as well. Blood lines began to appear one by one here and there.

Those lines began to draw a picture upon the mist-like liquor qi as if it were paper.

The Drunken Demon picked up a pebble from the ground and threw it at one of the lines.

Saaak.

As if cut by a treasured sword, the pebble was sliced in two.

More and more blood lines appeared around him, surrounding him like a spiderweb. It was the Body-Dividing Blood Net the Blood King had unleashed.

The Drunken Demon withdrew his liquor qi. Then the liquor qi that had spread like mist disappeared, and dozens of blood lines stretching around him became plainly visible.

Too many lines had already been drawn, making movement impossible. The only way was to break through with protective astral qi, but that would mean being prepared for severe injury.

Beyond the red lines, the Blood King could be seen. Though the consumption of internal energy from unfolding the Body-Dividing Blood Net had been tremendous, he was smiling. He was certain he could kill the Drunken Demon with this move.

‘I’ve trapped him properly.’

There was no gap through which to escape.

As if alive, the blood lines began to tighten toward the Drunken Demon.

The Drunken Demon drew the Blood Tears, the unique weapon hanging at his waist, and said,

“You don’t know this, do you?”

When he infused the Blood Tears with his unique internal energy, it vibrated once and emitted a pure white light.

The unique martial art, Wine God Art.

The final cup. Wine God’s Utter Drunkenness.

The Drunken Demon opened the stopper of the Blood Tears and gulped down the liquor inside.

Then the purple liquor qi flickering from the Drunken Demon’s body turned black, and from his eyes flowed a pure white, madness-tinged drunkenness.

That madness led to action. The Drunken Demon seized a blood line with his bare hand.

“When we’re drunk, we don’t see anything in front of us.”

Chapter 428: Why Fight This Hard?
---------------------------------
Sparks flared between the Drunken Demon’s hand and the blood thread, as though gangqi had collided with gangqi.

Chiiiiik.

Since the start of the fight, the Blood King had never been more shocked.

He had not imagined the man would seize a blood thread with his bare hands. And that was not all.

Jjiiiiik.

The Drunken Demon gripped the blood thread in both hands and tore at it.

“Uaaaaah!”

The Drunken Demon let out a scream full of agony. Even so, he did not let go of the blood thread.

At the very instant his cry reached its peak—

Snap!

The blood thread, which could slice through iron with the slightest touch, was severed.

The moment the blood thread was cut, a short scream burst from the Blood King’s mouth.

“Urgh!”

At that moment, the Blood King had connected the Divided-Body Blood Net to his own true qi in order to unleash even greater power. It was to kill the Drunken Demon with this one move.

But when the blood thread was severed, the meridians through which he circulated his true qi suffered a tremendous shock.

The Blood King gritted his teeth and endured it.

‘Surely he won’t cut another one?’

But the utterly drunk Drunken Demon had no eyes for anything else. Forgetting the pain he had just suffered, he seized the second blood thread in a single motion, as if grabbing a clothesline.

“Aaaaaaaah!”

With as much force as there was agony in his scream.

Duduk!

This time too, he tore it apart and snapped it.

“Ugh!”

When the second blood thread was cut, an even louder scream than before burst from the Blood King. This time, the shock was greater, and his meridians twisted.

In the end, the Blood King could no longer endure it and gave up executing the technique.

Sseut.

The blood threads drawn in every direction vanished.

At that very instant, the Drunken Demon began charging fiercely toward the Blood King.

It was not qinggong used to execute a martial art.

Like an enraged bull, the Drunken Demon charged, scattering a madness-soaked drunken qi.

The Blood King was one who could block sword qi and sword gang as they flew at him without so much as blinking. Yet in this moment, fear arose in his heart. The Drunken Demon, who had torn apart the blood threads with his bare hands and was now rushing at him, was frightening.

‘Not now!’

He had drunk the wine from the Blood Pavilion and ripped through the blood threads. In other words, if he was caught by that madman in his current state, he too might be torn apart.

Still, there was no way that strength could last forever.

‘There’s only one way!’

Just as the Drunken Demon charged like a bull and was about to throw a punch—

Whoooosh.

Something spread from the Blood King’s body like wings and swallowed the Drunken Demon as though enveloping him.

It was the secret art of the Myriad Serpent Blood Art, the Binding Blood Veil.

The Drunken Demon flailed inside the veil.

“Let me out, you bastard! When I get out, you’re dead!”

The Drunken Demon cursed and shouted like a drunkard making a scene.

Then, just when the inside of the blood veil seemed to grow quiet—

Jjiiiiik.

The Drunken Demon tore through the blood veil as well and stuck his head out.

“You bastard, you’re dead!”

Without hesitation, the Blood King released a palm force toward the Drunken Demon.

Swaaaaaak.

Pow!

Struck by the Blood King’s palm, the Drunken Demon was sent flying with his head still protruding from the blood veil.

The blood veil rolled across the ground. It did not move as it rolled, making it seem as though he had died, but then the Drunken Demon began crawling out of the veil like a grub.

Leaping lightly into the air, the Blood King unleashed blood qi in rapid succession.

Boom! Kwaaang! Boom boom!

The earth was overturned, and dust rose into the air. He poured blood qi into the ground as if intending to erase everything in that place altogether.

After that barrage of attacks ended, the rising dust began to settle.

From within it came the sound of ragged breathing.

When the dust cleared, the Drunken Demon stood among the gouged-out holes in the ground. Somehow, he had escaped the blood veil and withstood the blood qi that had poured down upon him.

Even so, a smile formed on the Blood King’s lips. His plan had succeeded. Earlier, when the Drunken Demon had severed the blood threads, the wine qi around his body had been black, but now it had returned to purple.

The effect of the final technique of the Wine God Art, the Wine God’s Utter Intoxication, had faded.

This final technique required an immense amount of inner force, and so it was a technique never used unless one stood at the brink of death.

The Drunken Demon, breathing hard, spotted an unbroken wine bottle among the bottles rolling near his feet and walked toward it.

Broken glass pierced the soles of his feet and drew blood, but the Drunken Demon paid it no mind.

That was also why the Blood King could not recklessly charge in. The Drunken Demon was still utterly drunk, and he was still engulfed in madness. The Drunken Demon picked up the wine bottle and gulped down the liquor.

From the beginning, he had placed dozens of bottles of wine in this yard and drunk from them. When facing the martial artists of the Northern Blood Sect, he had protected at least the wine bottles even amid attacks raining down with sword qi, but now almost all of them had been drunk or shattered, leaving only a few intact.

When the Blood King had first heard that the Drunken Demon had come with the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult, he had inwardly looked down on him. Among the Eight Demon Venerables, he had thought the Drunken Demon the easiest to deal with.

But his actual skill was enough to say with certainty that he fell behind no other Demon Venerable.

Moreover, most decisively, their martial arts compatibility was poor. Originally, the mere stench of blood exuded by his blood qi should have been enough to crush his opponent, but the Drunken Demon’s smell of alcohol had swallowed up the scent of blood.

His disposition was the same.

Something about the Drunken Demon did not match well with him. There should surely have been fear brought on by blood arts, but it did not work on this lunatic obsessed with liquor.

That was why he could say such a thing.

“Want some too?”

The Drunken Demon’s tongue was so twisted from drink that his words slurred. He was clearly drunk enough that it would not be strange for him to collapse at any moment, yet his eyes alone were clear to the point of transparency.

At the Drunken Demon’s words, the Blood King shook his head.

Did he want to drink? Right now, he felt like killing every bastard who ever drank in front of him again. Liquor? He did not even want to smell it.

Blood was flowing down from the Drunken Demon’s palms. They were wounds he had suffered when tearing through the blood threads and the blood veil. No matter how drunk he was, he should have felt the pain. The soles of his feet, his arms, and his side were all soaked in blood.

“Why are you trying to fight this far?”

Because if I don’t win, the Young Cult Leader will have to fight. Because a loathsome thing like you is one I want to finish with my own hands.

You cannot know the truth of a relationship just by looking. Only when something like this happens do you find out. How much you think of that person.

How much did he think of Geom Mugeuk?

If the Self-Destruction Art explodes, let it explode on me!

That was how he felt. As it happened, he was also the one wrapped in the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk.

“Is it because of the Young Cult Leader? Just what did the Young Cult Leader do for you?”

The Drunken Demon began walking slowly toward him, staggering and reeking of liquor.

“Good question. He always comes to me only at the very end.”

The Drunken Demon was staggering not simply because he was drunk. He was exhausted to the bone, and his inner force was nearly at rock bottom.

Watching the Drunken Demon walk toward him, the Blood King felt, for the first time in his life, the premonition that he might lose to someone.

That fear led the Blood King into rage.

‘I’ll kill him.’

That drunken bastard, I’ll kill him with my own hands! I’ll beat him to death with my fists. Let’s see what kind of face your Young Cult Leader makes.

From the Blood King’s two eyes, blood qi filled with killing intent surged forth.

The fight between the two men was a fight between blood and liquor.

Their battle resembled those stimulating, primal smells. It was emotional rather than logical. Curses came before words. It was a fight where the result—one must be killed—came before the question of why he had to be killed. A fight between blood and liquor seemed fated to be that sort of relationship.

The two infused the remaining inner force into both fists. They were not the Fist Demon, yet they fought with their fists like the Fist Demon.

Pow!

The Blood King’s fist struck the Drunken Demon in the chest. Despite that heavy power, the Drunken Demon did not step back. Was he enduring it despite the pain, or was he too drunk to feel pain at all? The Drunken Demon did not dodge and swung his fist in return.

The Drunken Demon’s first punch missed, but his second punch smashed straight into the Blood King’s face.

His jaw twisted, and the Blood King staggered. The punch had landed cleanly, but the Blood King did not fall.

He dodged the Drunken Demon’s next punch and counterattacked. Their fists crossed as though entangling, and his blow struck the Drunken Demon’s shoulder.

They struck well and dodged well. It looked as if they were swinging their fists wildly, but they were a Demon Venerable and the Blood King. And so the fierce exchange of punches continued, as if to see who would prevail.

Thud!

The Drunken Demon staggered.

Just as the Blood King rushed in, unwilling to miss the opportunity—

Crack!

The Drunken Demon’s headbutt exploded into the Blood King’s face.

It was truly an attack he had never imagined. A headbutt? The Blood King was so dumbfounded and enraged he could hardly stand it.

Puk!

At the same time came the sound of flesh being torn.

As the Blood King stepped back while touching his nose, he saw it: the Drunken Demon lightly retreating far away.

‘Why?’

Just now, when the headbutt had succeeded, should that not have been his chance to press the attack?

At that moment, the Blood King smelled an unfamiliar scent of blood.

The Blood King slowly lowered his gaze to his own chest.

The place where his heart was had been drenched dark with blood. He could feel something hot passing through his chest and flowing down to his abdomen.

The Blood King’s eyes flew wide open.

‘When?’

At the very instant he had taken the headbutt, the Drunken Demon had stabbed his heart with a dagger as swiftly as lightning.

From the very beginning, the Drunken Demon had aimed only for that one move. He had been dead drunk, but that was the Drunken Demon’s drunkenness. The Drunken Demon had charged like a bull not toward the Blood King, but toward this result.

Only then did the Blood King see the dagger in the Drunken Demon’s hand. It had pierced so deeply that the entire blade was stained with blood.

“I told you before, didn’t I? A drunkard’s fight is this filthy.”

He had been fine until then, but the moment he saw that dagger, the strength left the Blood King’s legs, and he sank to the ground.

He hurriedly pressed his acupoints to stop the bleeding, but the place he had been stabbed was the heart. It was not a condition that could be resolved by stopping the bleeding on the outside.

‘I’m dying like this? To that drunkard?’

But on this day, in this place, liquor was thicker than blood.

The Drunken Demon stood at a distance. Even though his fatal attack had succeeded—no, because it had succeeded—the Drunken Demon was cautious.

Because he did not know when the Blood King might use the Self-Destruction Art.

From afar, his eyes met the Blood King’s. As he quietly stared into the man’s eyes, one thing came to him instinctively.

“You can’t be…”

The Drunken Demon asked with a look that truly said, surely not.

“You didn’t learn the Self-Destruction Art?”

Just as one came to know an opponent better after fighting them, he somehow had the premonition that it was so.

And from the Blood King’s expression, he could tell. He truly had not learned the Self-Destruction Art.

He did not want his own end to be one in which he was blown to pieces.

“You, no matter what, this isn’t right. I don’t know about anyone else, but you, at least, should end in a truly splendid, massive explosion, shouldn’t you?”

Even though he had made his subordinates explode with the Self-Destruction Art like that.

“You asked before we fought, didn’t you? Why I didn’t want the Young Cult Leader to fight you people.”

With cold eyes, the Drunken Demon added,

“This is why. You and I, we’re both too…”

The Drunken Demon slowly walked toward him. The Blood King gritted his teeth and drew up his inner force.

He had not learned the Self-Destruction Art, but he had one final move to kill the Drunken Demon.

Flying Butterfly Blood Poison.

If he used the Flying Butterfly Blood Poison, half of his innate true qi would disappear permanently, so he could not use it carelessly. But if he was going to die anyway, he could die together with this bastard.

‘Just a little closer. Just a little more.’

As the Drunken Demon approached, he suddenly took steps backward and retreated.

“What reason do I have to trust a man like you?”

When drunk, the Drunken Demon could smell the scent of death even better.

The Drunken Demon picked up a broken stone lantern from the ground. It was the very stone lantern that had been thrown during their earlier fight and split in half.

Guessing what the Drunken Demon intended to do, the Blood King shouted with difficulty.

“No! Kill me like a martial artist!”

“No. This is perfect for you!”

Shuuuuuk!

The stone lantern, carrying the last of the Drunken Demon’s remaining inner force, flew toward him.

Kooong!

The stone lantern crushed his face and upper body outright.

Blood flowed out from beneath the stone lantern. Far more blood flowed from the Blood King’s corpse than from an ordinary person’s.

The Drunken Demon staggered over, sat leaning against the stone lantern, and drank.

At last, the life-or-death battle had ended.

The blood flowing across the ground soaked his backside. The Drunken Demon silently looked down at the flowing blood. His own face was reflected in it.

Whiiiiik.

Bang!

Two people fell down there.

The one who had slammed his opponent into the ground as if hurling him down was Geom Mugeuk.

The man who died along with the gouged-out ground was the very cook who had killed the innkeeper earlier at the inn. He had come here after being summoned by the Blood King, but died without even seeing his master’s face.

Geom Mugeuk and the Drunken Demon’s gazes met in midair. There were so many things he wanted to say, but what came out of the Drunken Demon’s mouth was this.

“You’re late again.”

At those words, a surge of emotion rose in Geom Mugeuk’s chest. The Drunken Demon would never know how much he had longed to hear those words again.

Geom Mugeuk walked over to Chwima and sat down beside him. Without a word, Chwima handed him the remaining liquor.

Geom Mugeuk drank deeply, then passed it back to Chwima and said,

“They got here a while ago.”

Beyond the shattered wall, corpses lay scattered everywhere. Like the cook who had died at the very end, they were the subordinates the Blood King had summoned.

“Quite a lot of them came.”

They had continued arriving while the two men fought, and Geom Mugeuk had dealt with them one after another as they appeared.

Chwima understood. If even a few of them had joined that razor-close fight, he would have died.

Geom Mugeuk stared straight at Chwima and said,

“You were amazing, hyung.”

A smile Chwima could not hide spread across his lips.

As he smelled the rising scent of the Blood King’s blood, Chwima gave now the answer he had not given earlier.

Why was he fighting so hard for the Young Cult Leader?

Because he wanted to hear those words.

You were amazing, hyung.

Chwima lifted the bottle, then grimaced.

“Ahh, now that the tension’s gone, it hurts like hell. I’m going to lie down. My whole body feels like it’s been beaten black and blue. Right, I really was beaten half to death, wasn’t I? Aaah. I’m going to die because I tried to act cool for no reason.”

Grumbling in a way that was both exaggerated and not, he lay down. The floor was soaked with blood, but he paid it no mind.

“If you were here, you should’ve come to save me first.”

Geom Mugeuk knew well that he did not mean it. Chwima had truly wanted to kill the Blood King with his own hands.

The Blood King’s men were one thing, but there had been another reason Geom Mugeuk had not joined in.

He had been worried that another of the Twelve Zodiac Kings might intervene.

In the last fight with the Fighting King, someone had taken him away at the end. There had been every chance the same thing might happen this time. They might even intervene in the battle directly.

So, in preparation for anyone barging into the fight, he had kept strict watch from the outside. Even while dealing with the Blood King’s subordinates, he had watched and watched again to see if anyone was approaching.

Was that why? Or had there been some other reason?

This time, no one appeared.

Yes. Just as their side had changed beyond comparison with the past, the circumstances on the other side must have changed greatly as well. Those who should have been alive were dying, and they were likely failing to obtain what they were supposed to obtain.

Grrrr.

At the sound of snoring, he turned and saw that Chwima had fallen asleep. Having exhausted both his internal energy and his mental strength, he was completely drained.

Geom Mugeuk was about to lift him from where he slept in that blood-soaked place, then stopped.

The expression on Chwima’s sleeping face looked far too peaceful. Right now, he was not lying in a pool of blood.

He looked as peaceful as he had when, drunk, he had jumped into the lake in front of Chwimong Pavilion. It was the very same expression he had worn lying on the lake, gazing up at the stars in the night sky. It seemed the blood soaking his back had become a lake, calling that moment back to him.

Geom Mugeuk quietly closed his eyes as well. Thinking of the stars in the night sky he had looked up at together with him while lying on the lake, he drank the remaining liquor.

“This liquor tastes good.”

Chapter 429: I Am Resistant to the Cold.
----------------------------------------
The Ice Palace Mistress stood by the window.

She was looking far off, in the direction of Geom Mugeuk and Chwima’s residence.

She had received the report that the battle had begun there quite some time ago, yet no report had come up saying it was over.

She wanted to run there at once, but the Ice Palace Mistress forced herself to remain calm.

Below the Palace Mistress’s Hall outside the window, the North Wind Unit, the elite organization directly under the Palace Mistress, was standing by. Not only they, but the various organizations and masters of the Ice Palace were waiting at their assigned locations.

Originally, this was something all of them should have gone to fight.

—Let the monsters be caught by the devils.

Were they catching them properly? Surely they were not being devoured by the monsters instead?

If that happened?

The king of devils who had lost his child would come here for revenge. At that time, perhaps no one would be able to stop him.

Han Seol silently watched the Ice Palace Mistress. Though her figure remained the same as she looked out the window, her mother now was different from usual.

For one thing, her clothes were different. She was dressed in martial attire easy to move in, and on her arm she wore even an artifact she did not usually wear. Beneath her sleeve, the Great Ice Ring, a bracelet that raised the power of ice arts to the extreme, could be seen.

She was truly in a state where, if anything happened, she was ready to rush out to fight at once.

Han Seol’s gaze, which had been on the Ice Palace Mistress, turned toward Ian. Unlike her mother, from whom a sense of urgency could be felt, this side was peace itself.

Ian was reading a book. It was a book that had been on the bookshelf in the Palace Mistress’s Hall. She had asked permission to read it and was sitting there reading. This was the first time Han Seol had seen anyone read a book here. She had thought the books on the shelf were merely for decoration as well.

‘What kind of book is she reading?’

Han Seol walked over and sat down in front of Ian.

Then Ian took her eyes off the book and looked at Han Seol.

“There is a Demon Venerable who loves books. He has read all sorts of books, so there’s nothing he doesn’t know. He always says this: if you want to be good at martial arts, you have to read many books.”

“Do you mean martial arts manuals?”

“No. He says to read without discrimination.”

As she said that, she showed the cover of the book she was reading. Truly out of nowhere, it was a book about house-building in the northern region.

“That helps with martial arts?”

“Who knows? While running away, I might find a place to hide somewhere in a house built in this way.”

Han Seol’s face showed that she could not understand.

“Do I look too carefree? When the Young Cult Master is fighting?”

This time as well, Han Seol nodded honestly. If it were her, she felt the book would not even enter her eyes.

“If the Young Cult Master dies, I can just follow him in death.”
